```python
"""Sync subjective dimensions into the plan queue.

Two independent sync functions:

- **sync_unscored_dimensions** — prepend never-scored (placeholder) dimensions
  to the *front* of the queue unconditionally (onboarding priority).
- **sync_stale_dimensions** — append stale (previously-scored) dimensions to
  the *back* of the queue when no objective items remain.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib
from dataclasses import dataclass, field

from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import PlanModel, ensure_plan_defaults
from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel

SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX = "subjective::"
SYNTHESIS_ID = "synthesis::pending"


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Result dataclasses
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

@dataclass
class StaleDimensionSyncResult:
    """What changed during a stale-dimension sync."""

    injected: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    pruned: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)

    @property
    def changes(self) -> int:
        return len(self.injected) + len(self.pruned)


@dataclass
class UnscoredDimensionSyncResult:
    """What changed during an unscored-dimension sync."""

    injected: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    pruned: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)

    @property
    def changes(self) -> int:
        return len(self.injected) + len(self.pruned)


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# ID helpers
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def _current_stale_ids(state: StateModel) -> set[str]:
    """Return the set of ``subjective::<slug>`` IDs that are currently stale."""
    from desloppify.engine._work_queue.helpers import slugify
    from desloppify.engine.planning.scorecard_projection import (
        scorecard_subjective_entries,
    )

    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {}) or {}
    if not dim_scores:
        return set()

    stale: set[str] = set()
    for entry in scorecard_subjective_entries(state, dim_scores=dim_scores):
        if not entry.get("stale"):
            continue
        dim_key = entry.get("dimension_key", "")
        if dim_key:
            stale.add(f"{SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX}{slugify(dim_key)}")
    return stale


def _current_unscored_ids(state: StateModel) -> set[str]:
    """Return the set of ``subjective::<slug>`` IDs that are currently unscored (placeholder).

    Checks ``subjective_assessments`` directly because
    ``scorecard_subjective_entries`` filters out placeholder dimensions
    (they are hidden from the scorecard display).
    """
    from desloppify.engine._work_queue.helpers import slugify

    assessments = state.get("subjective_assessments")
    if not isinstance(assessments, dict) or not assessments:
        return set()

    unscored: set[str] = set()
    for dim_key, payload in assessments.items():
        if not isinstance(payload, dict):
            continue
        if not payload.get("placeholder"):
            continue
        if dim_key:
            unscored.add(f"{SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX}{slugify(dim_key)}")
    return unscored


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Unscored dimension sync (front of queue, unconditional)
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def sync_unscored_dimensions(
    plan: PlanModel,
    state: StateModel,
) -> UnscoredDimensionSyncResult:
    """Keep the plan queue in sync with unscored (placeholder) subjective dimensions.

    1. **Prune** — remove ``subjective::*`` IDs from ``queue_order`` that are
       no longer unscored AND not stale (avoids pruning stale IDs — that is
       ``sync_stale_dimensions``' responsibility).
    2. **Inject** — unconditionally prepend currently-unscored IDs to the
       *front* of ``queue_order`` so initial reviews are the first priority.
    """
    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
    result = UnscoredDimensionSyncResult()
    unscored_ids = _current_unscored_ids(state)
    stale_ids = _current_stale_ids(state)
    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]

    # --- Cleanup: prune subjective IDs that are no longer unscored --------
    # Only prune IDs that are neither unscored nor stale (stale sync owns those).
    to_remove: list[str] = [
        fid for fid in order
        if fid.startswith(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX)
        and fid not in unscored_ids
        and fid not in stale_ids
    ]
    for fid in to_remove:
        order.remove(fid)
        result.pruned.append(fid)

    # --- Inject: prepend unscored IDs to front ----------------------------
    existing = set(order)
    for uid in reversed(sorted(unscored_ids)):
        if uid not in existing:
            order.insert(0, uid)
            result.injected.append(uid)

    return result


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Stale dimension sync (back of queue, conditional)
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def sync_stale_dimensions(
    plan: PlanModel,
    state: StateModel,
) -> StaleDimensionSyncResult:
    """Keep the plan queue in sync with stale subjective dimensions.

    1. Remove any ``subjective::*`` IDs from ``queue_order`` that are no
       longer stale and not unscored (avoids pruning IDs owned by
       ``sync_unscored_dimensions``).
    2. If no objective items remain after cleanup, inject all currently-stale
       dimension IDs so the plan surfaces them as actionable work.
    """
    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
    result = StaleDimensionSyncResult()
    stale_ids = _current_stale_ids(state)
    unscored_ids = _current_unscored_ids(state)
    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]

    # --- Cleanup: prune resolved subjective IDs --------------------------
    # Only prune IDs that are neither stale nor unscored.
    to_remove: list[str] = [
        fid for fid in order
        if fid.startswith(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX)
        and fid not in stale_ids
        and fid not in unscored_ids
    ]
    for fid in to_remove:
        order.remove(fid)
        result.pruned.append(fid)

    # --- Inject: populate when no objective items remain -----------------
    has_real_items = any(fid for fid in order if not fid.startswith(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX))
    if not has_real_items and stale_ids:
        existing = set(order)
        for sid in sorted(stale_ids):
            if sid not in existing:
                order.append(sid)
                result.injected.append(sid)

    return result


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Synthesis snapshot hash + sync
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def review_finding_snapshot_hash(state: StateModel) -> str:
    """Hash open review finding IDs to detect changes.

    Returns empty string when there are no open review findings.
    """
    findings = state.get("findings", {})
    review_ids = sorted(
        fid for fid, f in findings.items()
        if f.get("status") == "open"
        and f.get("detector") in ("review", "concerns")
    )
    if not review_ids:
        return ""
    return hashlib.sha256("|".join(review_ids).encode()).hexdigest()[:16]


@dataclass
class SynthesisSyncResult:
    """What changed during a synthesis sync."""

    injected: bool = False
    pruned: bool = False

    @property
    def changes(self) -> int:
        return int(self.injected) + int(self.pruned)


def sync_synthesis_needed(
    plan: PlanModel,
    state: StateModel,
) -> SynthesisSyncResult:
    """Inject ``synthesis::pending`` at front of queue when review findings change.

    Never auto-prunes — only explicit completion (``_apply_completion``) removes it.
    """
    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
    result = SynthesisSyncResult()
    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
    meta = plan.get("epic_synthesis_meta", {})
    already_present = SYNTHESIS_ID in order

    current_hash = review_finding_snapshot_hash(state)
    last_hash = meta.get("finding_snapshot_hash", "")

    # If hash changed and not already present, inject at front
    if current_hash and current_hash != last_hash and not already_present:
        order.insert(0, SYNTHESIS_ID)
        result.injected = True

    # Never auto-prune: synthesis::pending is only removed by explicit completion

    return result


__all__ = [
    "SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX",
    "SYNTHESIS_ID",
    "StaleDimensionSyncResult",
    "SynthesisSyncResult",
    "UnscoredDimensionSyncResult",
    "_current_unscored_ids",
    "review_finding_snapshot_hash",
    "sync_stale_dimensions",
    "sync_synthesis_needed",
    "sync_unscored_dimensions",
]
```

```
    22→

    24→def _cmd_plan_generate(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:

    29→def _cmd_plan_show(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    30→    """Show plan metadata summary."""
    31→    plan = load_plan()
    32→    ordered = len(plan.get("queue_order", []))
    33→    skipped = plan.get("skipped", {})
    34→    total_skipped = len(skipped)
    35→    temp_count = sum(1 for e in skipped.values() if e.get("kind") == "temporary")
    36→    perm_count = sum(1 for e in skipped.values() if e.get("kind") == "permanent")
    37→    fp_count = sum(1 for e in skipped.values() if e.get("kind") == "false_positive")
    38→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
    39→    overrides = plan.get("overrides", {})
    40→    active = plan.get("active_cluster")
    41→    superseded = len(plan.get("superseded", {}))
    42→
    43→    # Count meaningful annotations (descriptions and notes)
    44→    described = sum(1 for o in overrides.values() if o.get("description"))
    45→    noted = sum(1 for o in overrides.values() if o.get("note"))
    46→
    47→    print(colorize("  Living Plan Status", "bold"))
    48→    print(colorize("  " + "─" * 40, "dim"))
    49→    print(f"  Queue:            {ordered} items prioritized")
    50→    if total_skipped:
    51→        print(f"  Skipped:          {total_skipped} (temp: {temp_count}, wontfix: {perm_count}, fp: {fp_count})")
    52→    else:
    53→        print(f"  Skipped:          0")
    54→    print(f"  Clusters:         {len(clusters)}")
    55→    if clusters:
    56→        for name, cluster in clusters.items():
    57→            desc = cluster.get("description") or ""
    58→            member_count = len(cluster.get("finding_ids", []))
    59→            marker = " (focused)" if name == active else ""
    60→            desc_str = f" — {desc}" if desc else ""
    61→            print(f"    {name}: {member_count} items{desc_str}{marker}")
    62→    if described or noted:
    63→        print(f"  Annotations:      {described} described, {noted} noted")
    64→    if active:
    65→        print(f"  Focus:            {active}")
    66→    if superseded:
    67→        print(f"  Disappeared:      {superseded} (resolved or removed since last scan)")
    68→
    69→
    70→def _cmd_plan_reset(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    71→    """Reset the plan to empty."""
    72→    plan = load_plan()
    73→    queue_len = len(plan.get("queue_order", []))
    74→    cluster_count = len(plan.get("clusters", {}))
    75→    reset_plan(plan)
    76→    append_log_entry(
    77→        plan, "reset", actor="user",
    78→        detail={"previous_queue_size": queue_len, "previous_cluster_count": cluster_count},
    79→    )
    80→    save_plan(plan)
    81→    print(colorize("  Plan reset to empty.", "green"))
    82→
    83→
    84→def cmd_plan(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    85→    """Dispatch plan subcommand or generate markdown output."""
    86→    plan_action = getattr(args, "plan_action", None)
    87→
    88→    if plan_action is None:
    89→        _cmd_plan_generate(args)
    90→        return
    91→
    92→    if plan_action == "show":
    93→        _cmd_plan_show(args)
    94→        return
    95→
    96→    if plan_action == "queue":
    97→        cmd_plan_queue(args)
    98→        return
    99→
   100→    if plan_action == "reset":
   101→        _cmd_plan_reset(args)
   102→        return
   103→
   104→    if plan_action == "move":
   105→        cmd_plan_move(args)
   106→        return
   107→
   108→    if plan_action in (
   109→        "describe", "note", "focus",
   110→        "skip", "unskip", "reopen", "done",
   111→    ):
   112→        dispatch = {
   113→            "describe": cmd_plan_describe,
   114→            "done": cmd_plan_done,
   115→            "note": cmd_plan_note,
   116→            "focus": cmd_plan_focus,
   117→            "skip": cmd_plan_skip,
   118→            "unskip": cmd_plan_unskip,
   119→            "reopen": cmd_plan_reopen,
   120→        }
   121→        dispatch[plan_action](args)
   122→        return
   123→
   124→    if plan_action == "cluster":
   125→        cmd_cluster_dispatch(args)
   126→        return
   127→
   128→    if plan_action == "synthesize":
   129→        from desloppify.app.commands.plan.synthesize_handlers import cmd_plan_synthesize
   130→
   131→        cmd_plan_synthesize(args)
   132→        return
   133→
   134→    print(f"Unknown plan action: {plan_action}")
   135→
   136→
   137→# Backwards-compatible alias
   138→cmd_plan_output = cmd_plan
   139→
   140→__all__ = ["cmd_plan", "cmd_plan_output"]
```

```
1→"""Compact queue table renderer for ``plan queue``."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import argparse
     6→
     7→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.runtime import command_runtime
     8→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.state import require_completed_scan
     9→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize, print_table
    10→from desloppify.engine.plan import load_plan
    11→from desloppify.engine.work_queue import QueueBuildOptions, build_work_queue
    12→
    13→
    14→def _truncate(text: str, width: int) -> str:
    15→    if len(text) <= width:
    16→        return text
    17→    return text[: width - 1] + "\u2026"
    18→
    19→
    20→def _cluster_tier_label(item: dict) -> str:
    21→    """Compute a tier label from cluster members, e.g. 'T2' or 'T1-T3'."""
    22→    members = item.get("members", [])
    23→    if not members:
    24→        return ""
    25→    tiers = {int(m.get("effective_tier", m.get("tier", 3))) for m in members}
    26→    lo, hi = min(tiers), max(tiers)
    27→    if lo == hi:
    28→        return f"T{lo}"
    29→    return f"T{lo}-T{hi}"
    30→
    31→
    32→def _resolve_plan_context(plan: dict, cluster_filter: str | None) -> tuple[dict | None, str | None]:
    33→    plan_data: dict | None = None
    34→    if plan.get("queue_order") or plan.get("overrides") or plan.get("clusters"):
    35→        plan_data = plan
    36→
    37→    effective_cluster = cluster_filter
    38→    if plan_data and not cluster_filter:
    39→        active_cluster = plan_data.get("active_cluster")
    40→        if active_cluster:
    41→            effective_cluster = active_cluster
    42→    return plan_data, effective_cluster
    43→
    44→
    45→def _print_queue_header(
    46→    *,
    47→    items: list[dict],
    48→    tier_counts: dict,
    49→    include_skipped: bool,
    50→    plan: dict,
    51→    plan_data: dict | None,
    52→) -> None:
    53→    total = len(items)
    54→    skipped_count = sum(1 for it in items if it.get("plan_skipped"))
    55→    non_skipped = total - skipped_count
    56→    tc_parts = " ".join(f"T{t}:{tier_counts.get(t, 0)}" for t in (1, 2, 3, 4))
    57→    finding_total = sum(tier_counts.get(t, 0) for t in (1, 2, 3, 4))
    58→    if finding_total != non_skipped:
    59→        print(colorize(f"\n  Queue: {non_skipped} entries  ({finding_total} findings: {tc_parts})", "bold"))
    60→    else:
    61→        print(colorize(f"\n  Queue: {non_skipped} items  ({tc_parts})", "bold"))
    62→
    63→    focus = plan.get("active_cluster") if plan_data else None
    64→    if focus:
    65→        print(colorize(f"  Focus: {focus}", "cyan"))
    66→
    67→    if include_skipped or skipped_count != 0:
    68→        return
    69→    plan_skipped_count = len(plan.get("skipped", {})) if plan_data else 0
    70→    if not plan_skipped_count:
    71→        return
    72→    print(
    73→        colorize(
    74→            f"  ({plan_skipped_count} skipped item{'s' if plan_skipped_count != 1 else ''}"
    75→            " hidden — use --include-skipped)",
    76→            "dim",
    77→        )
    78→    )
    79→
    80→
    81→def _queue_display_items(items: list[dict], *, top: int) -> list[dict]:
    82→    if top > 0 and len(items) > top:
    83→        return items[:top]
    84→    return items
    85→
    86→
    87→def _build_rows(display_items: list[dict]) -> list[list[str]]:
    88→    rows: list[list[str]] = []
    89→    for idx, item in enumerate(display_items, 1):
    90→        pos = str(idx)
    91→        kind = item.get("kind", "finding")
    92→
    93→        if kind == "cluster":
    94→            tier_str = _cluster_tier_label(item)
    95→            member_count = item.get("member_count", 0)
    96→            detector = item.get("detector", "cluster")
    97→            summary = f"[{member_count} items] {item.get('summary', '')}"
    98→            cluster_name = item.get("cluster_name", item.get("id", ""))
    99→        else:
   100→            tier_val = int(item.get("effective_tier", item.get("tier", 3)))
   101→            tier_str = f"T{tier_val}"
   102→            detector = item.get("detector", "")
   103→            summary = item.get("summary", "")
   104→            plan_cluster = item.get("plan_cluster")
   105→            cluster_name = plan_cluster.get("name", "") if isinstance(plan_cluster, dict) else ""
   106→
   107→        suffix = " [skip]" if item.get("plan_skipped") else ""
   108→        summary_display = _truncate(summary, 48) + suffix
   109→        rows.append([pos, tier_str, detector, summary_display, cluster_name])
   110→    return rows
   111→
   112→
   113→def cmd_plan_queue(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   114→    """Render a compact table of all upcoming queue items."""
   115→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
   116→    state = runtime.state
   117→    if not require_completed_scan(state):
   118→        return
   119→
   120→    top = getattr(args, "top", 30)
   121→    tier_filter = getattr(args, "tier", None)
   122→    cluster_filter = getattr(args, "cluster", None)
   123→    include_skipped = bool(getattr(args, "include_skipped", False))
   124→
   125→    plan = load_plan()
   126→    plan_data, effective_cluster = _resolve_plan_context(plan, cluster_filter)
   127→
   128→    queue = build_work_queue(
   129→        state,
   130→        options=QueueBuildOptions(
   131→            tier=tier_filter,
   132→            count=None,
   133→            scan_path=state.get("scan_path"),
   134→            status="open",
   135→            include_subjective=True,
   136→            plan=plan_data,
   137→            include_skipped=include_skipped,
   138→            cluster=effective_cluster,
   139→            collapse_clusters=True,
   140→        ),
   141→    )
   142→    items = queue.get("items", [])
   143→    tier_counts = queue.get("tier_counts", {})
   144→
   145→    _print_queue_header(
   146→        items=items,
   147→        tier_counts=tier_counts,
   148→        include_skipped=include_skipped,
   149→        plan=plan,
   150→        plan_data=plan_data,
   151→    )
   152→
   153→    if not items:
   154→        print(colorize("\n  Queue is empty.", "green"))
   155→        return
   156→
   157→    # Determine which items to show
   158→    display_items = _queue_display_items(items, top=top)
   159→    headers = ["#", "Tier", "Detector", "Summary", "Cluster"]
   160→    rows = _build_rows(display_items)
   161→
   162→    print()
   163→    widths = [4, 4, 12, 50, 16]
   164→    print_table(headers, rows, widths=widths)
   165→
   166→    if top > 0 and len(items) > top:
   167→        remaining = len(items) - top
   168→        print(colorize(
   169→            f"\n  ... and {remaining} more (use --top 0 to show all)", "dim"
   170→        ))
   171→    print()
   172→
   173→
   174→__all__ = ["cmd_plan_queue"]
   175→
```

```
1→"""Plan schema types, defaults, and validation."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from typing import Any, Required, TypedDict
     6→
     7→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import utc_now
     8→
     9→PLAN_VERSION = 5
    10→
    11→VALID_SKIP_KINDS = {"temporary", "permanent", "false_positive", "synthesized_out"}
    12→
    13→EPIC_PREFIX = "epic/"
    14→VALID_EPIC_DIRECTIONS = {
    15→    "delete", "merge", "flatten", "enforce",
    16→    "simplify", "decompose", "extract", "inline",
    17→}
    18→
    19→
    20→class SkipEntry(TypedDict, total=False):
    21→    finding_id: Required[str]
    22→    kind: Required[str]  # "temporary" | "permanent" | "false_positive"
    23→    reason: str | None
    24→    note: str | None  # required for permanent (wontfix note)
    25→    attestation: str | None  # required for permanent/false_positive
    26→    created_at: str
    27→    review_after: int | None  # re-surface after N scans (temporary only)
    28→    skipped_at_scan: int  # state.scan_count when skipped
    29→
    30→
    31→class ItemOverride(TypedDict, total=False):
    32→    finding_id: Required[str]
    33→    description: str | None
    34→    note: str | None
    35→    cluster: str | None
    36→    created_at: str
    37→    updated_at: str
    38→
    39→
    40→class Cluster(TypedDict, total=False):
    41→    name: Required[str]
    42→    description: str | None
    43→    finding_ids: list[str]
    44→    created_at: str
    45→    updated_at: str
    46→    auto: bool  # True for auto-generated clusters
    47→    cluster_key: str  # Deterministic grouping key (for regeneration)
    48→    action: str | None  # Primary resolution command/guidance text
    49→    user_modified: bool  # True when user manually edits membership
    50→
    51→
    52→class ExecutionLogEntry(TypedDict, total=False):
    53→    timestamp: Required[str]
    54→    action: Required[str]  # "done", "skip", "unskip", "resolve", "reconcile", "cluster_done", "focus", "reset"
    55→    finding_ids: list[str]
    56→    cluster_name: str | None
    57→    actor: str  # "user" | "system" | "agent"
    58→    note: str | None
    59→    detail: dict  # action-specific extra data
    60→
    61→
    62→class SupersededEntry(TypedDict, total=False):
    63→    original_id: Required[str]
    64→    original_detector: str
    65→    original_file: str
    66→    original_summary: str
    67→    status: str  # "superseded" | "remapped" | "dismissed"
    68→    superseded_at: str
    69→    remapped_to: str | None
    70→    candidates: list[str]
    71→    note: str | None
    72→
    73→
    74→class PlanModel(TypedDict, total=False):
    75→    version: Required[int]
    76→    created: Required[str]
    77→    updated: Required[str]
    78→    queue_order: list[str]
    79→    deferred: list[str]  # kept empty for migration compat
    80→    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry]
    81→    active_cluster: str | None
    82→    overrides: dict[str, ItemOverride]
    83→    clusters: dict[str, Cluster]
    84→    superseded: dict[str, SupersededEntry]
    85→    promoted_ids: list[str]  # IDs user explicitly positioned via move_items()
    86→    plan_start_scores: dict  # frozen score snapshot from plan creation cycle
    87→    execution_log: list[ExecutionLogEntry]
    88→    epic_synthesis_meta: dict  # synthesis engine metadata
    89→
    90→
    91→def empty_plan() -> PlanModel:
    92→    """Return a new empty plan payload."""
    93→    now = utc_now()
    94→    return {
    95→        "version": PLAN_VERSION,
    96→        "created": now,
    97→        "updated": now,
    98→        "queue_order": [],
    99→        "deferred": [],
   100→        "skipped": {},
   101→        "active_cluster": None,
   102→        "overrides": {},
   103→        "clusters": {},
   104→        "superseded": {},
   105→        "promoted_ids": [],
   106→        "plan_start_scores": {},
   107→        "execution_log": [],
   108→        "epic_synthesis_meta": {},
   109→    }
   110→
   111→
   112→def _ensure_container_types(plan: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
   113→    if not isinstance(plan.get("queue_order"), list):
   114→        plan["queue_order"] = []
   115→    if not isinstance(plan.get("deferred"), list):
   116→        plan["deferred"] = []
   117→    if not isinstance(plan.get("skipped"), dict):
   118→        plan["skipped"] = {}
   119→    if not isinstance(plan.get("overrides"), dict):
   120→        plan["overrides"] = {}
   121→    if not isinstance(plan.get("clusters"), dict):
   122→        plan["clusters"] = {}
   123→    if not isinstance(plan.get("superseded"), dict):
   124→        plan["superseded"] = {}
   125→    if not isinstance(plan.get("promoted_ids"), list):
   126→        plan["promoted_ids"] = []
   127→    if not isinstance(plan.get("plan_start_scores"), dict):
   128→        plan["plan_start_scores"] = {}
   129→    if not isinstance(plan.get("execution_log"), list):
   130→        plan["execution_log"] = []
   131→    if not isinstance(plan.get("epic_synthesis_meta"), dict):
   132→        plan["epic_synthesis_meta"] = {}
   133→
   134→
   135→def _migrate_deferred_to_skipped(plan: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
   136→    deferred: list[str] = plan["deferred"]
   137→    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry] = plan["skipped"]
   138→    if not deferred:
   139→        return
   140→
   141→    now = utc_now()
   142→    for fid in list(deferred):
   143→        if fid in skipped:
   144→            continue
   145→        skipped[fid] = {
   146→            "finding_id": fid,
   147→            "kind": "temporary",
   148→            "reason": None,
   149→            "note": None,
   150→            "attestation": None,
   151→            "created_at": now,
   152→            "review_after": None,
   153→            "skipped_at_scan": 0,
   154→        }
   155→    deferred.clear()
   156→
   157→
   158→def _normalize_cluster_defaults(plan: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
   159→    for cluster in plan["clusters"].values():
   160→        if not isinstance(cluster, dict):
   161→            continue
   162→        if not isinstance(cluster.get("finding_ids"), list):
   163→            cluster["finding_ids"] = []
   164→        cluster.setdefault("auto", False)
   165→        cluster.setdefault("cluster_key", "")
   166→        cluster.setdefault("action", None)
   167→        cluster.setdefault("user_modified", False)
   168→
   169→
   170→def _migrate_epics_to_clusters(plan: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
   171→    """Migrate v3 top-level ``epics`` dict into ``clusters`` (v4 unification)."""
   172→    epics = plan.pop("epics", None)
   173→    if not isinstance(epics, dict) or not epics:
   174→        return
   175→    clusters = plan["clusters"]
   176→    now = utc_now()
   177→    for name, epic in epics.items():
   178→        if not isinstance(epic, dict):
   179→            continue
   180→        if name in clusters:
   181→            continue  # don't overwrite existing cluster
   182→        clusters[name] = {
   183→            "name": name,
   184→            "description": epic.get("thesis", ""),
   185→            "finding_ids": epic.get("finding_ids", []),
   186→            "auto": True,
   187→            "cluster_key": f"epic::{name}",
   188→            "action": f"desloppify plan focus {name}",
   189→            "user_modified": False,
   190→            "created_at": epic.get("created_at", now),
   191→            "updated_at": epic.get("updated_at", now),
   192→            # Epic-specific fields
   193→            "thesis": epic.get("thesis", ""),
   194→            "direction": epic.get("direction", "simplify"),
   195→            "root_cause": epic.get("root_cause", ""),
   196→            "supersedes": epic.get("supersedes", []),
   197→            "dismissed": epic.get("dismissed", []),
   198→            "agent_safe": epic.get("agent_safe", False),
   199→            "dependency_order": epic.get("dependency_order", 999),
   200→            "action_steps": epic.get("action_steps", []),
   201→            "source_clusters": epic.get("source_clusters", []),
   202→            "status": epic.get("status", "pending"),
   203→            "synthesis_version": epic.get("synthesis_version", 0),
   204→        }
   205→
   206→
   207→def ensure_plan_defaults(plan: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
   208→    """Normalize a loaded plan to ensure all keys exist.
   209→
   210→    Handles migration from v1 (deferred list) to v2 (skipped dict),
   211→    and v3 (top-level epics) to v4 (epics unified into clusters).
   212→    """
   213→    defaults = empty_plan()
   214→    for key, value in defaults.items():
   215→        plan.setdefault(key, value)
   216→
   217→    _ensure_container_types(plan)
   218→    _migrate_deferred_to_skipped(plan)
   219→    _migrate_epics_to_clusters(plan)
   220→    _normalize_cluster_defaults(plan)
   221→
   222→
   223→def synthesis_clusters(plan: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Cluster]:
   224→    """Return clusters whose name starts with ``EPIC_PREFIX``."""
   225→    return {
   226→        name: cluster
   227→        for name, cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).items()
   228→        if name.startswith(EPIC_PREFIX)
   229→    }
   230→
   231→
   232→def validate_plan(plan: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
   233→    """Raise ValueError when plan invariants are violated."""
   234→    if not isinstance(plan.get("version"), int):
   235→        raise ValueError("plan.version must be an int")
   236→    if not isinstance(plan.get("queue_order"), list):
   237→        raise ValueError("plan.queue_order must be a list")
   238→
   239→    # No ID should appear in both queue_order and skipped
   240→    skipped_ids = set(plan.get("skipped", {}).keys())
   241→    overlap = set(plan["queue_order"]) & skipped_ids
   242→    if overlap:
   243→        raise ValueError(
   244→            f"IDs cannot appear in both queue_order and skipped: {sorted(overlap)}"
   245→        )
   246→
   247→    # Validate skip entry kinds
   248→    for fid, entry in plan.get("skipped", {}).items():
   249→        kind = entry.get("kind")
   250→        if kind not in VALID_SKIP_KINDS:
   251→            raise ValueError(
   252→                f"Invalid skip kind {kind!r} for {fid}; must be one of {sorted(VALID_SKIP_KINDS)}"
   253→            )
   254→
   255→
   256→__all__ = [
   257→    "EPIC_PREFIX",
   258→    "ExecutionLogEntry",
   259→    "PLAN_VERSION",
   260→    "Cluster",
   261→    "ItemOverride",
   262→    "PlanModel",
   263→    "SkipEntry",
   264→    "SupersededEntry",
   265→    "VALID_EPIC_DIRECTIONS",
   266→    "VALID_SKIP_KINDS",
   267→    "empty_plan",
   268→    "ensure_plan_defaults",
   269→    "synthesis_clusters",
   270→    "validate_plan",
   271→]
   272→
```

```
1→"""Post-scan plan reconciliation — handle finding churn."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from dataclasses import dataclass, field
     6→from datetime import UTC, datetime, timedelta
     7→
     8→from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import EPIC_PREFIX, PlanModel, SupersededEntry, ensure_plan_defaults
     9→from desloppify.engine._plan.stale_dimensions import SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX, SYNTHESIS_ID
    10→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel, utc_now
    11→
    12→
    13→SUPERSEDED_TTL_DAYS = 90
    14→
    15→
    16→@dataclass
    17→class ReconcileResult:
    18→    """Summary of changes made during reconciliation."""
    19→
    20→    superseded: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    21→    pruned: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    22→    resurfaced: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    23→    changes: int = 0
    24→
    25→
    26→def _find_candidates(
    27→    state: StateModel, detector: str, file: str
    28→) -> list[str]:
    29→    """Find open findings that could be remaps for a disappeared finding."""
    30→    candidates: list[str] = []
    31→    for fid, finding in state.get("findings", {}).items():
    32→        if finding.get("status") != "open":
    33→            continue
    34→        if finding.get("detector") == detector and finding.get("file") == file:
    35→            candidates.append(fid)
    36→    return candidates
    37→
    38→
    39→def _is_finding_alive(state: StateModel, finding_id: str) -> bool:
    40→    """Return True if the finding exists and is open."""
    41→    finding = state.get("findings", {}).get(finding_id)
    42→    if finding is None:
    43→        return False
    44→    return finding.get("status") == "open"
    45→
    46→
    47→def _supersede_id(
    48→    plan: PlanModel,
    49→    state: StateModel,
    50→    finding_id: str,
    51→    now: str,
    52→) -> bool:
    53→    """Move a disappeared finding to superseded. Returns True if changed."""
    54→    finding = state.get("findings", {}).get(finding_id)
    55→    detector = ""
    56→    file = ""
    57→    summary = ""
    58→    if finding:
    59→        detector = finding.get("detector", "")
    60→        file = finding.get("file", "")
    61→        summary = finding.get("summary", "")
    62→
    63→    candidates = _find_candidates(state, detector, file) if detector else []
    64→    # Don't include the original in candidates
    65→    candidates = [c for c in candidates if c != finding_id]
    66→
    67→    entry: SupersededEntry = {
    68→        "original_id": finding_id,
    69→        "original_detector": detector,
    70→        "original_file": file,
    71→        "original_summary": summary,
    72→        "status": "superseded",
    73→        "superseded_at": now,
    74→        "remapped_to": None,
    75→        "candidates": candidates[:5],
    76→    }
    77→
    78→    # Preserve any existing override note
    79→    override = plan.get("overrides", {}).get(finding_id)
    80→    if override and override.get("note"):
    81→        entry["note"] = override["note"]
    82→
    83→    plan["superseded"][finding_id] = entry
    84→
    85→    # Remove from queue_order, skipped, promoted_ids, cluster finding_ids
    86→    order: list[str] = plan.get("queue_order", [])
    87→    skipped: dict = plan.get("skipped", {})
    88→    promoted: list[str] = plan.get("promoted_ids", [])
    89→    if finding_id in order:
    90→        order.remove(finding_id)
    91→    skipped.pop(finding_id, None)
    92→    if finding_id in promoted:
    93→        promoted.remove(finding_id)
    94→    for cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).values():
    95→        ids = cluster.get("finding_ids", [])
    96→        if finding_id in ids:
    97→            ids.remove(finding_id)
    98→
    99→    # Clear stale cluster reference from override
   100→    override = plan.get("overrides", {}).get(finding_id)
   101→    if override and override.get("cluster"):
   102→        override["cluster"] = None
   103→        override["updated_at"] = now
   104→
   105→    return True
   106→
   107→
   108→def _prune_old_superseded(plan: PlanModel, now_dt: datetime) -> list[str]:
   109→    """Remove superseded entries older than TTL. Returns pruned IDs."""
   110→    superseded = plan.get("superseded", {})
   111→    cutoff = now_dt - timedelta(days=SUPERSEDED_TTL_DAYS)
   112→    to_prune: list[str] = []
   113→
   114→    for fid, entry in superseded.items():
   115→        ts = entry.get("superseded_at", "")
   116→        try:
   117→            entry_dt = datetime.fromisoformat(ts)
   118→            if entry_dt.tzinfo is None:
   119→                entry_dt = entry_dt.replace(tzinfo=UTC)
   120→            if entry_dt < cutoff:
   121→                to_prune.append(fid)
   122→        except (ValueError, TypeError):
   123→            to_prune.append(fid)
   124→
   125→    for fid in to_prune:
   126→        superseded.pop(fid, None)
   127→        # Also clean up stale overrides
   128→        plan.get("overrides", {}).pop(fid, None)
   129→
   130→    return to_prune
   131→
   132→
   133→def reconcile_plan_after_scan(
   134→    plan: PlanModel,
   135→    state: StateModel,
   136→) -> ReconcileResult:
   137→    """Reconcile plan against current state after a scan.
   138→
   139→    Finds IDs referenced in the plan that no longer exist or are no longer
   140→    open, moves them to superseded, and prunes old superseded entries.
   141→    """
   142→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   143→    result = ReconcileResult()
   144→    now = utc_now()
   145→    now_dt = datetime.now(UTC)
   146→
   147→    # Collect all finding IDs referenced by the plan
   148→    referenced_ids: set[str] = set()
   149→    referenced_ids.update(plan.get("queue_order", []))
   150→    referenced_ids.update(plan.get("skipped", {}).keys())
   151→    for override_id in plan.get("overrides", {}):
   152→        referenced_ids.add(override_id)
   153→    for cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).values():
   154→        referenced_ids.update(cluster.get("finding_ids", []))
   155→
   156→    # Exclude already-superseded IDs and synthetic IDs (managed by stale_dimensions)
   157→    already_superseded = set(plan.get("superseded", {}).keys())
   158→    referenced_ids -= already_superseded
   159→    referenced_ids = {
   160→        fid for fid in referenced_ids
   161→        if not fid.startswith(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX)
   162→        and fid != SYNTHESIS_ID
   163→    }
   164→
   165→    # Check each referenced ID
   166→    for fid in sorted(referenced_ids):
   167→        if not _is_finding_alive(state, fid):
   168→            if _supersede_id(plan, state, fid, now):
   169→                result.superseded.append(fid)
   170→                result.changes += 1
   171→
   172→    # Reconcile epic clusters: remove dead findings, delete empty epics
   173→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
   174→    epic_names_to_delete: list[str] = []
   175→    for name, cluster in list(clusters.items()):
   176→        if not name.startswith(EPIC_PREFIX):
   177→            continue
   178→        finding_ids = cluster.get("finding_ids", [])
   179→        alive_ids = [fid for fid in finding_ids if _is_finding_alive(state, fid)]
   180→        if alive_ids != finding_ids:
   181→            cluster["finding_ids"] = alive_ids
   182→            result.changes += 1
   183→        if not alive_ids:
   184→            epic_names_to_delete.append(name)
   185→    for name in epic_names_to_delete:
   186→        clusters.pop(name, None)
   187→        result.changes += 1
   188→
   189→    # Resurface stale temporary skips
   190→    scan_count = state.get("scan_count", 0)
   191→    from desloppify.engine._plan.operations import resurface_stale_skips
   192→
   193→    resurfaced = resurface_stale_skips(plan, scan_count)
   194→    if resurfaced:
   195→        result.resurfaced = resurfaced
   196→        result.changes += len(resurfaced)
   197→
   198→    # Prune old superseded entries
   199→    pruned = _prune_old_superseded(plan, now_dt)
   200→    result.pruned = pruned
   201→    result.changes += len(pruned)
   202→
   203→    # Log reconciliation if any changes were made
   204→    if result.changes > 0:
   205→        from desloppify.engine._plan.operations import append_log_entry
   206→
   207→        append_log_entry(
   208→            plan,
   209→            "reconcile",
   210→            finding_ids=result.superseded,
   211→            actor="system",
   212→            detail={
   213→                "superseded_count": len(result.superseded),
   214→                "pruned_count": len(result.pruned),
   215→                "resurfaced_count": len(result.resurfaced),
   216→            },
   217→        )
   218→
   219→    return result
   220→
   221→
   222→__all__ = [
   223→    "ReconcileResult",
   224→    "reconcile_plan_after_scan",
   225→]
   226→
```

```
1→"""Pure mutation operations on the plan model."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import (
     6→    Cluster,
     7→    ExecutionLogEntry,
     8→    PlanModel,
     9→    SkipEntry,
    10→    empty_plan,
    11→    ensure_plan_defaults,
    12→)
    13→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import utc_now
    14→
    15→
    16→_DEFAULT_MAX_LOG_ENTRIES = 10000
    17→
    18→
    19→def _get_log_cap() -> int:
    20→    """Read execution_log_max_entries from config. Returns 0 for unlimited."""
    21→    try:
    22→        from desloppify.core.config import load_config
    23→
    24→        config = load_config()
    25→        value = config.get("execution_log_max_entries", _DEFAULT_MAX_LOG_ENTRIES)
    26→        return max(0, int(value))
    27→    except (ImportError, OSError, ValueError, TypeError):
    28→        return _DEFAULT_MAX_LOG_ENTRIES
    29→
    30→
    31→def append_log_entry(
    32→    plan: PlanModel,
    33→    action: str,
    34→    *,
    35→    finding_ids: list[str] | None = None,
    36→    cluster_name: str | None = None,
    37→    actor: str = "user",
    38→    note: str | None = None,
    39→    detail: dict | None = None,
    40→) -> None:
    41→    """Append a structured entry to the plan's execution log."""
    42→    log = plan.get("execution_log", [])
    43→    entry: ExecutionLogEntry = {
    44→        "timestamp": utc_now(),
    45→        "action": action,
    46→        "finding_ids": finding_ids or [],
    47→        "cluster_name": cluster_name,
    48→        "actor": actor,
    49→        "note": note,
    50→        "detail": detail or {},
    51→    }
    52→    log.append(entry)
    53→    cap = _get_log_cap()
    54→    if cap > 0 and len(log) > cap:
    55→        plan["execution_log"] = log[-cap:]
    56→
    57→
    58→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    59→# Helpers
    60→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    61→
    62→def _remove_id_from_lists(plan: PlanModel, finding_id: str) -> None:
    63→    """Remove a finding ID from queue_order and skipped."""
    64→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
    65→    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry] = plan.get("skipped", {})
    66→    if finding_id in order:
    67→        order.remove(finding_id)
    68→    skipped.pop(finding_id, None)
    69→
    70→
    71→def _resolve_position(
    72→    order: list[str],
    73→    position: str,
    74→    target: str | None = None,
    75→    offset: int | None = None,
    76→    finding_ids: list[str] | None = None,
    77→) -> int:
    78→    """Resolve a position specifier to an insertion index.
    79→
    80→    *finding_ids* are the IDs being moved — used to calculate relative
    81→    positions when they already exist in the list.
    82→    """
    83→    moving = set(finding_ids or [])
    84→
    85→    if position == "top":
    86→        return 0
    87→    if position == "bottom":
    88→        return len(order)
    89→
    90→    if position in {"before", "after"} and target:
    91→        return _resolve_relative_position(order, moving, position=position, target=target)
    92→
    93→    if position in {"up", "down"} and offset is not None:
    94→        return _resolve_offset_position(
    95→            order,
    96→            moving,
    97→            position=position,
    98→            offset=offset,
    99→            finding_ids=finding_ids,
   100→        )
   101→
   102→    return len(order)
   103→
   104→
   105→def _resolve_relative_position(
   106→    order: list[str],
   107→    moving: set[str],
   108→    *,
   109→    position: str,
   110→    target: str,
   111→) -> int:
   112→    for i, item_id in enumerate(order):
   113→        if item_id == target and item_id not in moving:
   114→            return i if position == "before" else i + 1
   115→    return 0 if position == "before" else len(order)
   116→
   117→
   118→def _find_index(items: list[str], target: str) -> int | None:
   119→    for i, item_id in enumerate(items):
   120→        if item_id == target:
   121→            return i
   122→    return None
   123→
   124→
   125→def _resolve_offset_position(
   126→    order: list[str],
   127→    moving: set[str],
   128→    *,
   129→    position: str,
   130→    offset: int,
   131→    finding_ids: list[str] | None,
   132→) -> int:
   133→    if not finding_ids:
   134→        return 0 if position == "up" else len(order)
   135→    first_id = finding_ids[0]
   136→    clean_order = [x for x in order if x not in moving]
   137→    current_idx = _find_index(clean_order, first_id)
   138→    if current_idx is None:
   139→        if position == "up":
   140→            return max(0, len(clean_order) - offset)
   141→        return len(clean_order)
   142→    if position == "up":
   143→        return max(0, current_idx - offset)
   144→    return min(len(clean_order), current_idx + offset)
   145→
   146→
   147→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   148→# Move items
   149→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   150→
   151→def move_items(
   152→    plan: PlanModel,
   153→    finding_ids: list[str],
   154→    position: str,
   155→    target: str | None = None,
   156→    offset: int | None = None,
   157→) -> int:
   158→    """Move finding IDs to a position in queue_order. Returns count moved."""
   159→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   160→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   161→
   162→    # Remove from skipped if present
   163→    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry] = plan.get("skipped", {})
   164→    for fid in finding_ids:
   165→        skipped.pop(fid, None)
   166→
   167→    # Remove from current position in order
   168→    for fid in finding_ids:
   169→        if fid in order:
   170→            order.remove(fid)
   171→
   172→    # Resolve insertion point
   173→    idx = _resolve_position(order, position, target, offset, finding_ids)
   174→
   175→    # Insert in original order
   176→    for i, fid in enumerate(finding_ids):
   177→        order.insert(idx + i, fid)
   178→
   179→    # Track user-promoted IDs only when explicitly moved to the front.
   180→    # Moving to bottom/down/after should not create a promotion barrier.
   181→    if position == "top":
   182→        promoted: list[str] = plan.get("promoted_ids", [])
   183→        existing_promoted = set(promoted)
   184→        for fid in finding_ids:
   185→            if fid not in existing_promoted:
   186→                promoted.append(fid)
   187→                existing_promoted.add(fid)
   188→        plan["promoted_ids"] = promoted
   189→
   190→    return len(finding_ids)
   191→
   192→
   193→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   194→# Skip / unskip
   195→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   196→
   197→def skip_items(
   198→    plan: PlanModel,
   199→    finding_ids: list[str],
   200→    *,
   201→    kind: str = "temporary",
   202→    reason: str | None = None,
   203→    note: str | None = None,
   204→    attestation: str | None = None,
   205→    review_after: int | None = None,
   206→    scan_count: int = 0,
   207→) -> int:
   208→    """Move finding IDs to the skipped dict. Returns count skipped."""
   209→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   210→    now = utc_now()
   211→    count = 0
   212→    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry] = plan["skipped"]
   213→    skip_set = set(finding_ids)
   214→    promoted: list[str] = plan.get("promoted_ids", [])
   215→    plan["promoted_ids"] = [p for p in promoted if p not in skip_set]
   216→    for fid in finding_ids:
   217→        _remove_id_from_lists(plan, fid)
   218→        skipped[fid] = {
   219→            "finding_id": fid,
   220→            "kind": kind,
   221→            "reason": reason,
   222→            "note": note,
   223→            "attestation": attestation,
   224→            "created_at": now,
   225→            "review_after": review_after,
   226→            "skipped_at_scan": scan_count,
   227→        }
   228→        count += 1
   229→    return count
   230→
   231→
   232→def unskip_items(
   233→    plan: PlanModel, finding_ids: list[str]
   234→) -> tuple[int, list[str]]:
   235→    """Bring finding IDs back from skipped to the end of queue_order.
   236→
   237→    Returns ``(count_unskipped, permanent_ids_needing_state_reopen)``
   238→    where the second list contains IDs that were permanent or false_positive
   239→    and need their state-layer status reopened by the caller.
   240→    """
   241→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   242→    count = 0
   243→    need_reopen: list[str] = []
   244→    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry] = plan["skipped"]
   245→    for fid in finding_ids:
   246→        entry = skipped.pop(fid, None)
   247→        if entry is not None:
   248→            if entry.get("kind") in ("permanent", "false_positive"):
   249→                need_reopen.append(fid)
   250→            if fid not in plan["queue_order"]:
   251→                plan["queue_order"].append(fid)
   252→            count += 1
   253→    return count, need_reopen
   254→
   255→
   256→def resurface_stale_skips(
   257→    plan: PlanModel, current_scan_count: int
   258→) -> list[str]:
   259→    """Move temporary skips past their review_after threshold back to queue.
   260→
   261→    Returns list of resurfaced finding IDs.
   262→    """
   263→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   264→    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry] = plan["skipped"]
   265→    resurfaced: list[str] = []
   266→    for fid in list(skipped):
   267→        entry = skipped[fid]
   268→        if entry.get("kind") != "temporary":
   269→            continue
   270→        review_after = entry.get("review_after")
   271→        if review_after is None:
   272→            continue
   273→        skipped_at = entry.get("skipped_at_scan", 0)
   274→        if current_scan_count >= skipped_at + review_after:
   275→            skipped.pop(fid)
   276→            if fid not in plan["queue_order"]:
   277→                plan["queue_order"].append(fid)
   278→            resurfaced.append(fid)
   279→    return resurfaced
   280→
   281→
   282→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   283→# Describe / annotate
   284→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   285→
   286→def describe_finding(
   287→    plan: PlanModel, finding_id: str, description: str | None
   288→) -> None:
   289→    """Set or clear an augmented description on a finding."""
   290→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   291→    now = utc_now()
   292→    overrides = plan["overrides"]
   293→    if finding_id not in overrides:
   294→        overrides[finding_id] = {"finding_id": finding_id, "created_at": now}
   295→    overrides[finding_id]["description"] = description
   296→    overrides[finding_id]["updated_at"] = now
   297→
   298→
   299→def annotate_finding(
   300→    plan: PlanModel, finding_id: str, note: str | None
   301→) -> None:
   302→    """Set or clear a note on a finding."""
   303→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   304→    now = utc_now()
   305→    overrides = plan["overrides"]
   306→    if finding_id not in overrides:
   307→        overrides[finding_id] = {"finding_id": finding_id, "created_at": now}
   308→    overrides[finding_id]["note"] = note
   309→    overrides[finding_id]["updated_at"] = now
   310→
   311→
   312→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   313→# Clusters
   314→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   315→
   316→def create_cluster(
   317→    plan: PlanModel,
   318→    name: str,
   319→    description: str | None = None,
   320→    action: str | None = None,
   321→) -> Cluster:
   322→    """Create a named cluster. Raises ValueError if it already exists."""
   323→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   324→    if name.startswith("auto/"):
   325→        raise ValueError(
   326→            f"Cluster names starting with 'auto/' are reserved for auto-clusters: {name!r}"
   327→        )
   328→    if name.startswith("epic/"):
   329→        raise ValueError(
   330→            f"Cluster names starting with 'epic/' are reserved for synthesis epics: {name!r}"
   331→        )
   332→    if name in plan["clusters"]:
   333→        raise ValueError(f"Cluster {name!r} already exists")
   334→    now = utc_now()
   335→    cluster: Cluster = {
   336→        "name": name,
   337→        "description": description,
   338→        "finding_ids": [],
   339→        "created_at": now,
   340→        "updated_at": now,
   341→        "auto": False,
   342→        "cluster_key": "",
   343→        "action": action,
   344→        "user_modified": False,
   345→    }
   346→    plan["clusters"][name] = cluster
   347→    return cluster
   348→
   349→
   350→def add_to_cluster(
   351→    plan: PlanModel, cluster_name: str, finding_ids: list[str]
   352→) -> int:
   353→    """Add finding IDs to a cluster. Returns count added."""
   354→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   355→    cluster = plan["clusters"].get(cluster_name)
   356→    if cluster is None:
   357→        raise ValueError(f"Cluster {cluster_name!r} does not exist")
   358→
   359→    member_ids: list[str] = cluster["finding_ids"]
   360→    count = 0
   361→    now = utc_now()
   362→    for fid in finding_ids:
   363→        if fid not in member_ids:
   364→            member_ids.append(fid)
   365→            count += 1
   366→        # Update override to track cluster membership
   367→        overrides = plan["overrides"]
   368→        if fid not in overrides:
   369→            overrides[fid] = {"finding_id": fid, "created_at": now}
   370→        overrides[fid]["cluster"] = cluster_name
   371→        overrides[fid]["updated_at"] = now
   372→
   373→    cluster["updated_at"] = now
   374→    return count
   375→
   376→
   377→def remove_from_cluster(
   378→    plan: PlanModel, cluster_name: str, finding_ids: list[str]
   379→) -> int:
   380→    """Remove finding IDs from a cluster. Returns count removed."""
   381→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   382→    cluster = plan["clusters"].get(cluster_name)
   383→    if cluster is None:
   384→        raise ValueError(f"Cluster {cluster_name!r} does not exist")
   385→
   386→    member_ids: list[str] = cluster["finding_ids"]
   387→    now = utc_now()
   388→    count = 0
   389→    for fid in finding_ids:
   390→        if fid in member_ids:
   391→            member_ids.remove(fid)
   392→            count += 1
   393→        # Clear cluster from override
   394→        override = plan["overrides"].get(fid)
   395→        if override and override.get("cluster") == cluster_name:
   396→            override["cluster"] = None
   397→            override["updated_at"] = now
   398→
   399→    # Mark auto-clusters as user_modified when items are manually removed
   400→    if count > 0 and cluster.get("auto"):
   401→        cluster["user_modified"] = True
   402→
   403→    cluster["updated_at"] = now
   404→    return count
   405→
   406→
   407→def delete_cluster(plan: PlanModel, name: str) -> list[str]:
   408→    """Delete a cluster and clear cluster refs from overrides. Returns orphaned IDs."""
   409→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   410→    cluster = plan["clusters"].pop(name, None)
   411→    if cluster is None:
   412→        raise ValueError(f"Cluster {name!r} does not exist")
   413→
   414→    orphaned = list(cluster.get("finding_ids", []))
   415→    now = utc_now()
   416→    for fid in orphaned:
   417→        override = plan["overrides"].get(fid)
   418→        if override and override.get("cluster") == name:
   419→            override["cluster"] = None
   420→            override["updated_at"] = now
   421→
   422→    if plan.get("active_cluster") == name:
   423→        plan["active_cluster"] = None
   424→
   425→    return orphaned
   426→
   427→
   428→def move_cluster(
   429→    plan: PlanModel,
   430→    cluster_name: str,
   431→    position: str,
   432→    target: str | None = None,
   433→    offset: int | None = None,
   434→) -> int:
   435→    """Move all cluster members as a contiguous block. Returns count moved."""
   436→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   437→    cluster = plan["clusters"].get(cluster_name)
   438→    if cluster is None:
   439→        raise ValueError(f"Cluster {cluster_name!r} does not exist")
   440→
   441→    member_ids = list(cluster.get("finding_ids", []))
   442→    if not member_ids:
   443→        return 0
   444→
   445→    return move_items(plan, member_ids, position, target, offset)
   446→
   447→
   448→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   449→# Focus
   450→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   451→
   452→def set_focus(plan: PlanModel, cluster_name: str) -> None:
   453→    """Set the active cluster focus."""
   454→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   455→    if cluster_name not in plan["clusters"]:
   456→        raise ValueError(f"Cluster {cluster_name!r} does not exist")
   457→    plan["active_cluster"] = cluster_name
   458→
   459→
   460→def clear_focus(plan: PlanModel) -> None:
   461→    """Clear the active cluster focus."""
   462→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   463→    plan["active_cluster"] = None
   464→
   465→
   466→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   467→# Reset
   468→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   469→
   470→def reset_plan(plan: PlanModel) -> None:
   471→    """Reset plan to empty state, preserving version and created timestamp."""
   472→    created = plan.get("created", utc_now())
   473→    plan.clear()
   474→    for k, v in empty_plan().items():
   475→        plan[k] = v
   476→    plan["created"] = created
   477→
   478→
   479→def purge_ids(plan: PlanModel, finding_ids: list[str]) -> int:
   480→    """Remove finding IDs from the plan entirely.
   481→
   482→    Cleans queue_order, skipped, and all cluster memberships.
   483→    Does NOT touch overrides (descriptions/notes are kept for history).
   484→    Returns count of IDs that were actually present somewhere.
   485→    """
   486→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   487→    found = 0
   488→
   489→    purge_set = set(finding_ids)
   490→    promoted: list[str] = plan.get("promoted_ids", [])
   491→    plan["promoted_ids"] = [p for p in promoted if p not in purge_set]
   492→
   493→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   494→    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry] = plan["skipped"]
   495→    for fid in finding_ids:
   496→        was_present = False
   497→        if fid in order:
   498→            order.remove(fid)
   499→            was_present = True
   500→        if fid in skipped:
   501→            skipped.pop(fid)
   502→            was_present = True
   503→        for cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).values():
   504→            ids = cluster.get("finding_ids", [])
   505→            if fid in ids:
   506→                ids.remove(fid)
   507→                was_present = True
   508→        # Clear stale cluster reference from override (preserve notes/descriptions)
   509→        override = plan.get("overrides", {}).get(fid)
   510→        if override and override.get("cluster"):
   511→            override["cluster"] = None
   512→            override["updated_at"] = utc_now()
   513→        if was_present:
   514→            found += 1
   515→
   516→    return found
   517→
   518→
   519→__all__ = [
   520→    "add_to_cluster",
   521→    "annotate_finding",
   522→    "append_log_entry",
   523→    "clear_focus",
   524→    "create_cluster",
   525→    "delete_cluster",
   526→    "describe_finding",
   527→    "move_cluster",
   528→    "move_items",
   529→    "purge_ids",
   530→    "remove_from_cluster",
   531→    "reset_plan",
   532→    "resurface_stale_skips",
   533→    "set_focus",
   534→    "skip_items",
   535→    "unskip_items",
   536→]
   537→
```

```
    79→def synthesis_phase_banner(plan: PlanModel) -> str:
    80→    """Return a banner string when ``synthesis::pending`` is in the queue."""
    81→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
    82→    if SYNTHESIS_ID not in plan.get("queue_order", []):
    83→        return ""
    84→    meta = plan.get("epic_synthesis_meta", {})
    85→    stages = meta.get("synthesis_stages", {})
    86→    completed = [s for s in ("observe", "reflect", "organize") if s in stages]
    87→    if completed:
    88→        return (
    89→            f"Synthesis in progress ({len(completed)}/4 stages complete). "
    90→            "Run: desloppify plan synthesize"
    91→        )
    92→    return (
    93→        "Synthesis needed — review findings require analysis before fixing. "
    94→        "Run: desloppify plan synthesize"
    95→    )
    96→
```

```
    60→def _is_subjective_queue_item(item: dict) -> bool:
    61→    """Return True for queue items that represent subjective work."""
    62→    return item.get("kind") == "subjective_dimension" or bool(item.get("is_subjective"))
    63→
    64→
    65→def _apply_subjective_interleave_guardrail(
    66→    items: list[dict],
    67→    *,
    68→    objective_burst: int = 3,
    69→    subjective_burst: int = 1,
    70→) -> list[dict]:
    71→    """Interleave subjective work with objective work to avoid starvation."""
    72→    if objective_burst < 1 or subjective_burst < 1:
    73→        return items
    74→
    75→    subjective_items = deque(
    76→        item for item in items if _is_subjective_queue_item(item)
    77→    )
    78→    objective_items = deque(
    79→        item for item in items if not _is_subjective_queue_item(item)
    80→    )
    81→    if not subjective_items or not objective_items:
    82→        return items
    83→
    84→    interleaved: list[dict] = []
    85→    while objective_items and subjective_items:
    86→        for _ in range(objective_burst):
    87→            if not objective_items:
    88→                break
    89→            interleaved.append(objective_items.popleft())
    90→        for _ in range(subjective_burst):
    91→            if not subjective_items:
    92→                break
    93→            interleaved.append(subjective_items.popleft())
    94→
    95→    interleaved.extend(objective_items)
    96→    interleaved.extend(subjective_items)
    97→    return interleaved
    98→
    99→
   100→def _apply_plan_order(
   101→    items: list[dict],
   102→    plan: dict,
   103→    *,
   104→    include_skipped: bool = False,
   105→    cluster: str | None = None,
   106→) -> list[dict]:
   107→    """Reorder items according to the living plan.
   108→
   109→    1. Items in ``queue_order`` appear first, in that order.
   110→    2. Remaining items keep their mechanical sort.
   111→    3. Skipped items are appended last (or excluded).
   112→    4. Each item is annotated with plan metadata.
   113→    """
   114→    queue_order: list[str] = plan.get("queue_order", [])
   115→    skipped_map: dict = plan.get("skipped", {})
   116→    skipped_ids: set[str] = set(skipped_map.keys())
   117→    overrides: dict = plan.get("overrides", {})
   118→    clusters: dict = plan.get("clusters", {})
   119→    active_cluster = plan.get("active_cluster")
   120→
   121→    # Build lookup
   122→    by_id: dict[str, dict] = {}
   123→    for item in items:
   124→        by_id[item["id"]] = item
   125→
   126→    # Annotate items with plan metadata
   127→    for item_id, item in by_id.items():
   128→        override = overrides.get(item_id, {})
   129→        if override.get("description"):
   130→            item["plan_description"] = override["description"]
   131→        if override.get("note"):
   132→            item["plan_note"] = override["note"]
   133→        if override.get("cluster"):
   134→            cluster_name = override["cluster"]
   135→            cluster_data = clusters.get(cluster_name, {})
   136→            item["plan_cluster"] = {
   137→                "name": cluster_name,
   138→                "description": cluster_data.get("description"),
   139→                "total_items": len(cluster_data.get("finding_ids", [])),
   140→                "sibling_ids": cluster_data.get("finding_ids", []),
   141→            }
   142→
   143→    # Split into ordered, remaining, skipped
   144→    ordered: list[dict] = []
   145→    ordered_ids: set[str] = set()
   146→    for fid in queue_order:
   147→        if fid in by_id and fid not in skipped_ids:
   148→            ordered.append(by_id[fid])
   149→            ordered_ids.add(fid)
   150→
```

```
   189→def _item_matches_tier(item: dict, tier: int) -> bool:
   190→    """Check if an item (or any of its members for clusters) matches a tier."""
   191→    if item.get("kind") == "cluster":
   192→        return any(
   193→            int(m.get("effective_tier", m.get("tier", 3))) == tier
   194→            for m in item.get("members", [])
   195→        )

   197→
   198→
   199→def _action_type_for_detector(detector: str) -> str:
   200→    """Look up the action_type for a detector from the registry."""
   201→    try:
   202→        from desloppify.core.registry import DETECTORS
   203→        meta = DETECTORS.get(detector)
   204→        if meta:
   205→            return meta.action_type
   206→    except ImportError as exc:
   207→        _ = exc
   208→    return "manual_fix"
   209→
   210→
   211→_ACTION_TYPE_PRIORITY = {"auto_fix": 0, "reorganize": 1, "refactor": 2, "manual_fix": 3}
   212→
   213→
   214→def _collapse_clusters(items: list[dict], plan: dict) -> list[dict]:
   215→    """Replace cluster member items with single cluster meta-items."""
   216→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
   217→    if not clusters:
   218→        return items
   219→
   220→    # Build mapping: finding_id → auto-cluster name
   221→    fid_to_cluster: dict[str, str] = {}
   222→    for name, cluster in clusters.items():
   223→        if not cluster.get("auto"):
   224→            continue
   225→        for fid in cluster.get("finding_ids", []):
   226→            fid_to_cluster[fid] = name
   227→
   228→    if not fid_to_cluster:
   229→        return items
   230→
   231→    # Collect members for each cluster, preserving order
   232→    cluster_members: dict[str, list[dict]] = {}
   233→    non_cluster_items: list[dict] = []
   234→
   235→    for item in items:
   236→        cname = fid_to_cluster.get(item.get("id", ""))
   237→        if cname:
   238→            cluster_members.setdefault(cname, []).append(item)
   239→        else:
   240→            non_cluster_items.append(item)
   241→
   242→    # Build cluster meta-items
   243→    result: list[dict] = list(non_cluster_items)
   244→    for cname, members in cluster_members.items():
   245→        # Don't collapse singletons — show them as individual findings
   246→        if len(members) < 2:
   247→            result.extend(members)
   248→            continue
   249→        cluster_data = clusters.get(cname, {})
   250→        detector = members[0].get("detector", "") if members else ""
   251→        action = cluster_data.get("action") or ""
   252→        # Derive action_type from the actual cluster action, not just the detector
   253→        if "desloppify fix" in action:
   254→            action_type = "auto_fix"
   255→        elif "desloppify move" in action:
   256→            action_type = "reorganize"
   257→        else:
   258→            action_type = _action_type_for_detector(detector)
   259→            # If detector says auto_fix but cluster has no fix command, it's really refactor
   260→            if action_type == "auto_fix" and "desloppify fix" not in action:
   261→                action_type = "refactor"
   262→
   263→        # Use stored description but patch the count if it differs from visible members
   264→        stored_desc = cluster_data.get("description") or ""
   265→        total_in_cluster = len(cluster_data.get("finding_ids", []))
   266→        if stored_desc and total_in_cluster != len(members):
   267→            # Replace the stored count with the visible count
   268→            summary = stored_desc.replace(str(total_in_cluster), str(len(members)))
   269→        else:
   270→            summary = stored_desc or f"{len(members)} findings"
   271→
   272→        primary_command = cluster_data.get("action")
   273→        if not primary_command:
   274→            primary_command = f"desloppify next --cluster {cname} --count 10"
   275→
   276→        meta_item: dict = {
   277→            "id": cname,
   278→            "kind": "cluster",
   279→            "action_type": action_type,
   280→            "summary": summary,
   281→            "members": members,
   282→            "member_count": len(members),
   283→            "primary_command": primary_command,
   284→            "cluster_name": cname,
   285→            "cluster_auto": True,
   286→            "confidence": "high",
   287→            "detector": detector,
   288→            "file": "",
   289→        }
   290→        result.append(meta_item)
   291→
   292→    result.sort(key=item_sort_key)
   293→    return result
   294→
   295→
   296→def build_work_queue(
   297→    state: StateModel,
   298→    *,
   299→    options: QueueBuildOptions | None = None,
   300→) -> WorkQueueResult:
   301→    """Build ranked queue items + tier metadata."""
   302→    resolved_options = options or QueueBuildOptions()
   303→
   304→    status = resolved_options.status
   305→    if status not in ALL_STATUSES:
   306→        raise ValueError(f"Unsupported status filter: {status}")
   307→    try:
   308→        subjective_threshold_value = float(resolved_options.subjective_threshold)
   309→    except (TypeError, ValueError):
   310→        subjective_threshold_value = 100.0
   311→    subjective_threshold_value = max(0.0, min(100.0, subjective_threshold_value))
   312→
   313→    finding_items = build_finding_items(
   314→        state,
   315→        scan_path=resolved_options.scan_path,
   316→        status_filter=status,
   317→        scope=resolved_options.scope,
   318→        chronic=resolved_options.chronic,
   319→    )
   320→
   321→    all_items = list(finding_items)
   322→    if (
   323→        resolved_options.include_subjective
   324→        and status in {"open", "all"}
   325→        and not resolved_options.chronic
   326→    ):
   327→        subjective_items = build_subjective_items(
   328→            state,
   329→            state.get("findings", {}),
   330→            threshold=subjective_threshold_value,
   331→        )
   332→        for item in subjective_items:
   333→            if scope_matches(item, resolved_options.scope):
   334→                all_items.append(item)
   335→
   336→    # Inject synthesis::pending synthetic item when plan requires it
   337→    if resolved_options.plan and status in {"open", "all"}:
   338→        synth_item = build_synthesis_item(resolved_options.plan, state)
   339→        if synth_item is not None:
   340→            all_items.append(synth_item)
   341→
   342→    all_items.sort(key=item_sort_key)
   343→
   344→    # Apply living plan ordering if provided
   345→    if resolved_options.plan:
   346→        all_items = _apply_plan_order(
   347→            all_items,
   348→            resolved_options.plan,
   349→            include_skipped=resolved_options.include_skipped,
```

```
1→"""Auto-clustering algorithm — groups findings into task clusters."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import os
     6→from collections import defaultdict
     7→
     8→from desloppify.core.registry import DETECTORS, DetectorMeta
     9→from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import Cluster, PlanModel, ensure_plan_defaults
    10→from desloppify.engine._plan.stale_dimensions import (
    11→    SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX,
    12→    _current_unscored_ids,
    13→)
    14→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel, utc_now
    15→
    16→AUTO_PREFIX = "auto/"
    17→_MIN_CLUSTER_SIZE = 2
    18→_STALE_KEY = "subjective::stale"
    19→_STALE_NAME = "auto/stale-review"
    20→_UNSCORED_KEY = "subjective::unscored"
    21→_UNSCORED_NAME = "auto/initial-review"
    22→_MIN_UNSCORED_CLUSTER_SIZE = 1
    23→
    24→
    25→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    26→# Grouping key computation
    27→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    28→
    29→def _extract_subtype(finding: dict) -> str | None:
    30→    """Extract the subtype/kind from a finding.
    31→
    32→    Checks detail.kind first, then falls back to parsing the finding ID
    33→    (format: ``detector::file::subtype`` or ``detector::file::symbol::subtype``).
    34→    """
    35→    detail = finding.get("detail") or {}
    36→    kind = detail.get("kind")
    37→    if kind:
    38→        return kind
    39→
    40→    # Parse from finding ID — third segment is often the subtype
    41→    fid = finding.get("id", "")
    42→    parts = fid.split("::")
    43→    if len(parts) >= 3:
    44→        # For IDs like smells::file.py::silent_except, the subtype is parts[2]
    45→        # For IDs like dict_keys::file.py::key::phantom_read, subtype is last part
    46→        candidate = parts[-1]
    47→        # Skip if it looks like a file path or symbol name (contains / or .)
    48→        if "/" not in candidate and "." not in candidate:
    49→            return candidate
    50→    return None
    51→
    52→
    53→def _grouping_key(finding: dict, meta: DetectorMeta | None) -> str | None:
    54→    """Compute a deterministic grouping key for a finding.
    55→
    56→    Returns None if the finding should not be auto-clustered.
    57→    """
    58→    detector = finding.get("detector", "")
    59→
    60→    if meta is None:
    61→        # Unknown detector — group by detector name
    62→        return f"detector::{detector}"
    63→
    64→    # Review findings → group by dimension
    65→    if detector in ("review", "subjective_review"):
    66→        detail = finding.get("detail") or {}
    67→        dimension = detail.get("dimension", "")
    68→        if dimension:
    69→            return f"review::{dimension}"
    70→        return f"detector::{detector}"
    71→
    72→    # Per-file detectors (structural, responsibility_cohesion)
    73→    if meta.needs_judgment and detector in (
    74→        "structural", "responsibility_cohesion",
    75→    ):
    76→        file = finding.get("file", "")
    77→        if file:
    78→            basename = os.path.basename(file)
    79→            return f"file::{detector}::{basename}"
    80→
    81→    # Needs-judgment detectors — group by subtype when available
    82→    if meta.needs_judgment:
    83→        subtype = _extract_subtype(finding)
    84→        if subtype:
    85→            return f"typed::{detector}::{subtype}"
    86→
    87→    # Pure auto-fix (no judgment needed) → all findings by detector
    88→    if meta.action_type == "auto_fix" and not meta.needs_judgment:
    89→        return f"auto::{detector}"
    90→
    91→    # Everything else → by detector
    92→    return f"detector::{detector}"
    93→
    94→
    95→def _cluster_name_from_key(key: str) -> str:
    96→    """Convert a grouping key to a cluster name with auto/ prefix."""
    97→    # auto::unused → auto/unused
    98→    # typed::dict_keys::phantom_read → auto/dict_keys-phantom_read
    99→    # file::structural::big_file.py → auto/structural-big_file.py
   100→    # review::abstraction_fitness → auto/review-abstraction_fitness
```
