```
1→"""Finding generation pipeline (phase execution and normalization)."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import sys
     6→from dataclasses import dataclass
     7→from pathlib import Path
     8→
     9→from desloppify.core._internal.text_utils import PROJECT_ROOT
    10→from desloppify.engine.planning.common import is_subjective_phase
    11→from desloppify.engine.policy.zones import ZONE_POLICIES, FileZoneMap
    12→from desloppify.core.file_paths import rel
    13→from desloppify.languages import auto_detect_lang, available_langs, get_lang
    14→from desloppify.languages._framework.base.types import DetectorPhase, LangConfig
    15→from desloppify.languages._framework.runtime import LangRun, make_lang_run
    16→from desloppify.state import Finding
    17→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize
    18→
    19→
    20→@dataclass
    21→class PlanScanOptions:
    22→    """Config object for scan execution behavior."""
    23→
    24→    include_slow: bool = True
    25→    zone_overrides: dict[str, str] | None = None
    26→    profile: str = "full"
    27→
    28→
    29→def _stderr(msg: str) -> None:
    30→    print(colorize(msg, "dim"), file=sys.stderr)
    31→
    32→
    33→def _resolve_lang(
    34→    lang: LangConfig | LangRun | None, project_root: Path
    35→) -> LangConfig | LangRun:
    36→    if lang is not None:
    37→        return lang
    38→
    39→    detected = auto_detect_lang(project_root)
    40→    if detected is None:
    41→        langs = available_langs()
    42→        if not langs:
    43→            raise ValueError("No language plugins available")
    44→        detected = langs[0]
    45→    return get_lang(detected)
    46→
    47→
    48→def _build_zone_map(path: Path, lang: LangRun, zone_overrides: dict[str, str] | None) -> None:
    49→    if not (lang.zone_rules and lang.file_finder):
    50→        return
    51→
    52→    files = lang.file_finder(path)
    53→    lang.zone_map = FileZoneMap(
    54→        files, lang.zone_rules, rel_fn=rel, overrides=zone_overrides
    55→    )
    56→    counts = lang.zone_map.counts()
    57→    zone_str = ", ".join(
    58→        f"{zone}: {count}" for zone, count in sorted(counts.items()) if count > 0
    59→    )
    60→    _stderr(f"  Zones: {zone_str}")
    61→
    62→    from desloppify.languages._framework.generic import capability_report
    63→
    64→    report = capability_report(lang)
    65→    if report is not None:
    66→        present, missing = report
    67→        if present:
    68→            _stderr(f"  Capabilities: {', '.join(present)}")
    69→        if missing:
    70→            _stderr(f"  Not available: {', '.join(missing)}")
    71→
    72→
    73→def _select_phases(lang: LangRun, *, include_slow: bool, profile: str) -> list[DetectorPhase]:
    74→    active_profile = profile if profile in {"objective", "full", "ci"} else "full"
    75→    phases = lang.phases
    76→    if not include_slow or active_profile == "ci":
    77→        phases = [phase for phase in phases if not phase.slow]
    78→    if active_profile in {"objective", "ci"}:
    79→        phases = [phase for phase in phases if not is_subjective_phase(phase)]
    80→    return phases
    81→
    82→
    83→def _run_phases(path: Path, lang: LangRun, phases: list[DetectorPhase]) -> tuple[list[Finding], dict[str, int]]:
    84→    findings: list[Finding] = []
    85→    all_potentials: dict[str, int] = {}
    86→
    87→    total = len(phases)
    88→    for idx, phase in enumerate(phases, start=1):
    89→        _stderr(f"  [{idx}/{total}] {phase.label}...")
    90→        phase_findings, phase_potentials = phase.run(path, lang)
    91→        all_potentials.update(phase_potentials)
    92→        findings.extend(phase_findings)
    93→
    94→    return findings, all_potentials
    95→
    96→
    97→def _stamp_finding_context(findings: list[Finding], lang: LangRun) -> None:
    98→    if not findings:
    99→        return
   100→
   101→    zone_policies = None
   102→    if lang.zone_map is not None:
   103→        zone_policies = ZONE_POLICIES
   104→
   105→    for finding in findings:
   106→        finding["lang"] = lang.name
   107→        if lang.zone_map is None:
   108→            continue
   109→
   110→        zone = lang.zone_map.get(finding.get("file", ""))
   111→        finding["zone"] = zone.value
   112→        policy = zone_policies.get(zone) if zone_policies else None
   113→        if policy and finding.get("detector") in policy.downgrade_detectors:
   114→            finding["confidence"] = "low"
   115→
   116→
   117→def _generate_findings_from_lang(
   118→    path: Path,
   119→    lang: LangRun,
   120→    *,
   121→    include_slow: bool = True,
   122→    zone_overrides: dict[str, str] | None = None,
   123→    profile: str = "full",
   124→) -> tuple[list[Finding], dict[str, int]]:
   125→    """Run detector phases from a LangRun."""
   126→    _build_zone_map(path, lang, zone_overrides)
   127→    phases = _select_phases(lang, include_slow=include_slow, profile=profile)
   128→    findings, all_potentials = _run_phases(path, lang, phases)
   129→    _stamp_finding_context(findings, lang)
   130→    _stderr(f"\n  Total: {len(findings)} findings")
   131→    return findings, all_potentials
   132→
   133→
   134→def generate_findings(
   135→    path: Path,
   136→    lang: LangConfig | LangRun | None = None,
   137→    *,
   138→    options: PlanScanOptions | None = None,
   139→) -> tuple[list[Finding], dict[str, int]]:
   140→    """Run all detectors and convert results to normalized findings."""
   141→    resolved_options = options or PlanScanOptions()
   142→
   143→    resolved_lang = _resolve_lang(lang, PROJECT_ROOT)
   144→    runtime_lang = make_lang_run(resolved_lang)
   145→    return _generate_findings_from_lang(
   146→        path,
   147→        runtime_lang,
   148→        include_slow=resolved_options.include_slow,
   149→        zone_overrides=resolved_options.zone_overrides,
   150→        profile=resolved_options.profile,
```

```
1→"""Sync subjective dimensions into the plan queue.
     2→
     3→Two independent sync functions:
     4→
     5→- **sync_unscored_dimensions** — prepend never-scored (placeholder) dimensions
     6→  to the *front* of the queue unconditionally (onboarding priority).
     7→- **sync_stale_dimensions** — append stale (previously-scored) dimensions to
     8→  the *back* of the queue when no objective items remain.
     9→"""

    11→from __future__ import annotations
    12→
    13→import hashlib
    14→from dataclasses import dataclass, field
    15→
    16→from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import PlanModel, ensure_plan_defaults
    17→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel

    19→SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX = "subjective::"

    24→# Result dataclasses
    25→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

    27→@dataclass
    28→class StaleDimensionSyncResult:
    29→    """What changed during a stale-dimension sync."""
    30→
    31→    injected: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    32→    pruned: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    33→
    34→    @property
    35→    def changes(self) -> int:
    36→        return len(self.injected) + len(self.pruned)
    37→
    38→

    43→    injected: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)

    55→def _current_stale_ids(state: StateModel) -> set[str]:
    56→    """Return the set of ``subjective::<slug>`` IDs that are currently stale."""
    57→    from desloppify.engine._work_queue.helpers import slugify
    58→    from desloppify.engine.planning.scorecard_projection import (
    59→        scorecard_subjective_entries,
    60→    )
    61→
    62→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {}) or {}
    63→    if not dim_scores:
    64→        return set()
    65→
    66→    stale: set[str] = set()
    67→    for entry in scorecard_subjective_entries(state, dim_scores=dim_scores):
    68→        if not entry.get("stale"):
    69→            continue
    70→        dim_key = entry.get("dimension_key", "")
    71→        if dim_key:
    72→            stale.add(f"{SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX}{slugify(dim_key)}")
    73→    return stale
    74→
    75→
    76→def _current_unscored_ids(state: StateModel) -> set[str]:
    77→    """Return the set of ``subjective::<slug>`` IDs that are currently unscored (placeholder).
    78→
    79→    Checks ``subjective_assessments`` directly because
    80→    ``scorecard_subjective_entries`` filters out placeholder dimensions
    81→    (they are hidden from the scorecard display).
    82→    """
    83→    from desloppify.engine._work_queue.helpers import slugify
    84→
    85→    assessments = state.get("subjective_assessments")
    86→    if not isinstance(assessments, dict) or not assessments:
    87→        return set()
    88→
    89→    unscored: set[str] = set()
    90→    for dim_key, payload in assessments.items():
    91→        if not isinstance(payload, dict):
    92→            continue
    93→        if not payload.get("placeholder"):
    94→            continue
    95→        if dim_key:
    96→            unscored.add(f"{SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX}{slugify(dim_key)}")
    97→    return unscored

   100→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   101→# Promoted-aware insertion helper
   102→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   103→
   104→def _after_promoted(order: list[str], plan: PlanModel) -> int:
   105→    """Return the insertion index just after the last promoted item in *order*.
   106→
   107→    When no promoted items are present (or none are in *order*), returns 0
   108→    so callers fall back to existing front-of-queue behavior.
   109→    """
   110→    promoted = set(plan.get("promoted_ids", []))
   111→    if not promoted:
   112→        return 0
   113→    last_idx = -1
   114→    for i, fid in enumerate(order):
   115→        if fid in promoted:
   116→            last_idx = i
   117→    return last_idx + 1 if last_idx >= 0 else 0
   118→
   119→

   121→# Unscored dimension sync (front of queue, unconditional)

   123→
   124→def sync_unscored_dimensions(
   125→    plan: PlanModel,

   127→) -> UnscoredDimensionSyncResult:

   134→       *front* of ``queue_order`` so initial reviews are the first priority.

   142→    # --- Cleanup: prune subjective IDs that are no longer unscored --------
   143→    # Only prune IDs that are neither unscored nor stale (stale sync owns those).
   144→    to_remove: list[str] = [
   145→        fid for fid in order
   146→        if fid.startswith(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX)
   147→        and fid not in unscored_ids
   148→        and fid not in stale_ids
   149→    ]
   150→    for fid in to_remove:
```

```
   165→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   166→# Stale dimension sync (back of queue, conditional)
   167→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   168→
   169→def sync_stale_dimensions(
   170→    plan: PlanModel,
   171→    state: StateModel,
   172→) -> StaleDimensionSyncResult:
   173→    """Keep the plan queue in sync with stale subjective dimensions.
   174→
   175→    1. Remove any ``subjective::*`` IDs from ``queue_order`` that are no
   176→       longer stale and not unscored (avoids pruning IDs owned by
   177→       ``sync_unscored_dimensions``).
   178→    2. If no objective items remain after cleanup, inject all currently-stale
   179→       dimension IDs so the plan surfaces them as actionable work.
   180→    """
   181→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   182→    result = StaleDimensionSyncResult()
   183→    stale_ids = _current_stale_ids(state)
   184→    unscored_ids = _current_unscored_ids(state)
   185→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   186→
   187→    # --- Cleanup: prune resolved subjective IDs --------------------------
   188→    # Only prune IDs that are neither stale nor unscored.
   189→    to_remove: list[str] = [
   190→        fid for fid in order
   191→        if fid.startswith(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX)
   192→        and fid not in stale_ids
   193→        and fid not in unscored_ids
   194→    ]
   195→    for fid in to_remove:
   196→        order.remove(fid)
   197→        result.pruned.append(fid)
   198→
   199→    # --- Inject: populate when no objective items remain -----------------
   200→    has_real_items = any(fid for fid in order if not fid.startswith(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX))
   201→    if not has_real_items and stale_ids:
   202→        existing = set(order)
   203→        for sid in sorted(stale_ids):
   204→            if sid not in existing:
   205→                order.append(sid)
   206→                result.injected.append(sid)
   207→
   208→    return result
   209→

   211→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   212→# Synthesis snapshot hash + sync
   213→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   214→
   215→def review_finding_snapshot_hash(state: StateModel) -> str:
   216→    """Hash open review finding IDs to detect changes.
   217→
   218→    Returns empty string when there are no open review findings.
   219→    """
   220→    findings = state.get("findings", {})
   221→    review_ids = sorted(
   222→        fid for fid, f in findings.items()
   223→        if f.get("status") == "open"
   224→        and f.get("detector") in ("review", "concerns")
   225→    )
   226→    if not review_ids:
   227→        return ""
   228→    return hashlib.sha256("|".join(review_ids).encode()).hexdigest()[:16]
   229→
   230→
   231→@dataclass
   232→class SynthesisSyncResult:
   233→    """What changed during a synthesis sync."""
   234→
   235→    injected: bool = False
   236→    pruned: bool = False
   237→
   238→    @property
   239→    def changes(self) -> int:
   240→        return int(self.injected) + int(self.pruned)
   241→

   249→    Never auto-prunes — only explicit completion (``_apply_completion``) removes it.
```

## Summary

I've thoroughly explored how the plan queue is displayed and managed in Desloppify. Here's what I found:

---

## 1. Plan Queue Display Commands

### **`desloppify plan queue`** (Primary Display)
**File:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/plan/queue_render.py`

This is the main command for rendering queue items in a compact table format. Key details:

- **Function:** `cmd_plan_queue()` (lines 113-172)
- **Column headers:** `["#", "Tier", "Detector", "Summary", "Cluster"]` (line 159)
- **Display format:** Uses `print_table()` with fixed widths: `[4, 4, 12, 50, 16]` (line 163)
- **Rows built by:** `_build_rows()` (lines 87-110) which creates:
  - **Position** (idx): Sequential numbering starting at 1
  - **Tier**: "T1", "T2", "T3", "T4", or range like "T1-T3" for clusters
  - **Detector**: Detector name (12 chars max, truncated)
  - **Summary**: Finding/cluster summary (48 chars max, truncated with "…")
  - **Cluster**: Cluster membership name (16 chars max)

**Special handling:**
- Adds `[skip]` suffix to summary if `plan_skipped` is true (line 107)
- For clusters: Shows `[member_count items] summary` format
- Top limit: 30 items by default, configurable via `--top` argument
- Options: `--tier`, `--cluster`, `--include-skipped` filters

### **`desloppify plan show`** (Metadata Summary)
**File:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/plan/cmd.py`

Function: `_cmd_plan_show()` (lines 29-68)

Displays plan metadata (not the queue itself):
```
Living Plan Status
─────────────────────────────────────
Queue:       X items prioritized
Skipped:     Y (temp: X, wontfix: Y, fp: Z)
Clusters:    N
Annotations: X described, Y noted
Focus:       cluster_name
Disappeared: Z resolved or removed since last scan
```

### **`desloppify next`** (Next Priority Item)
**File:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/next.py`

Shows the single highest-priority queue item with detailed context. Uses `build_work_queue()` with plan ordering applied.

### **`desloppify status`** (Scorecard with Tier Progress)
**File:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/status_cmd.py`

Displays per-tier progress with queue awareness through `plan_aware_queue_count()`.

### **`desloppify plan`** (Default: Markdown Plan)
Uses `cmd_plan_output()` to generate a prioritized markdown breakdown of all findings by tier.

---

## 2. How Queue Items Are Rendered

**Core rendering logic:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_work_queue/core.py`

Key function: `_apply_plan_order()` (lines 100-186)

**Process:**
1. Receives unsorted items from `build_finding_items()` and synthetic items (subjective dimensions, synthesis placeholders)
2. **Reorders** according to `plan.queue_order` (lines 114-149):
   - Items in `queue_order` appear first, in that order
   - Remaining items keep mechanical sort order
   - Skipped items appended last (or excluded)
3. **Annotates** each item with plan metadata (lines 127-141):
   - `plan_description`: From `overrides[id].description`
   - `plan_note`: From `overrides[id].note`
   - `plan_cluster`: Nested dict with cluster name, description, total_items, sibling_ids
   - `plan_skipped`: Boolean flag
   - `plan_skip_kind`: "temporary", "permanent", "false_positive"
4. **Assigns queue position** (lines 167-168): `item["queue_position"] = pos + 1`
5. **Filters by cluster** if requested (lines 179-184)

**Cluster collapsing:** `_collapse_clusters()` (lines 214-293) creates meta-items:
- Shows `[member_count] summary` instead of individual findings
- Derives action type from cluster action or detector
- Patches count if it differs from stored value

---

## 3. How Items Are Added to Queue During/After Scan

**Reconciliation workflow:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/scan/scan_workflow.py` → `_reconcile_plan_post_scan()` (lines 186-243)

Called after state merge (line 600). Multiple sync operations populate the queue:

### **A. Reconciliation: Remove superseded items**
**File:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_plan/reconcile.py`

Function: `reconcile_plan_after_scan()` (lines 133-219)

- Removes finding IDs from `queue_order` if they no longer exist or are closed
- Moves them to `superseded` dict instead
- Resumes temporarily skipped items that past their `review_after` scan threshold
- **No direct injection of new findings** — reconciliation only removes dead ones

### **B. Unscored Dimensions Sync: Initial reviews**
**File:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_plan/stale_dimensions.py`

Function: `sync_unscored_dimensions()` (lines 124-162)

- **Injects** new unscored (placeholder) subjective dimensions at **front** of queue (after promoted items)
- IDs format: `subjective::<slug>` (e.g., `subjective::abstraction_fitness`)
- Unconditional — runs on every scan

### **C. Stale Dimensions Sync: Dimension reviews**
Function: `sync_stale_dimensions()` (lines 169-208)

- **Appends** stale subjective dimensions to **back** of queue
- Only when no objective items remain (line 200)
- Prevents dimension starvation

### **D. Auto-clustering Sync**
**File:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_plan/auto_cluster.py`

Function: `auto_cluster_findings()` — regenerates automatic clusters based on detector/file/subtype patterns. Items remain in `queue_order`; clustering affects display only.

### **E. Synthesis Sync**
Function: `sync_synthesis_needed()` (line 243-248 of stale_dimensions.py)

- Injects `synthesis::pending` at front of queue when review findings change
- Detected by hash of open review finding IDs

**Critical gap:** The scan workflow does **NOT** automatically inject new findings into `queue_order`. They only appear in the queue if:
1. The plan already has a `queue_order` list (user has run `plan` before), OR
2. A subsequent scan reconciliation happens and user has queued findings manually via `plan move`

Otherwise, new findings appear in mechanical sort order until explicitly moved via `plan move`.

---

## 4. Timestamp/Created_At Tracking

**NO "added_at" field per queue item.** However, timestamps exist at multiple levels:

### **Plan-level timestamps:**
- `plan.created`: When the plan was first created (UTC ISO string)
- `plan.updated`: When plan was last modified

### **Per-item timestamps in related structures:**
- **Overrides:** `overrides[id].created_at`, `updated_at` (UTC ISO string)
  - Set when description/note/cluster is first added
  - Updated when modified
- **Skip entries:** `skipped[id].created_at` (UTC ISO string)
  - Set when finding is skipped
- **Clusters:** `cluster.created_at`, `updated_at`
- **Execution log:** `execution_log[].timestamp` (UTC ISO string)
  - Every action (move, skip, done, resolve, reconcile, cluster_done, focus, reset) is logged

### **Where timestamp is NOT tracked:**
- `queue_order` is just a list of strings (finding IDs) — no metadata per entry
- No "added_to_queue_at" timestamp on findings themselves

**Workaround to track insertion:** Check `execution_log` for "move" actions with `actor="user"` or system reconciliation logs for injection points.

---

## 5. Key Data Structures

### **Plan Model** (schema.py, lines 74-89)
```python
class PlanModel(TypedDict):
    queue_order: list[str]          # Ordered finding IDs
    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry]   # Skipped findings
    overrides: dict[str, ItemOverride]  # Descriptions, notes, cluster refs
    clusters: dict[str, Cluster]    # Named/auto clusters
    active_cluster: str | None      # Focused cluster
    promoted_ids: list[str]         # User-moved-to-top IDs
    superseded: dict[str, SupersededEntry]  # Resolved findings
    execution_log: list[ExecutionLogEntry]  # Audit trail
```

### **ItemOverride** (schema.py, lines 31-38)
```python
class ItemOverride(TypedDict):
    finding_id: str
    description: str | None
    note: str | None
    cluster: str | None
    created_at: str
    updated_at: str
```

### **ExecutionLogEntry** (schema.py, lines 52-60)
```python
class ExecutionLogEntry(TypedDict):
    timestamp: str
    action: str  # "done", "skip", "unskip", "resolve", "reconcile", etc.
    finding_ids: list[str]
    cluster_name: str | None
    actor: str  # "user" | "system" | "agent"
    note: str | None
    detail: dict
```

```
   186→def _reconcile_plan_post_scan(runtime: "ScanRuntime") -> None:
   187→    """Reconcile plan queue metadata and stale subjective review dimensions."""
   188→    try:
   189→        plan_path = runtime.state_path.parent / "plan.json" if runtime.state_path else None
   190→        plan = load_plan(plan_path)
   191→        dirty = False
   192→
   193→        if _apply_plan_reconciliation(plan, runtime.state, reconcile_plan_after_scan):
   194→            dirty = True
   195→
   196→        unscored_changed = _sync_unscored_dimensions(plan, runtime.state, sync_unscored_dimensions)
   197→        if unscored_changed:
   198→            dirty = True
   199→            append_log_entry(plan, "sync_unscored", actor="system",
   200→                             detail={"changes": True})
   201→
   202→        stale_changed = _sync_stale_dimensions(plan, runtime.state, sync_stale_dimensions)
   203→        if stale_changed:
   204→            dirty = True
   205→            append_log_entry(plan, "sync_stale", actor="system",
   206→                             detail={"changes": True})
   207→
   208→        auto_changed = _sync_auto_clusters(plan, runtime.state)
   209→        if auto_changed:
   210→            dirty = True
   211→            append_log_entry(plan, "auto_cluster", actor="system",
   212→                             detail={"changes": True})
   213→
   214→        synth_sync = sync_synthesis_needed(plan, runtime.state)
   215→        if synth_sync.changes:
   216→            dirty = True
   217→            if synth_sync.injected:
   218→                print(
   219→                    colorize(
   220→                        "  Plan: synthesis needed — review findings changed since last synthesis.",
   221→                        "cyan",
   222→                    )
   223→                )
   224→                append_log_entry(plan, "sync_synthesis", actor="system",
   225→                                 detail={"injected": True})
   226→
   227→        seeded = _seed_plan_start_scores(plan, runtime.state)
   228→        if seeded:
   229→            dirty = True
   230→            append_log_entry(plan, "seed_start_scores", actor="system",
   231→                             detail={})
   232→        # Only clear scores that existed before this reconcile pass —
   233→        # never clear scores we just seeded in the same scan.
   234→        if not seeded and _clear_plan_start_scores_if_queue_empty(runtime.state, plan):
   235→            dirty = True
   236→            append_log_entry(plan, "clear_start_scores", actor="system",
   237→                             detail={})
   238→
   239→        if dirty:
   240→            save_plan(plan, plan_path)
   241→    except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS as exc:
   242→        logging.debug("Plan operation skipped: %s", exc)

   330→def _configure_lang_runtime(
   331→    args: argparse.Namespace,
   332→    config: dict[str, object],
   333→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   334→    lang: LangRun | None,
   335→) -> LangRun | None:
   336→    """Populate runtime context and threshold overrides for a selected language."""
   337→    if not lang:
   338→        return None
   339→
   340→    lang_options = resolve_lang_runtime_options(args, lang)
   341→    lang_settings = resolve_lang_settings(config, lang)
   342→    runtime_lang = make_lang_run(
   343→        lang,
   344→        overrides=LangRunOverrides(
   345→            review_cache=state.get("review_cache", {}),
   346→            review_max_age_days=config.get("review_max_age_days", 30),
   347→            runtime_settings=lang_settings,
   348→            runtime_options=lang_options,
   349→            large_threshold_override=config.get("large_files_threshold", 0),
   350→            props_threshold_override=config.get("props_threshold", 0),
   351→        ),
   352→    )
   353→
   354→    lang_capabilities = _state_lang_capabilities(state)
   355→    lang_capabilities[runtime_lang.name] = {
   356→        "fixers": sorted(runtime_lang.fixers.keys()),
   357→        "typecheck_cmd": runtime_lang.typecheck_cmd,
   358→    }
   359→    return runtime_lang

   362→def _reset_subjective_assessments_for_scan_reset(
   363→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   364→    *,
   365→    lang_name: str | None = None,
   366→) -> int:
   367→    """Reset known subjective dimensions to 0 so the next scan starts fresh."""
   368→    assessments = _state_subjective_assessments(state)
   369→
   370→    reset_keys = {
   371→        key.strip()
   372→        for key in assessments
   373→        if isinstance(key, str) and key.strip()
   374→    }
   375→    reset_keys.update(_subjective_reset_dimensions(lang_name=lang_name))
   376→
   377→    now = state_mod.utc_now()
   378→    for key in sorted(reset_keys):
   379→        payload = assessments.get(key)
   380→        if isinstance(payload, dict):
   381→            payload["score"] = 0.0
   382→            payload["source"] = "scan_reset_subjective"
   383→            payload["assessed_at"] = now
   384→            payload["reset_by"] = "scan_reset_subjective"
   385→            payload["placeholder"] = True
   386→            payload.pop("integrity_penalty", None)
   387→            payload.pop("components", None)
   388→            payload.pop("component_scores", None)
   389→            continue
   390→        assessments[key] = {
   391→            "score": 0.0,
   392→            "source": "scan_reset_subjective",
   393→            "assessed_at": now,
   394→            "reset_by": "scan_reset_subjective",
   395→            "placeholder": True,
   396→        }
   397→    return len(reset_keys)
   398→
   399→
   400→def _expire_provisional_manual_override_assessments(
   401→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   402→) -> int:
   403→    """Expire provisional manual-override assessments at scan start."""
   404→    assessments = _state_subjective_assessments(state)
   405→
   406→    now = state_mod.utc_now()
   407→    expired = 0
   408→    for payload in assessments.values():
   409→        if not isinstance(payload, dict):
   410→            continue
   411→        if payload.get("provisional_override") is not True:
   412→            continue
   413→        payload["score"] = 0.0
   414→        payload["source"] = "manual_override_expired"
   415→        payload["assessed_at"] = now
   416→        payload["reset_by"] = "manual_override_expired"
   417→        payload["placeholder"] = True
   418→        payload.pop("provisional_override", None)
   419→        payload.pop("provisional_until_scan", None)
   420→        payload.pop("integrity_penalty", None)
   421→        payload.pop("components", None)
   422→        payload.pop("component_scores", None)
   423→        expired += 1
   424→    return expired
   425→
   426→
   427→def prepare_scan_runtime(args) -> ScanRuntime:
   428→    """Resolve state/config/language and apply scan-time runtime settings."""
   429→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
   430→    state_file = runtime.state_path
   431→    state = runtime.state if isinstance(runtime.state, dict) else {}
   432→    state_mod.ensure_state_defaults(state)
   433→    path = Path(args.path)
   434→    config = runtime.config if isinstance(runtime.config, dict) else {}
   435→    lang_config = resolve_lang(args)
   436→    reset_subjective_count = 0
   437→    expired_manual_override_count = _expire_provisional_manual_override_assessments(
   438→        state
   439→    )
   440→    if getattr(args, "reset_subjective", False):
   441→        reset_subjective_count = _reset_subjective_assessments_for_scan_reset(
   442→            state,
   443→            lang_name=getattr(lang_config, "name", None),
   444→        )
   445→
   446→    include_slow = not getattr(args, "skip_slow", False)
   447→    profile = _resolve_scan_profile(getattr(args, "profile", None), lang_config)
   448→    effective_include_slow = _effective_include_slow(include_slow, profile)
   449→
   450→    lang = _configure_lang_runtime(args, config, state, lang_config)
   451→    coverage_warnings = _seed_runtime_coverage_warnings(lang)
   452→    zone_overrides_raw = config.get("zone_overrides")
   453→    zone_overrides = zone_overrides_raw if isinstance(zone_overrides_raw, dict) else None
   454→
   455→    return ScanRuntime(
   456→        args=args,
   457→        state_path=state_file,
   458→        state=state,
   459→        path=path,
   460→        config=config,
   461→        lang=lang,
   462→        lang_label=f" ({lang.name})" if lang else "",
   463→        profile=profile,
   464→        effective_include_slow=effective_include_slow,
   465→        zone_overrides=zone_overrides,
   466→        reset_subjective_count=reset_subjective_count,
   467→        expired_manual_override_count=expired_manual_override_count,
   468→        coverage_warnings=coverage_warnings,
   469→    )
   470→
   471→
   472→def _augment_with_stale_exclusion_findings(
   473→    findings: list[dict[str, Any]],
   474→    runtime: ScanRuntime,
   475→) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
   476→    """Append stale exclude findings when excluded dirs are unreferenced."""
   477→    extra_exclusions = get_exclusions()
   478→    if not (extra_exclusions and runtime.lang and runtime.lang.file_finder):
   479→        return findings
   480→
   481→    scanned_files = runtime.lang.file_finder(runtime.path)
   482→    stale = _audit_excluded_dirs(
   483→        extra_exclusions, scanned_files, PROJECT_ROOT
   484→    )
   485→    if not stale:
   486→        return findings
   487→
   488→    augmented = list(findings)
   489→    augmented.extend(stale)
   490→    for stale_finding in stale:
   491→        print(colorize(f"  ℹ {stale_finding['summary']}", "dim"))
   492→    return augmented
   493→
   494→
   495→def _augment_with_stale_wontfix_findings(
   496→    findings: list[dict[str, Any]],
   497→    runtime: ScanRuntime,
   498→    *,
   499→    decay_scans: int,
   500→) -> tuple[list[dict[str, Any]], int]:
   501→    """Append re-triage findings for stale/worsening wontfix debt."""
   502→    return _augment_stale_wontfix_impl(
   503→        findings,
   504→        state=runtime.state,
   505→        scan_path=runtime.path,
   506→        project_root=PROJECT_ROOT,
   507→        decay_scans=decay_scans,
   508→    )
   509→
   510→
   511→def run_scan_generation(
   512→    runtime: ScanRuntime,
   513→) -> tuple[list[dict[str, Any]], dict[str, object], dict[str, object] | None]:
   514→    """Run detector pipeline and return findings, potentials, and codebase metrics."""
   515→    enable_file_cache()
   516→    enable_parse_cache()
   517→    try:
   518→        findings, potentials = plan_mod.generate_findings(
   519→            runtime.path,
   520→            lang=runtime.lang,
   521→            options=PlanScanOptions(
   522→                include_slow=runtime.effective_include_slow,
   523→                zone_overrides=runtime.zone_overrides,
   524→                profile=runtime.profile,
   525→            ),
   526→        )
   527→    finally:
   528→        disable_parse_cache()
   529→        disable_file_cache()
   530→
   531→    codebase_metrics = _collect_codebase_metrics(runtime.lang, runtime.path)
   532→    _warn_explicit_lang_with_no_files(
   533→        runtime.args, runtime.lang, runtime.path, codebase_metrics
   534→    )
   535→    findings = _augment_with_stale_exclusion_findings(findings, runtime)
   536→    decay_scans = _coerce_int(
   537→        runtime.config.get("wontfix_decay_scans"),
   538→        default=_WONTFIX_DECAY_SCANS_DEFAULT,
   539→    )
   540→    findings, monitored_wontfix = _augment_with_stale_wontfix_findings(
   541→        findings,
   542→        runtime,
   543→        decay_scans=max(decay_scans, 0),
   544→    )
   545→    potentials["stale_wontfix"] = monitored_wontfix
   546→    return findings, potentials, codebase_metrics
   547→
   548→
   549→def merge_scan_results(
   550→    runtime: ScanRuntime,
   551→    findings: list[dict[str, Any]],
   552→    potentials: dict[str, object],
   553→    codebase_metrics: dict[str, object] | None,
   554→) -> ScanMergeResult:
   555→    """Merge findings into persistent state and return diff + previous score snapshot."""
   556→    scan_path_rel = rel(str(runtime.path))
   557→    prev_scan_path = runtime.state.get("scan_path")
   558→    path_changed = prev_scan_path is not None and prev_scan_path != scan_path_rel
   559→
   560→    if not path_changed:
   561→        prev = state_mod.score_snapshot(runtime.state)
   562→    else:
   563→        prev = state_mod.ScoreSnapshot(None, None, None, None)
   564→    prev_dim_scores = (
   565→        runtime.state.get("dimension_scores", {}) if not path_changed else {}
   566→    )
   567→
   568→    if runtime.lang and runtime.lang.zone_map is not None:
   569→        runtime.state["zone_distribution"] = runtime.lang.zone_map.counts()
   570→    _persist_scan_coverage(runtime.state, runtime.lang)
   571→
   572→    target_score = target_strict_score_from_config(runtime.config, fallback=95.0)
   573→
   574→    diff = state_mod.merge_scan(
   575→        runtime.state,
   576→        findings,
   577→        options=state_mod.MergeScanOptions(
   578→            lang=runtime.lang.name if runtime.lang else None,
   579→            scan_path=scan_path_rel,
   580→            force_resolve=getattr(runtime.args, "force_resolve", False),
   581→            exclude=get_exclusions(),
   582→            potentials=potentials,
   583→            codebase_metrics=codebase_metrics,
   584→            include_slow=runtime.effective_include_slow,
   585→            ignore=runtime.config.get("ignore", []),
   586→            subjective_integrity_target=target_score,
   587→        ),
   588→    )
   589→
   590→    issues_mod.expire_stale_holistic(
   591→        runtime.state, runtime.config.get("holistic_max_age_days", 30)
   592→    )
   593→    state_mod.save_state(
   594→        runtime.state,
   595→        runtime.state_path,
   596→        subjective_integrity_target=target_score,
   597→    )
   598→
   599→    _clear_needs_rescan_flag(runtime.config)
   600→    _reconcile_plan_post_scan(runtime)
   601→
   602→    return ScanMergeResult(
   603→        diff=diff,
   604→        prev_overall=prev.overall,
   605→        prev_objective=prev.objective,
   606→        prev_strict=prev.strict,
   607→        prev_verified=prev.verified,
   608→        prev_dim_scores=prev_dim_scores,
   609→    )
   610→
   611→
   612→def resolve_noise_snapshot(
   613→    state: state_mod.StateModel,
   614→    config: dict[str, object],
   615→) -> ScanNoiseSnapshot:
   616→    """Resolve noise budget settings and hidden finding counters."""
   617→    noise_budget, global_noise_budget, budget_warning = (
   618→        state_mod.resolve_finding_noise_settings(config)
   619→    )
   620→    findings_by_id = _state_findings(state)
   621→    open_findings = [
   622→        finding
   623→        for finding in state_mod.path_scoped_findings(
   624→            findings_by_id, state.get("scan_path")
   625→        ).values()
   626→        if finding.get("status") == "open"
   627→    ]
   628→    _, hidden_by_detector = state_mod.apply_finding_noise_budget(
   629→        open_findings,
   630→        budget=noise_budget,
   631→        global_budget=global_noise_budget,
   632→    )
   633→
   634→    return ScanNoiseSnapshot(
   635→        noise_budget=noise_budget,
   636→        global_noise_budget=global_noise_budget,
   637→        budget_warning=budget_warning,
   638→        hidden_by_detector=hidden_by_detector,
   639→        hidden_total=sum(hidden_by_detector.values()),
   640→    )
   641→
   642→
   643→def persist_reminder_history(
   644→    runtime: ScanRuntime,
   645→    narrative: dict[str, object],
   646→) -> None:
   647→    """Persist reminder history emitted by narrative computation."""
   648→    if not (narrative and "reminder_history" in narrative):
   649→        return
   650→
   651→    runtime.state["reminder_history"] = narrative["reminder_history"]
   652→    target_score = target_strict_score_from_config(runtime.config, fallback=95.0)
   653→    state_mod.save_state(
   654→        runtime.state,
   655→        runtime.state_path,
   656→        subjective_integrity_target=target_score,
   657→    )
   658→
   659→
   660→__all__ = [
   661→    "ScanStateContractError",
   662→    "ScanMergeResult",
   663→    "ScanNoiseSnapshot",
   664→    "ScanRuntime",
   665→    "merge_scan_results",
   666→    "persist_reminder_history",
   667→    "prepare_scan_runtime",
   668→    "resolve_noise_snapshot",
   669→    "run_scan_generation",
   670→]
   671→
```

```
1→"""State filtering, ignore rules, and finding pattern matching."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import fnmatch
     6→
     7→__all__ = [
     8→    "finding_in_scan_scope",
     9→    "open_scope_breakdown",
    10→    "path_scoped_findings",
    11→    "is_ignored",
    12→    "matched_ignore_pattern",
    13→    "remove_ignored_findings",
    14→    "add_ignore",
    15→    "make_finding",
    16→]
    17→
    18→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import (
    19→    Finding,
    20→    StateModel,
    21→    ensure_state_defaults,
    22→    utc_now,
    23→    validate_state_invariants,
    24→)
    25→from desloppify.core.discovery_api import rel
    26→
    27→
    28→def path_scoped_findings(
    29→    findings: dict[str, Finding],
    30→    scan_path: str | None,
    31→) -> dict[str, Finding]:
    32→    """Filter findings to those within the given scan path."""
    33→    return {
    34→        finding_id: finding
    35→        for finding_id, finding in findings.items()
    36→        if finding_in_scan_scope(str(finding.get("file", "")), scan_path)
    37→    }
    38→
    39→
    40→def finding_in_scan_scope(file_path: str, scan_path: str | None) -> bool:
    41→    """Return True when a file path belongs to the active scan scope."""
    42→    if not scan_path or scan_path == ".":
    43→        return True
    44→    prefix = scan_path.rstrip("/") + "/"
    45→    return (
    46→        file_path.startswith(prefix)
    47→        or file_path == scan_path
    48→        or file_path == "."
    49→    )
    50→
    51→
    52→def open_scope_breakdown(
    53→    findings: dict[str, Finding],
    54→    scan_path: str | None,
    55→    *,
    56→    detector: str | None = None,
    57→) -> dict[str, int]:
    58→    """Return open-finding counts split by in-scope vs out-of-scope carryover."""
    59→    in_scope = 0
    60→    out_of_scope = 0
    61→
    62→    for finding in findings.values():
    63→        if finding.get("suppressed"):
    64→            continue
    65→        if finding.get("status") != "open":
    66→            continue
    67→        if detector is not None and finding.get("detector") != detector:
    68→            continue
    69→        file_path = str(finding.get("file", ""))
    70→        if finding_in_scan_scope(file_path, scan_path):
    71→            in_scope += 1
    72→        else:
    73→            out_of_scope += 1
    74→
    75→    return {
    76→        "in_scope": in_scope,
    77→        "out_of_scope": out_of_scope,
    78→        "global": in_scope + out_of_scope,
    79→    }
    80→
    81→
    82→def is_ignored(finding_id: str, file: str, ignore_patterns: list[str]) -> bool:
    83→    """Check if a finding matches any ignore pattern (glob, ID prefix, or file path)."""
    84→    return matched_ignore_pattern(finding_id, file, ignore_patterns) is not None
    85→
    86→
    87→def matched_ignore_pattern(
    88→    finding_id: str, file: str, ignore_patterns: list[str]
    89→) -> str | None:
    90→    """Return the ignore pattern that matched, if any."""
    91→    for pattern in ignore_patterns:
    92→        if "*" in pattern:
    93→            target = finding_id if "::" in pattern else file
    94→            if fnmatch.fnmatch(target, pattern):
    95→                return pattern
    96→            continue
    97→
    98→        if "::" in pattern:
    99→            if finding_id.startswith(pattern):
   100→                return pattern
   101→            continue
   102→
   103→        if file == pattern or file == rel(pattern):
   104→            return pattern
   105→
   106→    return None
   107→
   108→
   109→def remove_ignored_findings(state: StateModel, pattern: str) -> int:
   110→    """Suppress findings matching an ignore pattern. Returns count affected."""
   111→    ensure_state_defaults(state)
   112→    matched_ids = [
   113→        finding_id
   114→        for finding_id, finding in state["findings"].items()
   115→        if is_ignored(finding_id, finding["file"], [pattern])
   116→    ]
   117→    now = utc_now()
   118→    for finding_id in matched_ids:
   119→        finding = state["findings"][finding_id]
   120→        finding["suppressed"] = True
   121→        finding["suppressed_at"] = now
   122→        finding["suppression_pattern"] = pattern
   123→    # Deferred import to avoid circular dependency with engine._state.scoring
   124→    from desloppify.engine._state.scoring import _recompute_stats
   125→
   126→    _recompute_stats(state, scan_path=state.get("scan_path"))
   127→    validate_state_invariants(state)
   128→    return len(matched_ids)
   129→
   130→
   131→def add_ignore(state: StateModel, pattern: str) -> int:
   132→    """Add an ignore pattern and remove existing matching findings."""
   133→    ensure_state_defaults(state)
   134→    config = state.setdefault("config", {})
   135→    ignores = config.setdefault("ignore", [])
   136→    if pattern not in ignores:
   137→        ignores.append(pattern)
   138→    return remove_ignored_findings(state, pattern)
   139→
   140→
   141→def make_finding(
   142→    detector: str,
   143→    file: str,
   144→    name: str,
   145→    *,
   146→    tier: int,
   147→    confidence: str,
   148→    summary: str,
   149→    detail: dict | None = None,
   150→) -> Finding:
   151→    """Create a normalized finding dict with a stable ID."""
   152→    rfile = rel(file)
   153→    finding_id = f"{detector}::{rfile}::{name}" if name else f"{detector}::{rfile}"
   154→    now = utc_now()
   155→    return {
   156→        "id": finding_id,
   157→        "detector": detector,
   158→        "file": rfile,
   159→        "tier": tier,
   160→        "confidence": confidence,
   161→        "summary": summary,
   162→        "detail": detail or {},
   163→        "status": "open",
   164→        "note": None,
   165→        "first_seen": now,
   166→        "last_seen": now,
   167→        "resolved_at": None,
   168→        "reopen_count": 0,
   169→    }
   170→
   171→
   172→def _matches_pattern(finding_id: str, finding: dict[str, str], pattern: str) -> bool:
   173→    """Check if a finding matches by ID, glob, prefix, detector, or path."""
   174→    return (
   175→        finding_id == pattern
   176→        or ("*" in pattern and fnmatch.fnmatch(finding_id, pattern))
   177→        or ("::" in pattern and finding_id.startswith(pattern))
   178→        or (
   179→            finding.get("detector") == pattern
   180→            or finding["file"] == pattern
   181→            or finding["file"].startswith(pattern.rstrip("/") + "/")
   182→        )
   183→    )
   184→
```

```
    29→def _cmd_plan_show(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    30→    """Show plan metadata summary."""

    32→    ordered = len(plan.get("queue_order", []))
    33→    skipped = plan.get("skipped", {})
    34→    total_skipped = len(skipped)
    35→    temp_count = sum(1 for e in skipped.values() if e.get("kind") == "temporary")

    37→    fp_count = sum(1 for e in skipped.values() if e.get("kind") == "false_positive")
    38→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
    39→    overrides = plan.get("overrides", {})
    40→    active = plan.get("active_cluster")
    41→    superseded = len(plan.get("superseded", {}))
    42→
    43→    # Count meaningful annotations (descriptions and notes)
    44→    described = sum(1 for o in overrides.values() if o.get("description"))
    45→    noted = sum(1 for o in overrides.values() if o.get("note"))
    46→
    47→    print(colorize("  Living Plan Status", "bold"))
    48→    print(colorize("  " + "─" * 40, "dim"))
    49→    print(f"  Queue:            {ordered} items prioritized")
    50→    if total_skipped:
    51→        print(f"  Skipped:          {total_skipped} (temp: {temp_count}, wontfix: {perm_count}, fp: {fp_count})")
    52→    else:
    53→        print(f"  Skipped:          0")
    54→    print(f"  Clusters:         {len(clusters)}")
    55→    if clusters:
    56→        for name, cluster in clusters.items():
    57→            desc = cluster.get("description") or ""
    58→            member_count = len(cluster.get("finding_ids", []))
    59→            marker = " (focused)" if name == active else ""
    60→            desc_str = f" — {desc}" if desc else ""
    61→            print(f"    {name}: {member_count} items{desc_str}{marker}")
    62→    if described or noted:
    63→        print(f"  Annotations:      {described} described, {noted} noted")
    64→    if active:
    65→        print(f"  Focus:            {active}")
    66→    if superseded:
    67→        print(f"  Disappeared:      {superseded} (resolved or removed since last scan)")
    68→
    69→
    70→def _cmd_plan_reset(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    71→    """Reset the plan to empty."""
    72→    plan = load_plan()
    73→    queue_len = len(plan.get("queue_order", []))
    74→    cluster_count = len(plan.get("clusters", {}))
    75→    reset_plan(plan)
    76→    append_log_entry(
    77→        plan, "reset", actor="user",
    78→        detail={"previous_queue_size": queue_len, "previous_cluster_count": cluster_count},
    79→    )
    80→    save_plan(plan)
    81→    print(colorize("  Plan reset to empty.", "green"))
    82→
    83→
    84→def cmd_plan(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    85→    """Dispatch plan subcommand or generate markdown output."""
    86→    plan_action = getattr(args, "plan_action", None)
    87→
    88→    if plan_action is None:
    89→        _cmd_plan_generate(args)
    90→        return
    91→
    92→    if plan_action == "show":
    93→        _cmd_plan_show(args)
    94→        return
    95→
    96→    if plan_action == "queue":
    97→        cmd_plan_queue(args)
    98→        return
    99→
   100→    if plan_action == "reset":
   101→        _cmd_plan_reset(args)
   102→        return
   103→
   104→    if plan_action == "move":
   105→        cmd_plan_move(args)
   106→        return
   107→
   108→    if plan_action in (
   109→        "describe", "note", "focus",
   110→        "skip", "unskip", "reopen", "done",
   111→    ):
   112→        dispatch = {
   113→            "describe": cmd_plan_describe,
   114→            "done": cmd_plan_done,
   115→            "note": cmd_plan_note,
   116→            "focus": cmd_plan_focus,
   117→            "skip": cmd_plan_skip,
   118→            "unskip": cmd_plan_unskip,
   119→            "reopen": cmd_plan_reopen,
   120→        }
   121→        dispatch[plan_action](args)
   122→        return
   123→
   124→    if plan_action == "cluster":
   125→        cmd_cluster_dispatch(args)
   126→        return
   127→
   128→    if plan_action == "synthesize":
   129→        from desloppify.app.commands.plan.synthesize_handlers import cmd_plan_synthesize
   130→
   131→        cmd_plan_synthesize(args)
   132→        return
   133→
   134→    print(f"Unknown plan action: {plan_action}")
   135→
   136→
   137→# Backwards-compatible alias
   138→cmd_plan_output = cmd_plan
   139→
   140→__all__ = ["cmd_plan", "cmd_plan_output"]
   141→
```

```
    57→def _print_scan_header(lang_label: str) -> None:
    58→    """Print the scan header line."""

    62→def _print_scan_complete_banner() -> None:

    71→def _show_scan_visibility(noise, effective_include_slow: bool) -> None:
    72→    """Print fast-scan and noise budget visibility hints."""
    73→    if not effective_include_slow:
    74→        print(colorize("  * Fast scan — slow phases (duplicates) skipped", "yellow"))
    75→    if noise.budget_warning:
    76→        print(colorize(f"  * {noise.budget_warning}", "yellow"))
    77→    if noise.hidden_total:
    78→        print(
    79→            colorize(
    80→                f"  * {noise.hidden_total} findings hidden (showing {noise.noise_budget}/detector). "
    81→                "Use `desloppify show <detector>` to see all.",
    82→                "dim",
    83→            )
    84→        )
    85→

    87→def _show_coverage_preflight(runtime) -> None:
    88→    """Print preflight warnings when scan coverage confidence is reduced."""
    89→    warnings = getattr(runtime, "coverage_warnings", []) or []
    90→    if not isinstance(warnings, list) or not warnings:
    91→        return
    92→
    93→    for entry in warnings:
    94→        if not isinstance(entry, dict):
    95→            continue
    96→        summary = str(entry.get("summary", "")).strip()
    97→        impact = str(entry.get("impact", "")).strip()
    98→        remediation = str(entry.get("remediation", "")).strip()
    99→        detector = str(entry.get("detector", "")).strip() or "detector"
   100→
   101→        headline = summary or f"Coverage reduced for `{detector}`."
   102→        print(colorize(f"  * Coverage preflight: {headline}", "yellow"))
   103→        if impact:
   104→            print(colorize(f"    Repercussion: {impact}", "dim"))
   105→        if remediation:
   106→            print(colorize(f"    Fix: {remediation}", "dim"))
   107→

   111→

   115→def cmd_scan(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   116→    """Run all detectors, update persistent state, show diff."""
   117→    scan_preflight_mod.scan_queue_preflight(args)
   118→    try:
   119→        runtime = prepare_scan_runtime(args)
   120→    except LangRuntimeOptionsError as exc:
   121→        lang_cfg = resolve_lang(args)
   122→        lang_name = lang_cfg.name if lang_cfg else "selected"
   123→        print_lang_runtime_options_error(exc, lang_name=lang_name)
   124→        raise SystemExit(2) from exc
   125→    except ScanStateContractError as exc:
   126→        print(colorize(f"  {exc}", "red"))
   127→        raise SystemExit(2) from exc
   128→    orchestrator = ScanOrchestrator(
   129→        runtime,
   130→        run_scan_generation_fn=run_scan_generation,
   131→        merge_scan_results_fn=merge_scan_results,
   132→        resolve_noise_snapshot_fn=resolve_noise_snapshot,
   133→        persist_reminder_history_fn=persist_reminder_history,
   134→    )
   135→    _print_scan_header(runtime.lang_label)
   136→    if runtime.reset_subjective_count > 0:
   137→        print(
   138→            colorize(
   139→                "  * Subjective reset "
   140→                f"{runtime.reset_subjective_count} subjective dimensions to 0",
   141→                "yellow",
   142→            )
   143→        )
   144→    if runtime.expired_manual_override_count > 0:
   145→        print(
   146→            colorize(
   147→                "  * Expired provisional manual-override assessments: "
   148→                f"{runtime.expired_manual_override_count} dimension(s) reset to 0. "
   149→                "Use trusted `review --run-batches --runner codex --parallel --scan-after-import` to replace them.",
   150→                "yellow",
   151→            )
   152→        )
   153→    _show_coverage_preflight(runtime)
   154→
   155→    findings, potentials, codebase_metrics = orchestrator.generate()
   156→    merge = orchestrator.merge(findings, potentials, codebase_metrics)
   157→    _print_scan_complete_banner()
   158→
   159→    noise = orchestrator.noise_snapshot()
   160→
   161→    target_value = target_strict_score_from_config(runtime.config, fallback=95.0)
   162→
   163→    show_diff_summary(merge.diff)
   164→    show_score_delta(
   165→        runtime.state,
   166→        merge.prev_overall,
   167→        merge.prev_objective,
   168→        merge.prev_strict,
   169→        merge.prev_verified,
   170→        target_strict=target_value,
   171→    )
   172→    # Nudge: if plan_start_scores was just seeded, tell the agent about the lifecycle.
   173→    _print_plan_workflow_nudge(runtime.state)
   174→    _show_scan_visibility(noise, runtime.effective_include_slow)
   175→    show_scorecard_subjective_measures(runtime.state)
   176→    show_score_model_breakdown(runtime.state)
   177→
   178→    new_dim_scores = runtime.state.get("dimension_scores", {})
   179→    if new_dim_scores and merge.prev_dim_scores:
   180→        show_dimension_deltas(merge.prev_dim_scores, new_dim_scores)
   181→
   182→    warnings, narrative = show_post_scan_analysis(
   183→        merge.diff,
   184→        runtime.state,
   185→        runtime.lang,
   186→        target_strict_score=target_value,
   187→    )
   188→    orchestrator.persist_reminders(narrative)
   189→
   190→    write_query(
   191→        build_scan_query_payload(
   192→            runtime.state,
   193→            runtime.config,
   194→            runtime.profile,
   195→            merge.diff,
   196→            warnings,
   197→            narrative,
   198→            merge,
   199→            noise,
   200→        ),
   201→        query_file=query_file_path(),
   202→    )
   203→
   204→    badge_emit = emit_scorecard_badge(args, runtime.config, runtime.state)
   205→    if isinstance(badge_emit, tuple):
   206→        badge_path, _badge_result = badge_emit
   207→    else:  # Backward-compatible shape for monkeypatched tests.
   208→        badge_path = badge_emit
   209→    _print_llm_summary(runtime.state, badge_path, narrative, merge.diff)
   210→    auto_update_skill()

   212→
   213→__all__ = [
```

```
1→"""Score and diff summary output for scan command."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import logging
     6→
     7→from desloppify import state as state_mod
     8→from desloppify.app.commands.scan.scan_helpers import format_delta
     9→from desloppify.app.commands.status_parts.strict_target import (
    10→    format_strict_target_progress,
    11→)
    12→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.score_update import _print_strict_target_nudge
    13→from desloppify.app.commands.scan.scan_reporting_llm import _is_agent_environment
    14→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize
    15→from desloppify.engine.concerns import generate_concerns
    16→
    17→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    18→
    19→
    20→def _consecutive_subjective_integrity_status(state: dict, status: str) -> int:
    21→    """Return consecutive trailing scans with the given subjective-integrity status."""
    22→    history = state.get("scan_history", [])
    23→    if not isinstance(history, list):
    24→        return 0
    25→
    26→    streak = 0
    27→    for entry in reversed(history):
    28→        if not isinstance(entry, dict):
    29→            break
    30→        integrity = entry.get("subjective_integrity")
    31→        if not isinstance(integrity, dict):
    32→            break
    33→        if integrity.get("status") != status:
    34→            break
    35→        streak += 1
    36→    return streak
    37→
    38→
    39→def _show_score_reveal(
    40→    state: dict,
    41→    new: state_mod.ScoreSnapshot,
    42→    *,
    43→    target_strict: float | None = None,
    44→) -> None:
    45→    """Show before/after score comparison when a queue cycle just completed.
    46→
    47→    This fires when the plan had ``plan_start_scores`` and the queue was empty
    48→    at scan time (i.e. the reconcile block cleared plan_start_scores).  We detect
    49→    this by checking the plan *before* the clear happened — since merge_scan_results
    50→    clears it, we peek at the ``_last_score_reveal`` stash it leaves behind.
    51→    """
    52→    # scan_workflow stashes the old plan_start_scores on state as a transient
    53→    # key when it clears them (queue empty).  Pop it here for the reveal.
    54→    plan_start = state.pop("_plan_start_scores_for_reveal", None)
    55→    if not isinstance(plan_start, dict) or not plan_start.get("strict"):
    56→        return
    57→
    58→    old_strict = float(plan_start["strict"])
    59→    new_strict = float(new.strict or 0)
    60→    delta = round(new_strict - old_strict, 1)
    61→    delta_str = f" ({'+' if delta > 0 else ''}{delta:.1f})" if abs(delta) >= 0.05 else ""
    62→
    63→    bar = "=" * 50
    64→    print(colorize(f"  {bar}", "cyan"))
    65→    print(colorize("  SCORE UPDATE — Queue cycle complete!", "bold"))
    66→    print(colorize(f"  Plan-start: strict {old_strict:.1f}/100", "dim"))
    67→    print(colorize(f"  Updated:    strict {new_strict:.1f}/100{delta_str}", "cyan"))
    68→    if target_strict is not None:
    69→        target_gap = round(target_strict - new_strict, 1)
    70→        if target_gap > 0:
    71→            print(colorize(f"  Target:     {target_strict:.1f} (+{target_gap:.1f} to go)", "dim"))
    72→        else:
    73→            print(colorize(f"  Target:     {target_strict:.1f} — reached!", "green"))
    74→    print(colorize(f"  {bar}", "cyan"))
    75→
    76→
    77→def show_diff_summary(diff: dict):
    78→    """Print the +new / -resolved / reopened one-liner."""
    79→    diff_parts = []
    80→    if diff["new"]:
    81→        diff_parts.append(colorize(f"+{diff['new']} new", "yellow"))
    82→    if diff["auto_resolved"]:
    83→        diff_parts.append(colorize(f"-{diff['auto_resolved']} resolved", "green"))
    84→    if diff["reopened"]:
    85→        diff_parts.append(colorize(f"↻{diff['reopened']} reopened", "red"))
    86→    if diff_parts:
    87→        print(f"  {' · '.join(diff_parts)}")
    88→    else:
    89→        print(colorize("  No changes since last scan", "dim"))
    90→    if diff.get("suspect_detectors"):
    91→        print(
    92→            colorize(
    93→                "  ⚠ Skipped auto-resolve for: "
    94→                f"{', '.join(diff['suspect_detectors'])} (returned 0 — likely transient)",
    95→                "yellow",
    96→            )
    97→        )
    98→
    99→
   100→def show_score_delta(
   101→    state: dict,
   102→    prev_overall: float | None,
   103→    prev_objective: float | None,
   104→    prev_strict: float | None,
   105→    prev_verified: float | None = None,
   106→    non_comparable_reason: str | None = None,
   107→    *,
   108→    target_strict: float | None = None,
   109→):
   110→    """Print the canonical score quartet with deltas."""
   111→    stats = state["stats"]
   112→    new = state_mod.score_snapshot(state)
   113→
   114→    if (
   115→        new.overall is None
   116→        or new.objective is None
   117→        or new.strict is None
   118→        or new.verified is None
   119→    ):
   120→        print(
   121→            colorize(
   122→                "  Scores unavailable — run a full scan with language detectors enabled.",
   123→                "yellow",
   124→            )
   125→        )
   126→        return
   127→
   128→    # Detect score-reveal scan: plan_start_scores existed and queue was clear
   129→    _show_score_reveal(state, new, target_strict=target_strict)
   130→
   131→    overall_delta_str, overall_color = format_delta(new.overall, prev_overall)
   132→    objective_delta_str, objective_color = format_delta(new.objective, prev_objective)
   133→    strict_delta_str, strict_color = format_delta(new.strict, prev_strict)
   134→    verified_delta_str, verified_color = format_delta(new.verified, prev_verified)
   135→    print(
   136→        "  Scores: "
   137→        + colorize(f"overall {new.overall:.1f}/100{overall_delta_str}", overall_color)
   138→        + colorize(
   139→            f"  objective {new.objective:.1f}/100{objective_delta_str}",
   140→            objective_color,
   141→        )
   142→        + colorize(f"  strict {new.strict:.1f}/100{strict_delta_str}", strict_color)
   143→        + colorize(
   144→            f"  verified {new.verified:.1f}/100{verified_delta_str}",
   145→            verified_color,
   146→        )
   147→    )
   148→    if isinstance(non_comparable_reason, str) and non_comparable_reason.strip():
   149→        print(colorize(f"  Δ non-comparable: {non_comparable_reason.strip()}", "yellow"))
   150→    # Surface wontfix debt gap prominently when significant
   151→    wontfix = stats.get("wontfix", 0)
   152→    gap = (new.overall or 0) - (new.strict or 0)
   153→    if gap >= 5 and wontfix >= 10:
   154→        print(
   155→            colorize(
   156→                f"  ⚠ {gap:.1f}-point gap between overall and strict — "
   157→                f"{wontfix} wontfix items represent hidden debt",
   158→                "yellow",
   159→            )
   160→        )
   161→
   162→    # Score legend — shown on first scan or when strict gap is significant
   163→    scan_count = state.get("scan_count", 0)
   164→    if scan_count <= 1 or gap > 10 or _is_agent_environment():
   165→        print(colorize("  Score guide:", "dim"))
   166→        print(colorize("    overall  = 40% mechanical + 60% subjective (lenient — ignores wontfix)", "dim"))
   167→        print(colorize("    objective = mechanical detectors only (no subjective review)", "dim"))
   168→        print(colorize("    strict   = like overall, but wontfix counts against you  <-- your north star", "dim"))
   169→        print(colorize("    verified = strict, but only credits scan-verified fixes", "dim"))
   170→
   171→    # Show strict target progress
   172→    if target_strict is not None and new.strict is not None:
   173→        _print_strict_target_nudge(new.strict, target_strict, show_next=False)
   174→
   175→    integrity = state.get("subjective_integrity", {})
   176→    if isinstance(integrity, dict):
   177→        status = integrity.get("status")
   178→        matched_count = int(integrity.get("matched_count", 0) or 0)
   179→        target = integrity.get("target_score")
   180→        if status == "penalized":
   181→            print(
   182→                colorize(
   183→                    "  ⚠ Subjective integrity: "
   184→                    f"{matched_count} target-matched dimensions were reset to 0.0 "
   185→                    f"({'target ' + str(target) if target is not None else 'target threshold'}).",
   186→                    "red",
   187→                )
   188→            )
   189→            streak = _consecutive_subjective_integrity_status(state, "penalized")
   190→            if streak >= 2:
   191→                print(
   192→                    colorize(
   193→                        "    Repeated penalty across scans. Use a blind, isolated reviewer "
   194→                        "on `.desloppify/review_packet_blind.json` and re-import before trusting subjective scores.",
   195→                        "yellow",
   196→                    )
   197→                )
   198→        elif status == "warn":
   199→            print(
   200→                colorize(
   201→                    "  ⚠ Subjective integrity: "
   202→                    f"{matched_count} dimension matched the target "
   203→                    f"({'target ' + str(target) if target is not None else 'target threshold'}). Re-review recommended.",
   204→                    "yellow",
   205→                )
   206→            )
   207→            streak = _consecutive_subjective_integrity_status(state, "warn")
   208→            if streak >= 2:
   209→                print(
   210→                    colorize(
   211→                        "    This warning has repeated. Prefer "
   212→                        "`desloppify review --run-batches --runner codex --parallel --scan-after-import` "
   213→                        "or run a blind reviewer pass before import.",
   214→                        "yellow",
   215→                )
   216→            )
   217→
   218→
   219→def show_concern_count(state: dict, lang_name: str | None = None) -> None:
   220→    """Print concern count if any exist."""
   221→    try:
   222→        concerns = generate_concerns(state, lang_name=lang_name)
   223→        if concerns:
   224→            print(
   225→                colorize(
   226→                    f"  {len(concerns)} potential design concern{'s' if len(concerns) != 1 else ''}"
   227→                    " (run `show concerns` to view)",
   228→                    "cyan",
   229→                )
   230→            )
   231→    except (ImportError, AttributeError, TypeError, ValueError) as exc:
   232→        logger.debug("Concern generation failed (best-effort): %s", exc)
   233→
   234→
   235→def show_strict_target_progress(strict_target: dict | None) -> tuple[float | None, float | None]:
   236→    """Print strict target progress lines and return (target, gap)."""
   237→    lines, target, gap = format_strict_target_progress(strict_target)
   238→    for message, style in lines:
   239→        print(colorize(message, style))
   240→    return target, gap
   241→
   242→
   243→__all__ = ["show_concern_count", "show_diff_summary", "show_score_delta", "show_strict_target_progress"]
   244→
```

```
1→"""Post-scan narrative and integrity reporting."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify import state as state_mod
     6→from desloppify.engine.plan import has_living_plan, load_plan
     7→from desloppify.intelligence import narrative as narrative_mod
     8→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize
     9→
    10→
    11→def _coerce_coverage_confidence(value: object, *, default: float = 1.0) -> float:
    12→    try:
    13→        parsed = float(value)
    14→    except (TypeError, ValueError):
    15→        return default
    16→    return max(0.0, min(1.0, parsed))
    17→
    18→
    19→def _current_scan_coverage(state: dict, lang) -> dict:
    20→    scan_coverage = state.get("scan_coverage", {})
    21→    if not isinstance(scan_coverage, dict):
    22→        return {}
    23→    lang_name = getattr(lang, "name", None) if lang is not None else None
    24→    if isinstance(lang_name, str) and lang_name:
    25→        entry = scan_coverage.get(lang_name, {})
    26→        return entry if isinstance(entry, dict) else {}
    27→    return {}
    28→
    29→
    30→def _coverage_reduction_warnings(state: dict, lang) -> list[str]:
    31→    coverage = _current_scan_coverage(state, lang)
    32→    detectors = coverage.get("detectors", {})
    33→    if not isinstance(detectors, dict):
    34→        return []
    35→
    36→    warnings: list[str] = []
    37→    for detector, payload in detectors.items():
    38→        if not isinstance(payload, dict):
    39→            continue
    40→        status = str(payload.get("status", "full")).strip().lower()
    41→        confidence = _coerce_coverage_confidence(payload.get("confidence"), default=1.0)
    42→        if status != "reduced" and confidence >= 1.0:
    43→            continue
    44→
    45→        summary = str(payload.get("summary", "")).strip()
    46→        impact = str(payload.get("impact", "")).strip()
    47→        remediation = str(payload.get("remediation", "")).strip()
    48→        detector_label = str(detector).strip() or "detector"
    49→
    50→        line = f"Coverage reduced ({detector_label}): {summary or 'reduced detector confidence.'}"
    51→        if impact:
    52→            line += f" Repercussion: {impact}"
    53→        if remediation:
    54→            line += f" Fix: {remediation}"
    55→        warnings.append(line)
    56→    return warnings
    57→
    58→
    59→def show_post_scan_analysis(
    60→    diff: dict,
    61→    state: dict,
    62→    lang,
    63→    *,
    64→    target_strict_score: float = 95.0,
    65→) -> tuple[list[str], dict]:
    66→    """Print critical warnings + headline + pointers. Returns (warnings, narrative)."""
    67→    # Critical warnings only (reopened, cascading, chronic, coverage reduction)
    68→    warnings: list[str] = []
    69→    if diff["reopened"] > 5:
    70→        warnings.append(
    71→            f"{diff['reopened']} findings reopened — was a previous fix reverted?"
    72→        )
    73→    if diff["new"] > 10 and diff["auto_resolved"] < 3:
    74→        warnings.append(
    75→            f"{diff['new']} new findings with few resolutions — likely cascading."
    76→        )
    77→    chronic = diff.get("chronic_reopeners", [])
    78→    chronic_count = len(chronic) if isinstance(chronic, list) else chronic
    79→    if chronic_count > 0:
    80→        warnings.append(
    81→            f"⟳ {chronic_count} chronic reopener{'s' if chronic_count != 1 else ''} — "
    82→            "run `desloppify show --chronic` to see them."
    83→        )
    84→    warnings.extend(_coverage_reduction_warnings(state, lang))
    85→
    86→    for warning in warnings:
    87→        print(colorize(f"  {warning}", "yellow"))
    88→    if warnings:
    89→        print()
    90→
    91→    # Load plan for narrative cluster awareness
    92→    _plan_data = None
    93→    _plan = load_plan()
    94→    if _plan.get("queue_order") or _plan.get("clusters"):
    95→        _plan_data = _plan
    96→
    97→    # Single narrative headline
    98→    lang_name = lang.name if lang else None
    99→    narrative = narrative_mod.compute_narrative(
   100→        state,
   101→        context=narrative_mod.NarrativeContext(
   102→            diff=diff,
   103→            lang=lang_name,
   104→            command="scan",
   105→            plan=_plan_data,
   106→        ),
   107→    )
   108→    if narrative.get("headline"):
   109→        print(colorize(f"  → {narrative['headline']}", "cyan"))
   110→
   111→    # Pointers — include plan reference when active
   112→    _has_plan = _plan_data is not None or has_living_plan()
   113→
   114→    print(colorize("  Run `desloppify next` for the highest-priority item.", "dim"))
   115→    if _has_plan:
   116→        print(colorize("  Run `desloppify plan` to see the updated living plan.", "dim"))
   117→    print(colorize("  Run `desloppify status` for the full dashboard.", "dim"))
   118→    print()
   119→
   120→    return warnings, narrative
   121→
   122→
   123→def show_score_integrity(state: dict, diff: dict):
   124→    """Show Score Integrity section — surfaces wontfix debt and ignored findings."""
   125→    stats = state.get("stats", {})
   126→    wontfix = stats.get("wontfix", 0)
   127→    ignored = diff.get("ignored", 0)
   128→    ignore_patterns = diff.get("ignore_patterns", 0)
   129→    score_confidence = state.get("score_confidence", {})
   130→    confidence_reduced = (
   131→        isinstance(score_confidence, dict)
   132→        and str(score_confidence.get("status", "full")).lower() == "reduced"
   133→    )
   134→
   135→    if wontfix <= 5 and ignored <= 0 and ignore_patterns <= 0 and not confidence_reduced:
   136→        return
   137→
   138→    overall = state_mod.get_overall_score(state)
   139→    strict = state_mod.get_strict_score(state)
   140→    strict_gap = (
   141→        round(overall - strict, 1) if overall is not None and strict is not None else 0
   142→    )
   143→
   144→    # Wontfix % of actionable findings (open + wontfix + fixed + auto_resolved + false_positive)
   145→    actionable = (
   146→        stats.get("open", 0)
   147→        + wontfix
   148→        + stats.get("fixed", 0)
   149→        + stats.get("auto_resolved", 0)
   150→        + stats.get("false_positive", 0)
   151→    )
   152→    wontfix_pct = round(wontfix / actionable * 100) if actionable else 0
   153→
   154→    print(colorize("  " + "┄" * 2 + " Score Integrity " + "┄" * 37, "dim"))
   155→
   156→    if wontfix > 5:
   157→        if wontfix_pct > 50:
   158→            style = "red"
   159→            msg = (
   160→                f"  ❌ {wontfix} wontfix ({wontfix_pct}%) — over half of findings swept under rug. "
   161→                f"Strict gap: {strict_gap} pts"
   162→            )
   163→        elif wontfix_pct > 25:
   164→            style = "yellow"
   165→            msg = (
   166→                f"  ⚠ {wontfix} wontfix ({wontfix_pct}%) — review whether past "
   167→                "wontfix decisions still hold"
   168→            )
   169→        elif wontfix_pct > 10:
   170→            style = "yellow"
   171→            msg = (
   172→                f"  ⚠ {wontfix} wontfix findings ({wontfix_pct}%) — strict {strict_gap} "
   173→                "pts below lenient"
   174→            )
   175→        else:
   176→            style = "dim"
   177→            msg = f"  {wontfix} wontfix — strict gap: {strict_gap} pts"
   178→        print(colorize(msg, style))
   179→
   180→        # Show top 2 dimensions with biggest strict gap
   181→        dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {})
   182→        if dim_scores:
   183→            gaps = []
   184→            for name, data in dim_scores.items():
   185→                score = data.get("score", 100)
   186→                strict_value = data.get("strict", score)
   187→                gap = round(score - strict_value, 1)
   188→                if gap > 0:
   189→                    gaps.append((name, gap))
   190→            gaps.sort(key=lambda x: -x[1])
   191→            if gaps:
   192→                top = gaps[:2]
   193→                gap_str = ", ".join(f"{name} (−{gap} pts)" for name, gap in top)
   194→                print(colorize(f"    Biggest gaps: {gap_str}", "dim"))
   195→
   196→    if ignored > 0:
   197→        style = "red" if ignore_patterns > 5 or ignored > 100 else "yellow"
   198→        print(
   199→            colorize(
   200→                f"  ⚠ {ignore_patterns} ignore pattern{'s' if ignore_patterns != 1 else ''} "
   201→                f"suppressed {ignored} finding{'s' if ignored != 1 else ''} this scan",
   202→                style,
   203→            )
   204→        )
   205→        print(
   206→            colorize(
   207→                "    Suppressed findings still count against strict and verified scores",
   208→                "dim",
   209→            )
   210→        )
   211→    elif ignore_patterns > 0:
   212→        print(
   213→            colorize(
   214→                f"  {ignore_patterns} ignore pattern{'s' if ignore_patterns != 1 else ''} "
   215→                "active (0 findings suppressed this scan)",
   216→                "dim",
   217→            )
   218→        )
   219→
   220→    if confidence_reduced:
   221→        impacted_dimensions = score_confidence.get("dimensions", [])
   222→        detectors = score_confidence.get("detectors", [])
   223→        confidence = _coerce_coverage_confidence(
   224→            score_confidence.get("confidence"),
   225→            default=1.0,
   226→        )
   227→        dim_text = ""
   228→        if isinstance(impacted_dimensions, list) and impacted_dimensions:
   229→            preview = ", ".join(str(item) for item in impacted_dimensions[:3])
   230→            if len(impacted_dimensions) > 3:
   231→                preview += f", +{len(impacted_dimensions) - 3} more"
   232→            dim_text = f" (dimensions: {preview})"
   233→        print(

   239→        if isinstance(detectors, list):

   243→                summary = str(detector.get("summary", "")).strip()
   244→                remediation = str(detector.get("remediation", "")).strip()
   245→                if summary:

   247→                if remediation:
```

```
    11→class _DeprecatedAction(argparse.Action):
    12→    """Argparse action that prints a deprecation warning and stores the value."""
    13→
    14→    def __call__(self, parser, namespace, values, option_string=None):
    15→        print(
    16→            f"Warning: {option_string} is deprecated and will be removed in a future version.",
    17→            file=sys.stderr,
    18→        )
    19→        setattr(namespace, self.dest, values)
    20→
    21→
    22→class _DeprecatedBoolAction(argparse.Action):
    23→    """Argparse action for deprecated boolean flags (store_true equivalent)."""
    24→
    25→    def __init__(self, *args, **kwargs):
    26→        kwargs.setdefault("nargs", 0)
    27→        kwargs.setdefault("const", True)
    28→        kwargs.setdefault("default", False)
    29→        super().__init__(*args, **kwargs)
    30→
    31→    def __call__(self, parser, namespace, values, option_string=None):
    32→        print(
    33→            f"Warning: {option_string} is deprecated and will be removed in a future version.",
    34→            file=sys.stderr,
    35→        )
    36→        setattr(namespace, self.dest, True)
    37→
```
