```
    39→def _add_detect_parser(sub, detector_names: list[str]) -> None:
    40→    p_detect = sub.add_parser(
    41→        "detect",
    42→        help="Run a single detector directly (bypass state)",
    43→        epilog=f"detectors: {', '.join(detector_names)}",
    44→    )
    45→    p_detect.add_argument("detector", type=str, help="Detector to run")
    46→    p_detect.add_argument("--top", type=int, default=20, help="Max items to show (default: 20)")
    47→    p_detect.add_argument("--path", type=str, default=None, help="Project root directory (default: auto-detected)")
    48→    p_detect.add_argument("--json", action="store_true", help="Output as JSON")
    49→    p_detect.add_argument(
    50→        "--fix",
    51→        action="store_true",
    52→        help="Auto-fix detected issues (logs detector only)",
    53→    )
    54→    p_detect.add_argument(
    55→        "--category",
    56→        choices=["imports", "vars", "params", "all"],
    57→        default="all",
    58→        help="Filter unused by category",
    59→    )
    60→    p_detect.add_argument(
    61→        "--threshold",
    62→        type=float,
    63→        default=None,
    64→        help="LOC threshold (large) or similarity (dupes)",
    65→    )
    66→    p_detect.add_argument(
    67→        "--file", type=str, default=None, help="Show deps for specific file"

    69→    p_detect.add_argument(

    91→def _add_review_parser(sub) -> None:

    97→examples:
    98→  desloppify review --prepare
    99→  desloppify review --run-batches --runner codex --parallel --scan-after-import
   100→  desloppify review --external-start --external-runner claude

   105→

   107→    g_core = p_review.add_argument_group("core options")
   108→    g_core.add_argument("--path", type=str, default=None, help="Project root directory (default: auto-detected)")
   109→    g_core.add_argument("--state", type=str, default=None, help="Path to state file")
   110→    g_core.add_argument(
   111→        "--prepare",
   112→        action="store_true",
   113→        help="Prepare review data (output to query.json)",
   114→    )
   115→    g_core.add_argument(
   116→        "--import",
   117→        dest="import_file",
   118→        type=str,
   119→        metavar="FILE",
   120→        help="Import review findings from JSON file",
   121→    )
   122→    g_core.add_argument(
   123→        "--validate-import",
   124→        dest="validate_import_file",
   125→        type=str,
   126→        metavar="FILE",
   127→        help="Validate review import payload and selected trust mode without mutating state",
   128→    )
   129→    g_core.add_argument(
   130→        "--allow-partial",
   131→        action="store_true",
   132→        help=(
   133→            "Allow partial review import when invalid findings are skipped "
   134→            "(default: fail on any skipped finding)"
   135→        ),
   136→    )
   137→    g_core.add_argument(
   138→        "--dimensions",
   139→        type=str,
   140→        default=None,
   141→        help="Comma-separated dimensions to evaluate",
   142→    )
   143→    g_core.add_argument(
   144→        "--retrospective",
   145→        action="store_true",
   146→        help=(
   147→            "Include historical review issue status/note context in the packet "
   148→            "to support root-cause vs symptom analysis during review"
   149→        ),
   150→    )
   151→    g_core.add_argument(
   152→        "--retrospective-max-issues",
   153→        type=int,
   154→        default=30,
   155→        help="Max recent historical issues to include in review context (default: 30)",
   156→    )
   157→    g_core.add_argument(
   158→        "--retrospective-max-batch-items",
   159→        type=int,
   160→        default=20,
   161→        help="Max history items included per batch focus slice (default: 20)",
   162→    )
   163→    g_core.add_argument(
   164→        "--force-review-rerun",
   165→        action="store_true",
   166→        help="Bypass the objective-plan-drained gate for review reruns",
   167→    )
   168→
   169→    # -- external review --
   170→    g_external = p_review.add_argument_group("external review")
   171→    g_external.add_argument(
   172→        "--external-start",
   173→        action="store_true",
   174→        help=(
   175→            "Start a cloud external review session (generates blind packet, "
   176→            "session id/token, and reviewer template)"
   177→        ),
   178→    )
   179→    g_external.add_argument(
   180→        "--external-submit",
   181→        action="store_true",
   182→        help=(
   183→            "Submit external reviewer JSON via a started session; "
   184→            "CLI injects canonical provenance before import"
   185→        ),
   186→    )
   187→    g_external.add_argument(
   188→        "--session-id",
   189→        type=str,
   190→        default=None,
   191→        help="External review session id for --external-submit",
   192→    )
   193→    g_external.add_argument(
   194→        "--external-runner",
   195→        choices=["claude"],
   196→        default="claude",
   197→        help="External reviewer runner for --external-start (default: claude)",
   198→    )
   199→    g_external.add_argument(
   200→        "--session-ttl-hours",
   201→        type=int,
   202→        default=24,
   203→        help="External review session expiration in hours (default: 24)",
   204→    )
   205→
   206→    # -- batch execution --
   207→    g_batch = p_review.add_argument_group("batch execution")
   208→    g_batch.add_argument(
   209→        "--run-batches",
   210→        action="store_true",
   211→        help="Run holistic investigation batches with subagents and merge/import output",
   212→    )
   213→    g_batch.add_argument(
   214→        "--runner",
   215→        choices=["codex"],
   216→        default="codex",
   217→        help="Subagent runner backend (default: codex)",
   218→    )
   219→    g_batch.add_argument(
   220→        "--parallel", action="store_true", help="Run selected batches in parallel"
   221→    )
   222→    g_batch.add_argument(
   223→        "--max-parallel-batches",
   224→        type=int,
   225→        default=3,
   226→        help=(
   227→            "Max concurrent subagent batches when --parallel is enabled "
   228→            "(default: 3)"
   229→        ),
   230→    )
   231→    g_batch.add_argument(
   232→        "--batch-timeout-seconds",
   233→        type=int,
   234→        default=20 * 60,
   235→        help="Per-batch runner timeout in seconds (default: 1200)",
   236→    )
   237→    g_batch.add_argument(
   238→        "--batch-max-retries",
   239→        type=int,
   240→        default=1,
   241→        help=(
   242→            "Retries per failed batch for transient runner/network errors "
   243→            "(default: 1)"
   244→        ),
   245→    )
   246→    g_batch.add_argument(
   247→        "--batch-retry-backoff-seconds",
   248→        type=float,
   249→        default=2.0,
   250→        help=(
   251→            "Base backoff delay for transient batch retries in seconds "
   252→            "(default: 2.0)"
   253→        ),
   254→    )
   255→    g_batch.add_argument(
   256→        "--batch-heartbeat-seconds",
   257→        type=float,
   258→        default=15.0,
   259→        help=(
   260→            "Progress heartbeat interval during parallel batch runs in seconds "
   261→            "(default: 15.0)"
   262→        ),
   263→    )
   264→    g_batch.add_argument(
   265→        "--batch-stall-warning-seconds",
   266→        type=int,
   267→        default=0,
   268→        help=(
   269→            "Emit warning when a running batch exceeds this elapsed time "
   270→            "(0 disables warnings; does not terminate the batch)"
   271→        ),
   272→    )
   273→    g_batch.add_argument(
   274→        "--batch-stall-kill-seconds",
   275→        type=int,
   276→        default=120,
   277→        help=(
   278→            "Terminate a batch when output state is unchanged and runner streams are idle "
   279→            "for this many seconds (default: 120; 0 disables kill recovery)"
   280→        ),
   281→    )
   282→    g_batch.add_argument(
   283→        "--dry-run",
   284→        action="store_true",
   285→        help="Generate packet/prompts only (skip runner/import)",
   286→    )
   287→    g_batch.add_argument(
   288→        "--run-log-file",
   289→        type=str,
   290→        default=None,
   291→        help=(
   292→            "Optional explicit path for live run log output "
   293→            "(overrides default run artifacts path)"
   294→        ),
   295→    )
   296→    g_batch.add_argument(
   297→        "--packet",
   298→        type=str,
   299→        default=None,
   300→        help="Use an existing immutable packet JSON instead of preparing a new one",
   301→    )
   302→    g_batch.add_argument(
   303→        "--only-batches",
   304→        type=str,
   305→        default=None,
   306→        help="Comma-separated 1-based batch indexes to run (e.g. 1,3,5)",
   307→    )
   308→    g_batch.add_argument(
   309→        "--scan-after-import",
   310→        action="store_true",
   311→        help="Run `scan` after successful merged import",
   312→    )
   313→    g_batch.add_argument(
   314→        "--import-run",
   315→        dest="import_run_dir",
   316→        type=str,
   317→        metavar="DIR",
   318→        default=None,
   319→        help=(
   320→            "Re-import results from a completed run directory "
   321→            "(replays merge+import when the original pipeline was interrupted)"
   322→        ),
   323→    )
   324→
   325→    # -- trust & attestation --
   326→    g_trust = p_review.add_argument_group("trust & attestation")
   327→    g_trust.add_argument(
   328→        "--manual-override",
   329→        action="store_true",
   330→        help=(
   331→            "Allow untrusted assessment score imports. Findings always import; "
   332→            "scores require trusted blind provenance unless this override is set."
   333→        ),
   334→    )
   335→    g_trust.add_argument(
   336→        "--attested-external",
   337→        action="store_true",
   338→        help=(
   339→            "Accept external blind-run assessments as durable scores when "
   340→            "paired with --attest and valid blind packet provenance "
   341→            "(intended for cloud Claude subagent workflows)."
   342→        ),
   343→    )
   344→    g_trust.add_argument(
   345→        "--attest",
   346→        type=str,
   347→        default=None,
   348→        help=(
   349→            "Required with --manual-override or --attested-external. "
   350→            "For attested external imports include both phrases "
   351→            "'without awareness' and 'unbiased'."
   352→        ),
   353→    )
   354→
   355→    # -- post-processing --
   356→    g_post = p_review.add_argument_group("post-processing")
   357→    g_post.add_argument(
   358→        "--merge",
   359→        action="store_true",
   360→        help="Merge conceptually duplicate open review findings",
   361→    )
   362→    g_post.add_argument(
   363→        "--similarity",
   364→        type=float,
   365→        default=0.8,
   366→        help="Summary similarity threshold for merge (0-1, default: 0.8)",
   367→    )
   368→
   369→    # -- deprecated --
   370→    g_deprecated = p_review.add_argument_group("deprecated")
   371→    g_deprecated.add_argument(
   372→        "--max-age",
   373→        type=int,
   374→        default=None,
   375→        action=_DeprecatedAction,
   376→        help="Deprecated in holistic-only mode (ignored)",
   377→    )
   378→    g_deprecated.add_argument(
   379→        "--max-files",
   380→        type=int,
   381→        default=None,
   382→        action=_DeprecatedAction,
   383→        help="Deprecated in holistic-only mode (ignored)",
   384→    )
   385→    g_deprecated.add_argument(
   386→        "--refresh",
   387→        action=_DeprecatedBoolAction,
   388→        help="Deprecated in holistic-only mode (ignored)",
   389→    )
   390→    g_deprecated.add_argument(
   391→        "--holistic",
   392→        action=_DeprecatedBoolAction,
   393→        help="Deprecated: holistic is now the only review mode",
   394→    )
   395→    g_deprecated.add_argument(
   396→        "--save-run-log",
   397→        action="store_true",
   398→        help=(
   399→            "Deprecated no-op: run logs are now always saved while running "
   400→            "(default location: run artifacts dir)"
   401→        ),
   402→    )

   405→def _add_zone_parser(sub) -> None:
   406→    p_zone = sub.add_parser("zone", help="Show/set/clear zone classifications")
   407→    p_zone.add_argument("--path", type=str, default=None, help="Project root directory (default: auto-detected)")
   408→    p_zone.add_argument("--state", type=str, default=None, help="Path to state file")
   409→    zone_sub = p_zone.add_subparsers(dest="zone_action")
   410→    zone_sub.add_parser("show", help="Show zone classifications for all files")
   411→    z_set = zone_sub.add_parser("set", help="Override zone for a file")
   412→    z_set.add_argument("zone_path", type=str, help="Relative file path")
   413→    z_set.add_argument(
   414→        "zone_value",
   415→        type=str,
   416→        help="Zone (production, test, config, generated, script, vendor)",
   417→    )
   418→    z_clear = zone_sub.add_parser("clear", help="Remove zone override for a file")
   419→    z_clear.add_argument("zone_path", type=str, help="Relative file path")
   420→
   421→
   422→def _add_config_parser(sub) -> None:
   423→    p_config = sub.add_parser("config", help="Show/set/unset project configuration")
   424→    config_sub = p_config.add_subparsers(dest="config_action")
   425→    config_sub.add_parser("show", help="Show all config values")
   426→    c_set = config_sub.add_parser("set", help="Set a config value")
   427→    c_set.add_argument("config_key", type=str, help="Config key name")
   428→    c_set.add_argument("config_value", type=str, help="Value to set")
   429→    c_unset = config_sub.add_parser("unset", help="Reset a config key to default")
   430→    c_unset.add_argument("config_key", type=str, help="Config key name")
   431→
   432→
   433→def _fixer_help_lines(langs: list[str]) -> list[str]:
   434→    fixer_help_lines: list[str] = []
   435→    for lang_name in langs:
   436→        try:
   437→            fixer_names = sorted(get_lang(lang_name).fixers.keys())
   438→        except (ImportError, ValueError, TypeError, AttributeError):
   439→            fixer_names = []
   440→        fixer_list = ", ".join(fixer_names) if fixer_names else "none yet"
   441→        fixer_help_lines.append(f"fixers ({lang_name}): {fixer_list}")
   442→    fixer_help_lines.append("special: review — prepare structured review data")
   443→    return fixer_help_lines
   444→
   445→
   446→def _add_fix_parser(sub, langs: list[str]) -> None:
   447→    p_fix = sub.add_parser(
   448→        "fix",
   449→        help="Auto-fix mechanical issues",
   450→        epilog="\n".join(_fixer_help_lines(langs)),
   451→    )
   452→    p_fix.add_argument("fixer", type=str, help="What to fix")
   453→    p_fix.add_argument("--path", type=str, default=None, help="Project root directory (default: auto-detected)")
   454→    p_fix.add_argument("--state", type=str, default=None, help="Path to state file")
   455→    p_fix.add_argument(
   456→        "--dry-run",
   457→        action="store_true",
   458→        help="Show what would change without modifying files",
   459→    )
   460→
   461→
   462→def _add_plan_parser(sub) -> None:
   463→    p_plan = sub.add_parser(
   464→        "plan",
   465→        help="Living plan: generate, reorder, cluster, skip, note",
   466→        description="""\
   467→Manage the living plan — a persistent layer on top of the work queue.
   468→Track custom ordering, clusters, skips, and per-finding annotations.
   469→Run with no subcommand to generate a full prioritized markdown plan.""",
   470→        epilog="""\
   471→typical workflow:
   472→  desloppify scan                       # detect findings
   473→  desloppify plan                       # full prioritized markdown
   474→  desloppify plan queue                 # compact table of all items
   475→  desloppify plan cluster create ...    # group related findings
   476→  desloppify plan focus <cluster>       # narrow scope
   477→  desloppify next                       # work on the next item
   478→  desloppify plan done <id> --attest .. # mark as fixed
   479→
   480→subcommands:
   481→  show       Show plan metadata summary
   482→  queue      Compact table of upcoming queue items
   483→  reset      Reset plan to empty
   484→  move       Move findings in the queue
   485→  done       Mark findings as fixed (score movement + next-step)
   486→  describe   Set augmented description
   487→  note       Set note on findings
   488→  skip       Skip findings (temporary/permanent/false_positive)
   489→  unskip     Bring skipped findings back to queue
   490→  reopen     Reopen resolved findings
   491→  focus      Set or clear active cluster focus
   492→  cluster    Manage finding clusters""",
   493→        formatter_class=argparse.RawDescriptionHelpFormatter,
   494→    )
   495→    p_plan.add_argument("--state", type=str, default=None, help="Path to state file")
   496→    p_plan.add_argument(
   497→        "--output", type=str, metavar="FILE", help="Write to file instead of stdout"
   498→    )
   499→
   500→    plan_sub = p_plan.add_subparsers(dest="plan_action")
   501→
   502→    # plan show
   503→    plan_sub.add_parser("show", help="Show plan metadata summary")
   504→
   505→    # plan queue
   506→    p_queue = plan_sub.add_parser("queue", help="Compact table of upcoming queue items")
   507→    p_queue.add_argument("--top", type=int, default=30, help="Max items (default: 30, 0=all)")
   508→    p_queue.add_argument("--tier", type=int, choices=[1, 2, 3, 4], default=None,
   509→                         help="Show only items in this tier (T1=critical .. T4=cosmetic)")
   510→    p_queue.add_argument("--cluster", type=str, default=None, metavar="NAME",
   511→                         help="Filter to a specific cluster")
   512→    p_queue.add_argument("--include-skipped", action="store_true",
   513→                         help="Include skipped items at end")
   514→
   515→    # plan reset
   516→    plan_sub.add_parser("reset", help="Reset plan to empty")
   517→
   518→    # plan move <patterns> <position> [target|N]
   519→    p_move = plan_sub.add_parser(
   520→        "move",
   521→        help="Move findings in the queue",
   522→        epilog="""\
   523→examples:
   524→  desloppify plan move security top                    # prioritize a detector
   525→  desloppify plan move "unused::src/foo.ts::*" top     # prioritize by pattern
   526→  desloppify plan move smells bottom                   # deprioritize smells
   527→  desloppify plan move unused before security          # move before another""",
   528→        formatter_class=argparse.RawDescriptionHelpFormatter,
   529→    )
   530→    p_move.add_argument(
   531→        "patterns", nargs="+", metavar="PATTERN",
   532→        help="Finding ID(s), prefix, detector name, file path, or glob",
   533→    )
   534→    p_move.add_argument(
   535→        "position", choices=["top", "bottom", "before", "after", "up", "down"],
   536→        help="Where to move (top, bottom, before, after, up N, down N)",
   537→    )
   538→    p_move.add_argument(
   539→        "target", nargs="?", default=None,
   540→        help="Target finding ID (for before/after) or offset (for up/down)",
   541→    )
   542→
   543→    # plan describe <patterns> "<text>"
   544→    p_describe = plan_sub.add_parser("describe", help="Set augmented description")
   545→    p_describe.add_argument(
   546→        "patterns", nargs="+", metavar="PATTERN",
   547→        help="Finding ID(s), prefix, detector name, file path, or glob",
   548→    )
   549→    p_describe.add_argument("text", type=str, help="Description text")
   550→
   551→    # plan note <patterns> "<text>"
   552→    p_note = plan_sub.add_parser("note", help="Set note on findings")
   553→    p_note.add_argument(
   554→        "patterns", nargs="+", metavar="PATTERN",
   555→        help="Finding ID(s), prefix, detector name, file path, or glob",
   556→    )
   557→    p_note.add_argument("text", type=str, help="Note text")
   558→
   559→    # plan skip <patterns> [--reason] [--review-after N] [--permanent] [--false-positive] [--note] [--attest]
   560→    p_skip = plan_sub.add_parser(
   561→        "skip",
   562→        help="Skip findings: temporary (default), --permanent (wontfix), or --false-positive",
   563→    )
   564→    p_skip.add_argument(
   565→        "patterns", nargs="+", metavar="PATTERN",
   566→        help="Finding ID(s), prefix, detector name, file path, or glob",
   567→    )
   568→    p_skip.add_argument("--reason", type=str, default=None, help="Why this is being skipped")
   569→    p_skip.add_argument(
   570→        "--review-after", type=int, default=None, metavar="N",
   571→        help="Re-surface after N scans (temporary only)",
   572→    )
   573→    p_skip.add_argument(
   574→        "--permanent", action="store_true",
   575→        help="Mark as wontfix (score-affecting, requires --note and --attest)",
   576→    )
   577→    p_skip.add_argument(
   578→        "--false-positive", action="store_true",
   579→        help="Mark as false positive (requires --attest)",
   580→    )
   581→    p_skip.add_argument("--note", type=str, default=None, help="Explanation (required for --permanent)")
   582→    p_skip.add_argument(
   583→        "--attest", type=str, default=None,
   584→        help="Attestation (required for --permanent and --false-positive)",
   585→    )
   586→
   587→    # plan unskip <patterns>
   588→    p_unskip = plan_sub.add_parser(
   589→        "unskip", help="Bring skipped findings back to queue (reopens permanent/fp in state)"
   590→    )
   591→    p_unskip.add_argument(
   592→        "patterns", nargs="+", metavar="PATTERN",
   593→        help="Finding ID(s), prefix, detector name, file path, or glob",
   594→    )
   595→
   596→    # plan reopen <patterns>
   597→    p_reopen = plan_sub.add_parser(
   598→        "reopen", help="Reopen resolved findings and move back to queue"
   599→    )
   600→    p_reopen.add_argument(
   601→        "patterns", nargs="+", metavar="PATTERN",
   602→        help="Finding ID(s), prefix, detector name, file path, or glob",
   603→    )
   604→
   605→    # plan done <patterns> --attest [--note]
   606→    p_done = plan_sub.add_parser(
   607→        "done",
   608→        help="Mark findings as fixed (shows score movement + next step)",
   609→        epilog="""\
   610→examples:
   611→  desloppify plan done "unused::src/foo.tsx::React" \\
   612→    --attest "I have actually removed the import and I am not gaming the score."
   613→  desloppify plan done security --note "patched XSS" \\
   614→    --attest "I have actually ..."  """,
   615→        formatter_class=argparse.RawDescriptionHelpFormatter,
   616→    )
   617→    p_done.add_argument(
   618→        "patterns", nargs="+", metavar="PATTERN",
   619→        help="Finding ID(s), prefix, detector name, file path, or glob",
   620→    )
   621→    p_done.add_argument(
   622→        "--note", type=str, default=None, help="Explanation of the fix"
   623→    )
   624→    p_done.add_argument(
   625→        "--attest",
   626→        type=str,
   627→        default=None,
   628→        help=(
   629→            "Required anti-gaming attestation. Must include BOTH keywords "
   630→            "'I have actually' and 'not gaming'."
   631→        ),
   632→    )
   633→    p_done.add_argument(
   634→        "--confirm",
   635→        action="store_true",
   636→        default=False,
   637→        help="Auto-generate attestation from --note (requires --note)",
   638→    )
   639→
   640→    # plan focus <cluster> | --clear
   641→    p_focus = plan_sub.add_parser("focus", help="Set or clear active cluster focus")
   642→    p_focus.add_argument("cluster_name", nargs="?", default=None, help="Cluster name")
   643→    p_focus.add_argument("--clear", action="store_true", help="Clear focus")
   644→
   645→    # plan cluster ...
   646→    p_cluster = plan_sub.add_parser(
   647→        "cluster",
   648→        help="Manage finding clusters",
   649→        formatter_class=argparse.RawDescriptionHelpFormatter,
   650→    )
   651→    cluster_sub = p_cluster.add_subparsers(dest="cluster_action")
   652→
   653→    # plan cluster create <name> [--description "..."] [--action "..."]
   654→    p_cc = cluster_sub.add_parser("create", help="Create a cluster")
   655→    p_cc.add_argument("cluster_name", type=str, help="Cluster name (slug)")
   656→    p_cc.add_argument("--description", type=str, default=None, help="Cluster description")
   657→    p_cc.add_argument("--action", type=str, default=None, help="Primary action/command for this cluster")
   658→
   659→    # plan cluster add <cluster> <patterns...>
   660→    p_ca = cluster_sub.add_parser("add", help="Add findings to a cluster")
   661→    p_ca.add_argument("cluster_name", type=str, help="Cluster name")
   662→    p_ca.add_argument("patterns", nargs="+", metavar="PATTERN", help="Finding patterns")
   663→
   664→    # plan cluster remove <cluster> <patterns...>
```

```
1→"""CLI parser construction helpers."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import argparse
     6→from importlib.metadata import PackageNotFoundError, version as get_version
     7→
     8→from desloppify.app.cli_support.parser_groups import (
     9→    _add_config_parser,
    10→    _add_detect_parser,
    11→    _add_dev_parser,
    12→    _add_exclude_parser,
    13→    _add_fix_parser,
    14→    _add_ignore_parser,
    15→    _add_langs_parser,
    16→    _add_move_parser,
    17→    _add_next_parser,
    18→    _add_plan_parser,
    19→    _add_review_parser,
    20→    _add_scan_parser,
    21→    _add_show_parser,
    22→    _add_status_parser,
    23→    _add_tree_parser,
    24→    _add_update_skill_parser,
    25→    _add_viz_parser,
    26→    _add_zone_parser,
    27→)
    28→
    29→USAGE_EXAMPLES = """
    30→core workflow:
    31→  scan       Run detectors, update state, show diff
    32→  status     Score dashboard with dimension health
    33→  next       Show next highest-priority item to work on
    34→  plan       Living plan: prioritize, cluster, skip, done, annotate
    35→
    36→investigation:
    37→  show       Dig into findings by file/dir/detector/ID
    38→  tree       Annotated codebase tree (zoom with --focus)
    39→  detect     Run a single detector directly (bypass state)
    40→
    41→maintenance:
    42→  fix        Auto-fix mechanical issues
    43→  ignore     Suppress findings matching a pattern
    44→  exclude    Exclude path pattern from scanning
    45→  move       Move file/dir and update import references
    46→  review     Holistic subjective review (LLM-based)
    47→
    48→setup & admin:
    49→  zone       Show/set zone classifications
    50→  config     Project configuration
    51→  viz        Interactive HTML treemap
    52→  langs      List language plugins
    53→  dev        Developer utilities
    54→  update-skill  Install/update agent skill document
    55→
    56→examples:
    57→  desloppify scan
    58→  desloppify scan --skip-slow --profile ci
    59→  desloppify plan                        # full prioritized markdown
    60→  desloppify plan queue                  # compact table of all items
    61→  desloppify next --count 10             # top 10 queue items
    62→  desloppify show src/components/Modal.tsx
    63→  desloppify plan done "unused::src/foo.tsx::React" \\
    64→    --note "removed import" --attest "I have actually ..."
    65→  desloppify review --run-batches --parallel --scan-after-import
    66→"""
    67→
    68→
    69→class _NoAbbrevArgumentParser(argparse.ArgumentParser):
    70→    """Argparse parser variant that disables long-option abbreviation."""
    71→
    72→    def __init__(self, *args, **kwargs):
    73→        kwargs.setdefault("allow_abbrev", False)
    74→        super().__init__(*args, **kwargs)
    75→
    76→
    77→def _cli_version_string() -> str:
    78→    """Return the best available CLI version label."""
    79→    try:
    80→        return f"desloppify {get_version('desloppify')}"
    81→    except PackageNotFoundError:
    82→        return "desloppify (version unknown)"
    83→
    84→
    85→def create_parser(*, langs: list[str], detector_names: list[str]) -> argparse.ArgumentParser:
    86→    """Build top-level CLI parser with all subcommands."""
    87→    lang_help = ", ".join(langs) if langs else "registered languages"
    88→
    89→    parser = _NoAbbrevArgumentParser(
    90→        prog="desloppify",
    91→        description="Desloppify — codebase health tracker",
    92→        epilog=USAGE_EXAMPLES,
    93→        formatter_class=argparse.RawDescriptionHelpFormatter,
    94→    )
    95→    parser.add_argument(
    96→        "--lang",
    97→        type=str,
    98→        default=None,
    99→        help=f"Language to scan ({lang_help}). Auto-detected if omitted.",
   100→    )
   101→    parser.add_argument(
   102→        "--exclude",
   103→        action="append",
   104→        default=None,
   105→        metavar="PATTERN",
   106→        help="Path pattern to exclude (component/prefix match; repeatable)",
   107→    )
   108→    parser.add_argument(
   109→        "--version",
   110→        "-V",
   111→        action="version",
   112→        version=_cli_version_string(),
   113→    )

   136→    _add_update_skill_parser(sub)
```

```
     2→

    14→    auto_resolve_disappeared,
    15→    find_suspect_detectors,

    18→from desloppify.engine._state.merge_history import (

    24→)
    25→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import (

    32→from desloppify.engine._state.scoring import _recompute_stats
    33→
    34→# Mechanical detectors → subjective dimensions they provide evidence for.
    35→# When findings from these detectors change during a scan, the corresponding
    36→# subjective assessments are marked stale so reviewers know to re-evaluate.
    37→_DETECTOR_SUBJECTIVE_DIMENSIONS: dict[str, tuple[str, ...]] = {
    38→    "structural": ("design_coherence", "abstraction_fitness"),
    39→    "smells": ("design_coherence", "error_consistency"),
    40→    "global_mutable_config": ("initialization_coupling",),
    41→    "coupling": ("cross_module_architecture",),
    42→    "layer_violation": ("cross_module_architecture",),
    43→    "private_imports": ("cross_module_architecture",),
    44→    "dupes": ("convention_outlier",),
    45→    "boilerplate_duplication": ("convention_outlier",),
    46→    "naming": ("convention_outlier",),
    47→    "flat_dirs": ("package_organization",),
    48→    "orphaned": ("design_coherence",),
    49→    "uncalled_functions": ("design_coherence",),
    50→    "responsibility_cohesion": ("design_coherence", "abstraction_fitness"),
    51→    "cycles": ("cross_module_architecture", "dependency_health"),
    52→}
    53→
    54→
    55→def _mark_stale_on_mechanical_change(
    56→    state: StateModel,
    57→    *,
    58→    changed_detectors: set[str],
    59→    now: str,
    60→) -> None:
    61→    """Mark subjective assessments stale when mechanical findings change.
    62→
    63→    Only marks dimensions that already have an assessment — doesn't create
    64→    new entries for dimensions that have never been reviewed.
    65→    """
    66→    assessments = state.get("subjective_assessments")
    67→    if not isinstance(assessments, dict) or not assessments:
    68→        return
    69→
    70→    affected_dims: set[str] = set()
    71→    for detector in changed_detectors:
    72→        dims = _DETECTOR_SUBJECTIVE_DIMENSIONS.get(detector, ())
    73→        affected_dims.update(dims)
    74→
    75→    if not affected_dims:
    76→        return
    77→
    78→    for dimension in sorted(affected_dims):
    79→        if dimension not in assessments:
    80→            continue
    81→        payload = assessments[dimension]
    82→        if not isinstance(payload, dict):
    83→            continue
    84→        # Don't overwrite if already stale
    85→        if payload.get("needs_review_refresh"):
    86→            continue
    87→        payload["needs_review_refresh"] = True
    88→        payload["refresh_reason"] = "mechanical_findings_changed"
    89→        payload["stale_since"] = now
    90→
    91→
    92→@dataclass
    93→class MergeScanOptions:
    94→    """Configuration bundle for merging a scan into persisted state."""
    95→
    96→    lang: str | None = None
    97→    scan_path: str | None = None
    98→    force_resolve: bool = False
    99→    exclude: tuple[str, ...] = ()
   100→    potentials: dict[str, int] | None = None
   101→    merge_potentials: bool = False
   102→    codebase_metrics: dict[str, Any] | None = None
   103→    include_slow: bool = True
   104→    ignore: list[str] | None = None
   105→    subjective_integrity_target: float | None = None
   106→
   107→
   108→def merge_scan(
   109→    state: StateModel,
   110→    current_findings: list[dict],
   111→    options: MergeScanOptions | None = None,
   112→) -> ScanDiff:
   113→    """Merge a fresh scan into existing state and return a diff summary."""
   114→    ensure_state_defaults(state)
   115→    resolved_options = options or MergeScanOptions()
   116→
   117→    now = utc_now()
   118→    _record_scan_metadata(
   119→        state,
   120→        now,
   121→        lang=resolved_options.lang,
   122→        include_slow=resolved_options.include_slow,
   123→        scan_path=resolved_options.scan_path,
   124→    )
   125→    _merge_scan_inputs(
   126→        state,
   127→        lang=resolved_options.lang,
   128→        potentials=resolved_options.potentials,
   129→        merge_potentials=resolved_options.merge_potentials,
   130→        codebase_metrics=resolved_options.codebase_metrics,
   131→    )
   132→
   133→    existing = state["findings"]
   134→    ignore_patterns = (
   135→        resolved_options.ignore
   136→        if resolved_options.ignore is not None
   137→        else state.get("config", {}).get("ignore", [])
   138→    )
   139→    current_ids, new_count, reopened_count, current_by_detector, ignored_count, upsert_changed = (
   140→        upsert_findings(
   141→            existing,
   142→            current_findings,
   143→            ignore_patterns,
   144→            now,
   145→            lang=resolved_options.lang,
   146→        )
   147→    )
   148→
   149→    raw_findings = len(current_findings)
   150→    suppressed_pct = _compute_suppression(raw_findings, ignored_count)
   151→
   152→    ran_detectors = (
   153→        set(resolved_options.potentials.keys())
   154→        if resolved_options.potentials is not None
   155→        else None
   156→    )
   157→    suspect_detectors = find_suspect_detectors(
   158→        existing,
   159→        current_by_detector,
   160→        resolved_options.force_resolve,
   161→        ran_detectors,
   162→    )
   163→    auto_resolved, skipped_other_lang, skipped_out_of_scope, resolve_changed = auto_resolve_disappeared(
   164→        existing,
   165→        current_ids,
   166→        suspect_detectors,
   167→        now,
   168→        lang=resolved_options.lang,
   169→        scan_path=resolved_options.scan_path,
   170→        exclude=resolved_options.exclude,
   171→    )
   172→
   173→    # Mark subjective assessments stale when mechanical findings changed.
   174→    changed_detectors = upsert_changed | resolve_changed
   175→    if changed_detectors:
   176→        _mark_stale_on_mechanical_change(
   177→            state, changed_detectors=changed_detectors, now=now,
   178→        )
   179→
   180→    _recompute_stats(
   181→        state,
   182→        scan_path=resolved_options.scan_path,
   183→        subjective_integrity_target=resolved_options.subjective_integrity_target,
   184→    )
   185→    _append_scan_history(
   186→        state,
   187→        now=now,
   188→        lang=resolved_options.lang,
   189→        new_count=new_count,
   190→        auto_resolved=auto_resolved,
   191→        ignored_count=ignored_count,
   192→        raw_findings=raw_findings,
   193→        suppressed_pct=suppressed_pct,
   194→        ignore_pattern_count=len(ignore_patterns),
   195→    )
   196→
   197→    chronic_reopeners = [
   198→        finding
   199→        for finding in existing.values()
   200→        if finding.get("reopen_count", 0) >= 2 and finding["status"] == "open"
```

```
1→"""Finding upsert/auto-resolve helpers for scan merge."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify.engine._state.filtering import matched_ignore_pattern
     6→from desloppify.core.discovery_api import matches_exclusion
     7→
     8→
     9→def find_suspect_detectors(
    10→    existing: dict,
    11→    current_by_detector: dict[str, int],
    12→    force_resolve: bool,
    13→    ran_detectors: set[str] | None = None,
    14→) -> set[str]:
    15→    """Detectors that had open findings but likely did not actually run this scan."""
    16→    if force_resolve:
    17→        return set()
    18→
    19→    previous_open_by_detector: dict[str, int] = {}
    20→    for finding in existing.values():
    21→        if finding["status"] != "open":
    22→            continue
    23→        detector = finding.get("detector", "unknown")
    24→        previous_open_by_detector[detector] = (
    25→            previous_open_by_detector.get(detector, 0) + 1
    26→        )
    27→
    28→    # 'review' findings enter via `desloppify review --import`, not via scan phases.
    29→    # They are always marked suspect so the scan never auto-resolves them.
    30→    import_only_detectors = {"review"}
    31→    suspect: set[str] = set()
    32→
    33→    for detector, previous_count in previous_open_by_detector.items():
    34→        if detector in import_only_detectors:
    35→            suspect.add(detector)
    36→            continue
    37→        if current_by_detector.get(detector, 0) > 0:
    38→            continue
    39→        if ran_detectors is not None:
    40→            if detector not in ran_detectors:
    41→                suspect.add(detector)
    42→            continue
    43→        if previous_count >= 3:
    44→            suspect.add(detector)
    45→
    46→    return suspect
    47→
    48→
    49→def auto_resolve_disappeared(
    50→    existing: dict,
    51→    current_ids: set[str],
    52→    suspect_detectors: set[str],
    53→    now: str,
    54→    *,
    55→    lang: str | None,
    56→    scan_path: str | None,
    57→    exclude: tuple[str, ...] = (),
    58→) -> tuple[int, int, int, set[str]]:
    59→    """Auto-resolve open/wontfix/fixed/false_positive findings absent from scan.
    60→
    61→    Returns (resolved, skipped_other_lang, skipped_out_of_scope, resolved_detectors).
    62→    """
    63→    resolved = skipped_other_lang = skipped_out_of_scope = 0
    64→    resolved_detectors: set[str] = set()
    65→
    66→    for finding_id, previous in existing.items():
    67→        if finding_id in current_ids or previous["status"] not in (
    68→            "open",
    69→            "wontfix",
    70→            "fixed",
    71→            "false_positive",
    72→        ):
    73→            continue
    74→
    75→        if lang and previous.get("lang") and previous["lang"] != lang:
    76→            skipped_other_lang += 1
    77→            continue
    78→
    79→        if scan_path and scan_path != ".":
    80→            prefix = scan_path.rstrip("/") + "/"
    81→            if (
    82→                not previous["file"].startswith(prefix)
    83→                and previous["file"] != scan_path
    84→            ):
    85→                skipped_out_of_scope += 1
    86→                continue
    87→
    88→        if exclude and any(matches_exclusion(previous["file"], ex) for ex in exclude):
    89→            continue
    90→
    91→        if previous.get("detector", "unknown") in suspect_detectors:
    92→            continue
    93→
    94→        previous_status = previous["status"]
    95→        previous["status"] = "auto_resolved"
    96→        previous["resolved_at"] = now
    97→        previous["suppressed"] = False
    98→        previous["suppressed_at"] = None
    99→        previous["suppression_pattern"] = None
   100→        previous["resolution_attestation"] = {
   101→            "kind": "scan_verified",
   102→            "text": "Disappeared from detector output",
   103→            "attested_at": now,
   104→            "scan_verified": True,
   105→        }
   106→        previous["note"] = (
   107→            "Fixed despite wontfix — disappeared from scan (was wontfix)"
   108→            if previous_status == "wontfix"
   109→            else "Disappeared from scan — likely fixed"
   110→        )
   111→        resolved_detectors.add(previous.get("detector", "unknown"))
   112→        resolved += 1
   113→
   114→    return resolved, skipped_other_lang, skipped_out_of_scope, resolved_detectors
   115→
   116→
   117→def upsert_findings(
   118→    existing: dict,
   119→    current_findings: list[dict],
   120→    ignore: list[str],
   121→    now: str,
   122→    *,
   123→    lang: str | None,
   124→) -> tuple[set[str], int, int, dict[str, int], int, set[str]]:
   125→    """Insert new findings and update existing ones.
   126→
   127→    Returns (current_ids, new_count, reopened_count, by_detector, ignored_count, changed_detectors).
   128→    """
   129→    current_ids: set[str] = set()
   130→    new_count = reopened_count = ignored_count = 0
   131→    by_detector: dict[str, int] = {}
   132→    changed_detectors: set[str] = set()
   133→
   134→    for finding in current_findings:
   135→        finding_id = finding["id"]
   136→        detector = finding.get("detector", "unknown")
   137→        current_ids.add(finding_id)
   138→        by_detector[detector] = by_detector.get(detector, 0) + 1
   139→        matched_ignore = matched_ignore_pattern(finding_id, finding["file"], ignore)
   140→        if matched_ignore:
   141→            ignored_count += 1
   142→
   143→        if lang:
   144→            finding["lang"] = lang
   145→
   146→        if finding_id not in existing:
   147→            existing[finding_id] = dict(finding)
   148→            if matched_ignore:
   149→                existing[finding_id]["suppressed"] = True
   150→                existing[finding_id]["suppressed_at"] = now
   151→                existing[finding_id]["suppression_pattern"] = matched_ignore
   152→                continue
   153→            new_count += 1
   154→            changed_detectors.add(detector)
   155→            continue
   156→
   157→        previous = existing[finding_id]
   158→        previous.update(
   159→            last_seen=now,
   160→            tier=finding["tier"],
   161→            confidence=finding["confidence"],
   162→            summary=finding["summary"],
   163→            detail=finding.get("detail", {}),
   164→        )
   165→        if "zone" in finding:
   166→            previous["zone"] = finding["zone"]
   167→        if lang and not previous.get("lang"):
   168→            previous["lang"] = lang
   169→
   170→        if matched_ignore:
   171→            previous["suppressed"] = True
   172→            previous["suppressed_at"] = now
   173→            previous["suppression_pattern"] = matched_ignore
   174→            continue
   175→
   176→        previous["suppressed"] = False
   177→        previous["suppressed_at"] = None
   178→        previous["suppression_pattern"] = None
   179→
   180→        if previous["status"] in ("fixed", "auto_resolved"):
   181→            # subjective_review findings are condition-based.  When just
   182→            # auto-resolved by an agent import, skip reopening to avoid a
   183→            # resolve-then-reopen loop on the same scan cycle.
   184→            if (
   185→                detector == "subjective_review"
   186→                and previous["status"] == "auto_resolved"
   187→                and (previous.get("resolution_attestation") or {}).get("kind") == "agent_import"
   188→            ):
   189→                continue
   190→            previous_status = previous["status"]
   191→            previous["reopen_count"] = previous.get("reopen_count", 0) + 1
   192→            previous.pop("resolution_attestation", None)
   193→            previous.update(
   194→                status="open",
   195→                resolved_at=None,
   196→                note=(
   197→                    f"Reopened (×{previous['reopen_count']}) "
   198→                    f"— reappeared in scan (was {previous_status})"
   199→                ),
   200→            )
   201→            reopened_count += 1
   202→            changed_detectors.add(detector)
   203→
   204→    return current_ids, new_count, reopened_count, by_detector, ignored_count, changed_detectors
   205→
   206→
   207→__all__ = [
   208→    "auto_resolve_disappeared",
   209→    "find_suspect_detectors",
   210→    "upsert_findings",
   211→]
   212→
```

```
1→"""Scan metadata/history helpers for merge operations."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import ScanDiff, StateModel
     6→from desloppify.core.tooling import compute_tool_hash
     7→
     8→
     9→def _record_scan_metadata(
    10→    state: StateModel,
    11→    now: str,
    12→    *,
    13→    lang: str | None,
    14→    include_slow: bool,
    15→    scan_path: str | None,
    16→) -> None:
    17→    state["last_scan"] = now
    18→    state["scan_count"] = state.get("scan_count", 0) + 1
    19→    state["tool_hash"] = compute_tool_hash()
    20→    state["scan_path"] = scan_path
    21→    if lang:
    22→        state.setdefault("scan_completeness", {})[lang] = (
    23→            "full" if include_slow else "fast"
    24→        )
    25→
    26→
    27→def _merge_scan_inputs(
    28→    state: StateModel,
    29→    *,
    30→    lang: str | None,
    31→    potentials: dict[str, int] | None,
    32→    merge_potentials: bool,
    33→    codebase_metrics: dict | None,
    34→) -> None:
    35→    if potentials is not None and lang:
    36→        all_potentials = state.setdefault("potentials", {})
    37→        if merge_potentials and isinstance(all_potentials.get(lang), dict):
    38→            merged = dict(all_potentials[lang])
    39→            merged.update(potentials)
    40→            all_potentials[lang] = merged
    41→        else:
    42→            all_potentials[lang] = dict(potentials)
    43→
    44→    if codebase_metrics is not None and lang:
    45→        state.setdefault("codebase_metrics", {})[lang] = dict(codebase_metrics)
    46→
    47→
    48→def _compute_suppression(raw_findings: int, ignored_count: int) -> float:
    49→    return round(ignored_count / raw_findings * 100, 1) if raw_findings else 0.0
    50→
    51→
    52→def _subjective_integrity_snapshot(integrity: dict | None) -> dict[str, object] | None:
    53→    if not isinstance(integrity, dict):
    54→        return None
    55→    return {
    56→        "status": integrity.get("status"),
    57→        "matched_count": int(integrity.get("matched_count", 0) or 0),
    58→        "reset_count": len(
    59→            [
    60→                key
    61→                for key in integrity.get("reset_dimensions", [])
    62→                if isinstance(key, str) and key
    63→            ]
    64→        ),
    65→        "target_score": integrity.get("target_score"),
    66→    }
    67→
    68→
    69→def _score_confidence_snapshot(confidence: dict | None) -> dict[str, object] | None:
    70→    if not isinstance(confidence, dict):
    71→        return None
    72→    status = confidence.get("status")
    73→    if status is None:
    74→        return None
    75→    detectors = confidence.get("detectors", [])
    76→    detector_count = len(detectors) if isinstance(detectors, list) else 0
    77→    dimensions = confidence.get("dimensions", [])
    78→    dimension_count = len(dimensions) if isinstance(dimensions, list) else 0
    79→    return {
    80→        "status": status,
    81→        "confidence": confidence.get("confidence"),
    82→        "detector_count": detector_count,
    83→        "dimension_count": dimension_count,
    84→    }
    85→
    86→
    87→def _append_scan_history(
    88→    state: StateModel,
    89→    *,
    90→    now: str,
    91→    lang: str | None,
    92→    new_count: int,
    93→    auto_resolved: int,
    94→    ignored_count: int,
    95→    raw_findings: int,
    96→    suppressed_pct: float,
    97→    ignore_pattern_count: int,
    98→) -> None:
    99→    history = state.setdefault("scan_history", [])
   100→    history.append(
   101→        {
   102→            "timestamp": now,
   103→            "lang": lang,
   104→            "strict_score": state.get("strict_score"),
   105→            "verified_strict_score": state.get("verified_strict_score"),
   106→            "objective_score": state.get("objective_score"),
   107→            "overall_score": state.get("overall_score"),
   108→            "open": state["stats"]["open"],
   109→            "diff_new": new_count,
   110→            "diff_resolved": auto_resolved,
   111→            "ignored": ignored_count,
   112→            "raw_findings": raw_findings,
   113→            "suppressed_pct": suppressed_pct,
   114→            "ignore_patterns": ignore_pattern_count,
   115→            "subjective_integrity": _subjective_integrity_snapshot(
   116→                state.get("subjective_integrity")
   117→            ),
   118→            "score_confidence": _score_confidence_snapshot(
   119→                state.get("score_confidence")
   120→            ),
   121→            "dimension_scores": {
   122→                name: {"score": ds["score"], "strict": ds.get("strict", ds["score"])}
   123→                for name, ds in state.get("dimension_scores", {}).items()
   124→            }
   125→            if state.get("dimension_scores")
   126→            else None,
   127→        }
   128→    )
   129→
   130→    if len(history) > 20:
   131→        state["scan_history"] = history[-20:]
   132→
   133→
   134→def _build_merge_diff(
   135→    *,
   136→    new_count: int,
   137→    auto_resolved: int,
   138→    reopened_count: int,
   139→    current_ids: set[str],
   140→    suspect_detectors: set[str],
   141→    chronic_reopeners: list[dict],
   142→    skipped_other_lang: int,
   143→    skipped_out_of_scope: int,
   144→    ignored_count: int,
   145→    ignore_pattern_count: int,
   146→    raw_findings: int,
   147→    suppressed_pct: float,
   148→) -> ScanDiff:
   149→    return {
   150→        "new": new_count,
   151→        "auto_resolved": auto_resolved,
   152→        "reopened": reopened_count,
   153→        "total_current": len(current_ids),
   154→        "suspect_detectors": sorted(suspect_detectors) if suspect_detectors else [],
   155→        "chronic_reopeners": chronic_reopeners,
   156→        "skipped_other_lang": skipped_other_lang,
   157→        "skipped_out_of_scope": skipped_out_of_scope,
   158→        "ignored": ignored_count,
   159→        "ignore_patterns": ignore_pattern_count,
   160→        "raw_findings": raw_findings,
   161→        "suppressed_pct": suppressed_pct,
   162→    }
   163→
   164→
   165→__all__ = [
   166→    "_append_scan_history",
   167→    "_build_merge_diff",
   168→    "_compute_suppression",
   169→    "_merge_scan_inputs",
   170→    "_record_scan_metadata",
   171→    "_subjective_integrity_snapshot",
   172→]
   173→
```

```
1→"""Plan-aware queue progress and frozen score display helpers."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify.core.exception_sets import PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS
     6→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize
     7→
     8→
     9→def plan_aware_queue_count(state: dict, plan: dict | None = None) -> int:
    10→    """Count remaining plan-aware queue items (skips excluded, clusters collapsed)."""
    11→    from desloppify.engine.work_queue import QueueBuildOptions, build_work_queue
    12→
    13→    result = build_work_queue(
    14→        state,
    15→        options=QueueBuildOptions(
    16→            status="open",
    17→            count=None,
    18→            plan=plan,
    19→            collapse_clusters=True,
    20→            include_skipped=False,
    21→        ),
    22→    )
    23→    return result["total"]
    24→
    25→
    26→def get_plan_start_strict(plan: dict | None) -> float | None:
    27→    """Extract the frozen plan-start strict score, or None if unset."""
    28→    if not plan:
    29→        return None
    30→    return plan.get("plan_start_scores", {}).get("strict")
    31→
    32→
    33→def print_frozen_score_with_queue_context(
    34→    plan: dict,
    35→    queue_remaining: int,
    36→) -> None:
    37→    """Show frozen plan-start score + queue progress."""
    38→    scores = plan.get("plan_start_scores", {})
    39→    strict = scores.get("strict")
    40→    if strict is None:
    41→        return
    42→    print(
    43→        colorize(
    44→            f"\n  Score (frozen at plan start): strict {strict:.1f}/100",
    45→            "cyan",
    46→        )
    47→    )
    48→    print(
    49→        colorize(
    50→            f"  Queue: {queue_remaining} item{'s' if queue_remaining != 1 else ''}"
    51→            " remaining. Score will not update until the queue is clear and you run `desloppify scan`.",
    52→            "dim",
    53→        )
    54→    )
    55→
    56→
    57→def print_execution_or_reveal(
    58→    state: dict,
    59→    prev,
    60→    plan: dict | None,
    61→) -> None:
    62→    """Context-aware score display: frozen plan-start score or live scores.
    63→
    64→    If a plan has ``plan_start_scores`` and there are queue items remaining,
    65→    show the frozen score + queue progress.  Otherwise delegate to the
    66→    existing live ``print_score_update``.
    67→    """
    68→    if plan and plan.get("plan_start_scores", {}).get("strict") is not None:
    69→        try:
    70→            remaining = plan_aware_queue_count(state, plan)
    71→        except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS:
    72→            remaining = 0
    73→        if remaining > 0:
    74→            print_frozen_score_with_queue_context(plan, remaining)
    75→            return
    76→
    77→    # No active plan cycle or queue is clear — live scores
    78→    from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.score_update import print_score_update
    79→
    80→    print_score_update(state, prev)
    81→
    82→
    83→def show_score_with_plan_context(state: dict, prev) -> None:
    84→    """Load plan (best-effort) and show frozen or live score context.
    85→
    86→    Encapsulates the common load_plan + PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS + reveal
    87→    choreography so command modules don't each repeat it.
    88→    """
    89→    from desloppify.engine.plan import load_plan
    90→
    91→    try:
    92→        plan = load_plan()
    93→    except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS:
    94→        plan = None
    95→    print_execution_or_reveal(state, prev, plan)
    96→
    97→
    98→__all__ = [
    99→    "get_plan_start_strict",
   100→    "plan_aware_queue_count",
   101→    "print_execution_or_reveal",
   102→    "print_frozen_score_with_queue_context",
   103→    "show_score_with_plan_context",
   104→]
   105→
```

```
    22→

    24→def _cmd_plan_generate(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:

    29→def _cmd_plan_show(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    30→    """Show plan metadata summary."""
    31→    plan = load_plan()
    32→    ordered = len(plan.get("queue_order", []))
    33→    skipped = plan.get("skipped", {})
    34→    total_skipped = len(skipped)
    35→    temp_count = sum(1 for e in skipped.values() if e.get("kind") == "temporary")
    36→    perm_count = sum(1 for e in skipped.values() if e.get("kind") == "permanent")
    37→    fp_count = sum(1 for e in skipped.values() if e.get("kind") == "false_positive")
    38→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
    39→    overrides = plan.get("overrides", {})
    40→    active = plan.get("active_cluster")
    41→    superseded = len(plan.get("superseded", {}))
    42→
    43→    # Count meaningful annotations (descriptions and notes)
    44→    described = sum(1 for o in overrides.values() if o.get("description"))
    45→    noted = sum(1 for o in overrides.values() if o.get("note"))
    46→
    47→    print(colorize("  Living Plan Status", "bold"))
    48→    print(colorize("  " + "─" * 40, "dim"))
    49→    print(f"  Queue:            {ordered} items prioritized")
    50→    if total_skipped:
    51→        print(f"  Skipped:          {total_skipped} (temp: {temp_count}, wontfix: {perm_count}, fp: {fp_count})")
    52→    else:
    53→        print(f"  Skipped:          0")
    54→    print(f"  Clusters:         {len(clusters)}")
    55→    if clusters:
    56→        for name, cluster in clusters.items():
    57→            desc = cluster.get("description") or ""
    58→            member_count = len(cluster.get("finding_ids", []))
    59→            marker = " (focused)" if name == active else ""
    60→            desc_str = f" — {desc}" if desc else ""
    61→            print(f"    {name}: {member_count} items{desc_str}{marker}")
    62→    if described or noted:
    63→        print(f"  Annotations:      {described} described, {noted} noted")
    64→    if active:
    65→        print(f"  Focus:            {active}")
    66→    if superseded:
    67→        print(f"  Disappeared:      {superseded} (resolved or removed since last scan)")
    68→
    69→
    70→def _cmd_plan_reset(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    71→    """Reset the plan to empty."""
    72→    plan = load_plan()
    73→    queue_len = len(plan.get("queue_order", []))
    74→    cluster_count = len(plan.get("clusters", {}))
    75→    reset_plan(plan)
    76→    append_log_entry(
    77→        plan, "reset", actor="user",
    78→        detail={"previous_queue_size": queue_len, "previous_cluster_count": cluster_count},
    79→    )
    80→    save_plan(plan)
    81→    print(colorize("  Plan reset to empty.", "green"))
    82→
    83→
    84→def cmd_plan(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    85→    """Dispatch plan subcommand or generate markdown output."""
    86→    plan_action = getattr(args, "plan_action", None)
    87→
    88→    if plan_action is None:
    89→        _cmd_plan_generate(args)
    90→        return
    91→
    92→    if plan_action == "show":
    93→        _cmd_plan_show(args)
    94→        return
    95→
    96→    if plan_action == "queue":
    97→        cmd_plan_queue(args)
    98→        return
    99→
   100→    if plan_action == "reset":
   101→        _cmd_plan_reset(args)
   102→        return
   103→
   104→    if plan_action == "move":
   105→        cmd_plan_move(args)
   106→        return
   107→
   108→    if plan_action in (
   109→        "describe", "note", "focus",
   110→        "skip", "unskip", "reopen", "done",
   111→    ):
   112→        dispatch = {
   113→            "describe": cmd_plan_describe,
   114→            "done": cmd_plan_done,
   115→            "note": cmd_plan_note,
   116→            "focus": cmd_plan_focus,
   117→            "skip": cmd_plan_skip,
   118→            "unskip": cmd_plan_unskip,
   119→            "reopen": cmd_plan_reopen,
   120→        }
   121→        dispatch[plan_action](args)
   122→        return
   123→
   124→    if plan_action == "cluster":
   125→        cmd_cluster_dispatch(args)
   126→        return
   127→
   128→    if plan_action == "synthesize":
   129→        from desloppify.app.commands.plan.synthesize_handlers import cmd_plan_synthesize
   130→
   131→        cmd_plan_synthesize(args)
   132→        return
   133→
   134→    print(f"Unknown plan action: {plan_action}")
   135→
   136→
   137→# Backwards-compatible alias
   138→cmd_plan_output = cmd_plan
   139→
   140→__all__ = ["cmd_plan", "cmd_plan_output"]
   141→
```

```
1→"""Compact queue table renderer for ``plan queue``."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import argparse
     6→
     7→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.runtime import command_runtime
     8→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.state import require_completed_scan
     9→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize, print_table
    10→from desloppify.engine.plan import load_plan
    11→from desloppify.engine.work_queue import QueueBuildOptions, build_work_queue
    12→
    13→
    14→def _truncate(text: str, width: int) -> str:
    15→    if len(text) <= width:
    16→        return text
    17→    return text[: width - 1] + "\u2026"
    18→
    19→
    20→def _cluster_tier_label(item: dict) -> str:
    21→    """Compute a tier label from cluster members, e.g. 'T2' or 'T1-T3'."""
    22→    members = item.get("members", [])
    23→    if not members:
    24→        return ""
    25→    tiers = {int(m.get("effective_tier", m.get("tier", 3))) for m in members}
    26→    lo, hi = min(tiers), max(tiers)
    27→    if lo == hi:
    28→        return f"T{lo}"
    29→    return f"T{lo}-T{hi}"
    30→
    31→
    32→def _resolve_plan_context(plan: dict, cluster_filter: str | None) -> tuple[dict | None, str | None]:
    33→    plan_data: dict | None = None
    34→    if plan.get("queue_order") or plan.get("overrides") or plan.get("clusters"):
    35→        plan_data = plan
    36→
    37→    effective_cluster = cluster_filter
    38→    if plan_data and not cluster_filter:
    39→        active_cluster = plan_data.get("active_cluster")
    40→        if active_cluster:
    41→            effective_cluster = active_cluster
    42→    return plan_data, effective_cluster
    43→
    44→
    45→def _print_queue_header(
    46→    *,
    47→    items: list[dict],
    48→    tier_counts: dict,
    49→    include_skipped: bool,
    50→    plan: dict,
    51→    plan_data: dict | None,
    52→) -> None:
    53→    total = len(items)
    54→    skipped_count = sum(1 for it in items if it.get("plan_skipped"))
    55→    non_skipped = total - skipped_count
    56→    tc_parts = " ".join(f"T{t}:{tier_counts.get(t, 0)}" for t in (1, 2, 3, 4))
    57→    finding_total = sum(tier_counts.get(t, 0) for t in (1, 2, 3, 4))
    58→    if finding_total != non_skipped:
    59→        print(colorize(f"\n  Queue: {non_skipped} entries  ({finding_total} findings: {tc_parts})", "bold"))
    60→    else:
    61→        print(colorize(f"\n  Queue: {non_skipped} items  ({tc_parts})", "bold"))
    62→
    63→    focus = plan.get("active_cluster") if plan_data else None
    64→    if focus:
    65→        print(colorize(f"  Focus: {focus}", "cyan"))
    66→
    67→    if include_skipped or skipped_count != 0:
    68→        return
    69→    plan_skipped_count = len(plan.get("skipped", {})) if plan_data else 0
    70→    if not plan_skipped_count:
    71→        return
    72→    print(
    73→        colorize(
    74→            f"  ({plan_skipped_count} skipped item{'s' if plan_skipped_count != 1 else ''}"
    75→            " hidden — use --include-skipped)",
    76→            "dim",
    77→        )
    78→    )
    79→
    80→
    81→def _queue_display_items(items: list[dict], *, top: int) -> list[dict]:
    82→    if top > 0 and len(items) > top:
    83→        return items[:top]
    84→    return items
    85→
    86→
    87→def _build_rows(display_items: list[dict]) -> list[list[str]]:
    88→    rows: list[list[str]] = []
    89→    for idx, item in enumerate(display_items, 1):
    90→        pos = str(idx)
    91→        kind = item.get("kind", "finding")
    92→
    93→        if kind == "cluster":
    94→            tier_str = _cluster_tier_label(item)
    95→            member_count = item.get("member_count", 0)
    96→            detector = item.get("detector", "cluster")
    97→            summary = f"[{member_count} items] {item.get('summary', '')}"
    98→            cluster_name = item.get("cluster_name", item.get("id", ""))
    99→        else:
   100→            tier_val = int(item.get("effective_tier", item.get("tier", 3)))
   101→            tier_str = f"T{tier_val}"
   102→            detector = item.get("detector", "")
   103→            summary = item.get("summary", "")
   104→            plan_cluster = item.get("plan_cluster")
   105→            cluster_name = plan_cluster.get("name", "") if isinstance(plan_cluster, dict) else ""
   106→
   107→        suffix = " [skip]" if item.get("plan_skipped") else ""
   108→        summary_display = _truncate(summary, 48) + suffix
   109→        rows.append([pos, tier_str, detector, summary_display, cluster_name])
   110→    return rows
   111→
   112→
   113→def cmd_plan_queue(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   114→    """Render a compact table of all upcoming queue items."""
   115→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
   116→    state = runtime.state
   117→    if not require_completed_scan(state):
   118→        return
   119→
   120→    top = getattr(args, "top", 30)
   121→    tier_filter = getattr(args, "tier", None)
   122→    cluster_filter = getattr(args, "cluster", None)
   123→    include_skipped = bool(getattr(args, "include_skipped", False))
   124→
   125→    plan = load_plan()
   126→    plan_data, effective_cluster = _resolve_plan_context(plan, cluster_filter)
   127→
   128→    queue = build_work_queue(
   129→        state,
   130→        options=QueueBuildOptions(
   131→            tier=tier_filter,
   132→            count=None,
   133→            scan_path=state.get("scan_path"),
   134→            status="open",
   135→            include_subjective=True,
   136→            plan=plan_data,
   137→            include_skipped=include_skipped,
   138→            cluster=effective_cluster,
   139→            collapse_clusters=True,
   140→        ),
   141→    )
   142→    items = queue.get("items", [])
   143→    tier_counts = queue.get("tier_counts", {})
   144→
   145→    _print_queue_header(
   146→        items=items,
   147→        tier_counts=tier_counts,
   148→        include_skipped=include_skipped,
   149→        plan=plan,
   150→        plan_data=plan_data,
   151→    )
   152→
   153→    if not items:
   154→        print(colorize("\n  Queue is empty.", "green"))
   155→        return
   156→
   157→    # Determine which items to show
   158→    display_items = _queue_display_items(items, top=top)
   159→    headers = ["#", "Tier", "Detector", "Summary", "Cluster"]
   160→    rows = _build_rows(display_items)
   161→
   162→    print()
   163→    widths = [4, 4, 12, 50, 16]
   164→    print_table(headers, rows, widths=widths)
   165→
   166→    if top > 0 and len(items) > top:
   167→        remaining = len(items) - top
   168→        print(colorize(
   169→            f"\n  ... and {remaining} more (use --top 0 to show all)", "dim"
   170→        ))
   171→    print()
   172→
   173→
   174→__all__ = ["cmd_plan_queue"]
   175→
```

```
   186→def utc_now() -> str:
   187→    """Return current UTC timestamp with second-level precision."""
   188→    return datetime.now(UTC).isoformat(timespec="seconds")
   189→
   190→
   191→def empty_state() -> StateModel:
   192→    """Return a new empty state payload."""

   194→        "version": CURRENT_VERSION,
   195→        "created": utc_now(),
   196→        "last_scan": None,
   197→        "scan_count": 0,

   207→        "subjective_assessments": {},
   208→    }

   210→
   211→def _as_non_negative_int(value: Any, default: int = 0) -> int:
   212→    try:
   213→        parsed = int(value)
   214→    except (TypeError, ValueError):
   215→        return default
   216→    return parsed if parsed >= 0 else 0
   217→
   218→
   219→def ensure_state_defaults(state: StateModel | dict) -> None:
   220→    """Normalize loose/legacy state payloads to a valid base shape in-place."""
   221→    for key, value in empty_state().items():
   222→        state.setdefault(key, value)
   223→
   224→    if not isinstance(state.get("findings"), dict):
   225→        state["findings"] = {}
   226→    if not isinstance(state.get("stats"), dict):
   227→        state["stats"] = {}
   228→    if not isinstance(state.get("scan_history"), list):
   229→        state["scan_history"] = []
   230→    if not isinstance(state.get("scan_coverage"), dict):
   231→        state["scan_coverage"] = {}
   232→    if not isinstance(state.get("score_confidence"), dict):
   233→        state["score_confidence"] = {}
   234→    if not isinstance(state.get("subjective_integrity"), dict):
   235→        state["subjective_integrity"] = {}
   236→
   237→    findings = state["findings"]
   238→    to_remove: list[str] = []
   239→    for finding_id, finding in findings.items():
   240→        if not isinstance(finding, dict):
   241→            to_remove.append(finding_id)
   242→            continue
   243→
   244→        finding.setdefault("id", finding_id)
   245→        finding.setdefault("detector", "unknown")
   246→        finding.setdefault("file", "")
   247→        finding.setdefault("tier", 3)
   248→        finding.setdefault("confidence", "low")
   249→        finding.setdefault("summary", "")
```

```
    27→
    28→    runtime: ScanRuntime

    46→    def generate(
    47→        self,
    48→    ) -> tuple[list[dict[str, object]], dict[str, object], dict[str, object] | None]:
    49→        """Run detector generation and lifecycle augmenters."""
    50→        return self.run_scan_generation_fn(self.runtime)
    51→
    52→    def merge(
    53→        self,
    54→        findings: list[dict[str, object]],
    55→        potentials: dict[str, object],
    56→        codebase_metrics: dict[str, object] | None,
    57→    ) -> ScanMergeResult:

    60→            self.runtime,
    61→            findings,
    62→            potentials,
    63→            codebase_metrics,

    66→    def noise_snapshot(self) -> ScanNoiseSnapshot:
```

```
     2→

    13→from desloppify.app.output.tree_text import render_tree_lines
    14→from desloppify.core.file_paths import resolve_scan_file
    15→from desloppify.core.fallbacks import (

    17→    print_write_error,
    18→    warn_best_effort,
    19→)
    20→from desloppify.core.output_contract import OutputResult
    21→from desloppify.core.discovery_api import rel, safe_write_text
    22→from desloppify.state import get_objective_score, get_overall_score, get_strict_score

    25→D3_CDN_URL = "https://d3js.org/d3.v7.min.js"
    26→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    27→_RECOVERABLE_LANG_RESOLUTION_ERRORS = (
    28→    ImportError,
    29→    ValueError,
    30→    TypeError,
    31→    AttributeError,
    32→    OSError,
    33→    RuntimeError,
    34→)
    35→

    37→__all__ = ["D3_CDN_URL", "cmd_viz", "cmd_tree"]

    40→def _resolve_visualization_lang(path: Path, lang):
    41→    """Resolve language config for visualization if not already provided."""
    42→    if lang:
    43→        return lang
    44→    from desloppify.languages import auto_detect_lang, get_lang
    45→
    46→    search_roots = [path if path.is_dir() else path.parent]
    47→    search_roots.extend(search_roots[0].parents)
    48→    warned = False
    49→    for root in search_roots:
    50→        try:
    51→            detected = auto_detect_lang(root)
    52→        except _RECOVERABLE_LANG_RESOLUTION_ERRORS as exc:
    53→            log_best_effort_failure(
    54→                logger,
    55→                f"auto-detect visualization language for {root}",
    56→                exc,
    57→            )
    58→            if not warned:
    59→                warned = True
    60→                warn_best_effort(
    61→                    "Could not auto-detect language plugins for visualization; "
    62→                    f"using fallback source discovery ({type(exc).__name__}: {exc})."
    63→                )
    64→            continue
    65→        if detected:
    66→            try:
    67→                return get_lang(detected)
    68→            except _RECOVERABLE_LANG_RESOLUTION_ERRORS as exc:
    69→                log_best_effort_failure(
    70→                    logger,
    71→                    f"load visualization language plugin '{detected}'",
    72→                    exc,
    73→                )
    74→                if not warned:
    75→                    warned = True
    76→                    warn_best_effort(
    77→                        "Visualization language plugin failed to load; using fallback source discovery "
    78→                        f"({type(exc).__name__}: {exc})."
    79→                    )
    80→                continue
    81→    return None
    82→
    83→
    84→def _fallback_source_files(path: Path) -> list[str]:
    85→    """Collect source files using extensions from all registered language plugins."""
    86→    from desloppify.core.discovery_api import find_source_files
    87→    from desloppify.languages import available_langs, get_lang
    88→
    89→    extensions: set[str] = set()
    90→    warned = False
    91→    for lang_name in available_langs():
    92→        try:
    93→            cfg = get_lang(lang_name)
    94→        except _RECOVERABLE_LANG_RESOLUTION_ERRORS as exc:
    95→            log_best_effort_failure(
    96→                logger,
    97→                f"load fallback visualization language plugin '{lang_name}'",
    98→                exc,
    99→            )

   102→                warn_best_effort(
   103→                    "Some language plugins could not be loaded for visualization fallback; using available plugins only "
   104→                    f"({type(exc).__name__}: {exc})."
   105→                )
   106→            continue
   107→        extensions.update(cfg.extensions)
   108→    if not extensions:
   109→        return []
   110→    return find_source_files(path, sorted(extensions))
   111→
   112→
   113→def _collect_file_data(path: Path, lang=None) -> list[dict]:
   114→    """Collect LOC for all source files using the language's file finder."""
   115→    resolved_lang = _resolve_visualization_lang(path, lang)
   116→    if resolved_lang and resolved_lang.file_finder:
   117→        source_files = resolved_lang.file_finder(path)
   118→    else:
   119→        source_files = _fallback_source_files(path)
   120→    files = []
   121→    warned_read_failure = False
   122→    for filepath in source_files:
   123→        try:
   124→            p = resolve_scan_file(filepath, scan_root=path)
   125→            content = p.read_text()
   126→            loc = len(content.splitlines())
   127→            files.append(
   128→                {
   129→                    "path": rel(filepath),
   130→                    "abs_path": str(p.resolve()),
   131→                    "loc": loc,
   132→                }
   133→            )
   134→        except (OSError, UnicodeDecodeError) as exc:
   135→            log_best_effort_failure(
   136→                logger, f"read visualization source file {filepath}", exc
   137→            )
   138→            if not warned_read_failure:
   139→                warned_read_failure = True
   140→                warn_best_effort(
   141→                    "Some visualization source files could not be read; output may be incomplete."
   142→                )
   143→            continue
   144→    return files
   145→
   146→
   147→def _build_tree(files: list[dict], dep_graph: dict, findings_by_file: dict) -> dict:
   148→    """Build nested tree structure for D3 treemap."""
   149→    root: dict = {"name": "src", "children": {}}
   150→
   151→    for f in files:
   152→        parts = f["path"].split("/")
   153→        # Skip leading 'src/' since root is already 'src'
   154→        if parts and parts[0] == "src":
   155→            parts = parts[1:]
   156→        node = root
   157→        for part in parts[:-1]:
   158→            if part not in node["children"]:
   159→                node["children"][part] = {"name": part, "children": {}}
   160→            node = node["children"][part]
   161→
   162→        filename = parts[-1]
   163→        resolved = f["abs_path"]
   164→        dep_entry = dep_graph.get(resolved, {"import_count": 0, "importer_count": 0})
   165→        file_findings = findings_by_file.get(f["path"], [])
   166→        open_findings = [ff for ff in file_findings if ff.get("status") == "open"]
   167→
   168→        node["children"][filename] = {
   169→            "name": filename,
   170→            "path": f["path"],
   171→            "loc": max(f["loc"], 1),  # D3 needs >0 values
   172→            "fan_in": dep_entry.get("importer_count", 0),
   173→            "fan_out": dep_entry.get("import_count", 0),
   174→            "findings_total": len(file_findings),
   175→            "findings_open": len(open_findings),
   176→            "finding_summaries": [ff.get("summary", "") for ff in open_findings[:20]],
   177→        }
   178→
   179→    # Convert children dicts to arrays (D3 format)
   180→    def to_array(node: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
   181→        if "children" in node and isinstance(node["children"], dict):
   182→            children = list(node["children"].values())
   183→            for child in children:
   184→                to_array(child)
   185→            node["children"] = children
   186→            # Remove empty directories
   187→            node["children"] = [
   188→                c
   189→                for c in node["children"]
   190→                if "loc" in c or ("children" in c and c["children"])
   191→            ]
   192→
   193→    to_array(root)
   194→    return root
```
