```
1→"""Text-tree rendering helpers for LLM-readable codebase views."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→
     6→def _aggregate(node: dict) -> dict:
     7→    """Compute aggregate stats for a tree node."""
     8→    if "children" not in node:
     9→        return {
    10→            "files": 1,
    11→            "loc": node.get("loc", 0),
    12→            "findings": node.get("findings_open", 0),
    13→            "max_coupling": node.get("fan_in", 0) + node.get("fan_out", 0),
    14→        }
    15→    agg = {"files": 0, "loc": 0, "findings": 0, "max_coupling": 0}
    16→    for child in node["children"]:
    17→        child_agg = _aggregate(child)
    18→        agg["files"] += child_agg["files"]
    19→        agg["loc"] += child_agg["loc"]
    20→        agg["findings"] += child_agg["findings"]
    21→        agg["max_coupling"] = max(agg["max_coupling"], child_agg["max_coupling"])
    22→    return agg
    23→
    24→
    25→def _render_leaf_node(
    26→    node: dict,
    27→    *,
    28→    prefix: str,
    29→    min_loc: int,
    30→    detail: bool,
    31→    lines: list[str],
    32→) -> bool:
    33→    loc = node.get("loc", 0)
    34→    if loc < min_loc:
    35→        return False
    36→    findings = node.get("findings_open", 0)
    37→    coupling = node.get("fan_in", 0) + node.get("fan_out", 0)
    38→    parts = [f"{loc:,} LOC"]
    39→    if findings > 0:
    40→        parts.append(f"⚠{findings}")
    41→    if coupling > 10:
    42→        parts.append(f"c:{coupling}")
    43→    lines.append(f"{prefix}{node['name']}  ({', '.join(parts)})")
    44→    if detail and node.get("finding_summaries"):
    45→        for summary in node["finding_summaries"]:
    46→            lines.append(f"{prefix}  → {summary}")
    47→    return True
    48→
    49→

    51→    if sort_by == "findings":

    53→    if sort_by == "coupling":

    55→    return sorted(children, key=lambda child: -_aggregate(child)["loc"])

    57→
```

```
def cmd_tree(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    """Print annotated codebase tree to terminal."""
    path, lang, state = load_cmd_context(args)
    print(
        generate_tree_text(
            path,
            state,
            options=TreeTextOptions(
                max_depth=getattr(args, "depth", 2),
                focus=getattr(args, "focus", None),
                min_loc=getattr(args, "min_loc", 0),
                sort_by=getattr(args, "sort", "loc"),
                detail=getattr(args, "detail", False),
            ),
            lang=lang,
        )
    )


def _get_html_template() -> str:
    """Read the HTML treemap template from the external file."""
    return (Path(__file__).parent / "_viz_template.html").read_text()
```

## Summary

I've found the CLI argument setup for the `plan queue` command and similar commands. Here's what I discovered:

### 1. **`plan queue` Arguments Definition**
**File:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/cli_support/parser_groups_admin.py`
**Lines:** 505-513

```python
# plan queue
p_queue = plan_sub.add_parser("queue", help="Compact table of upcoming queue items")
p_queue.add_argument("--top", type=int, default=30, help="Max items (default: 30, 0=all)")
p_queue.add_argument("--tier", type=int, choices=[1, 2, 3, 4], default=None,
                     help="Show only items in this tier (T1=critical .. T4=cosmetic)")
p_queue.add_argument("--cluster", type=str, default=None, metavar="NAME",
                     help="Filter to a specific cluster")
p_queue.add_argument("--include-skipped", action="store_true",
                     help="Include skipped items at end")
```

**Current Arguments:**
- `--top`: integer, default=30
- `--tier`: integer choice from [1, 2, 3, 4], default=None
- `--cluster`: string, default=None, uses metavar="NAME"
- `--include-skipped`: boolean flag

### 2. **Implementation Handler**
**File:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/plan/queue_render.py`
**Lines:** 113-172

The `cmd_plan_queue` function reads arguments with `getattr`:
```python
def cmd_plan_queue(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    runtime = command_runtime(args)
    state = runtime.state
    if not require_completed_scan(state):
        return

    top = getattr(args, "top", 30)
    tier_filter = getattr(args, "tier", None)
    cluster_filter = getattr(args, "cluster", None)
    include_skipped = bool(getattr(args, "include_skipped", False))
```

### 3. **`--sort` Flag Pattern (From `tree` Command)**
**File:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/cli_support/parser_groups.py`
**Lines:** 134-136

```python
p_tree.add_argument(
    "--sort", choices=["loc", "findings", "coupling"], default="loc",
    help="Sort order (default: loc)",
)
```

**Implementation:** `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/output/visualize.py`
```python
sort_by=getattr(args, "sort", "loc"),
```

And used in `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/output/tree_text.py` lines 50-55:
```python
def _sorted_children(children: list[dict], *, sort_by: str) -> list[dict]:
    if sort_by == "findings":
        return sorted(children, key=lambda child: -_aggregate(child)["findings"])
    if sort_by == "coupling":
        return sorted(children, key=lambda child: -_aggregate(child)["max_coupling"])
    return sorted(children, key=lambda child: -_aggregate(child)["loc"])
```

### 4. **Pattern Examples for Other Arguments**
Other commands using `choices` parameter:

| Command | Argument | Choices | File:Line |
|---------|----------|---------|-----------|
| `scan` | `--profile` | `["objective", "full", "ci"]` | parser_groups.py:71 |
| `tree` | `--sort` | `["loc", "findings", "coupling"]` | parser_groups.py:134 |
| `next` | `--group` | `["item", "file", "detector", "tier"]` | parser_groups.py:231 |
| `next` | `--format` | `["terminal", "json", "md"]` | parser_groups.py:237 |
| `show`/`next` | `--status` | Dynamic choices from enums | parser_groups.py:163, 225 |

### Key Pattern for Adding New Arguments

The pattern to follow when adding a new `--sort` or `--recent` flag to `plan queue`:

```python

    "--sort", 
    choices=["queue", "tier", "detector", "recent"],  # your choices
    default="queue",  # sensible default
    help="Sort order for queue items (default: queue)"
)
```

## Summary

I've traced the scan workflow reporting and related logic in the desloppify codebase. Here's what gets printed to the user after a scan completes and how new findings are tracked:

---

## 1. Main Scan Flow & Reporting

**File: `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/scan/scan.py` (Lines 115-210)**

The main `cmd_scan()` function orchestrates the entire flow:

1. **Prepare runtime** (line 119)
2. **Run generation** (line 155) → calls `orchestrator.generate()`
3. **Merge findings** (line 156) → calls `orchestrator.merge()`
4. **Print completion banner** (line 157) → `_print_scan_complete_banner()`
5. **Resolve noise snapshot** (line 159)
6. **Print diff summary** (line 163) → `show_diff_summary(merge.diff)`
7. **Print score delta** (lines 164-171) → `show_score_delta()`
8. **Print plan workflow nudge** (line 173) → `_print_plan_workflow_nudge()`
9. **Show visibility hints** (line 174) → `_show_scan_visibility()`
10. **Print post-scan analysis** (line 182) → `show_post_scan_analysis()`
11. **Build and write query payload** (lines 190-202)
12. **Print LLM summary** (line 209) → `_print_llm_summary()`

---

## 2. "N New Findings" Reporting

**File: `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/scan/scan_reporting_summary.py` (Lines 77-89)**

```python
def show_diff_summary(diff: dict):
    """Print the +new / -resolved / reopened one-liner."""
    diff_parts = []
    if diff["new"]:
        diff_parts.append(colorize(f"+{diff['new']} new", "yellow"))
    if diff["auto_resolved"]:
        diff_parts.append(colorize(f"-{diff['auto_resolved']} resolved", "green"))
    if diff["reopened"]:
        diff_parts.append(colorize(f"↻{diff['reopened']} reopened", "red"))
    if diff_parts:
        print(f"  {' · '.join(diff_parts)}")
    else:
        print(colorize("  No changes since last scan", "dim"))
```

This function prints the main finding count report. The `diff["new"]` value comes from the merge logic.

---

## 3. State Merge Logic & New Finding Tracking

**File: `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_state/merge_findings.py` (Lines 117-204)**

**Function: `upsert_findings()` - This is where new findings are counted:**

```python
def upsert_findings(
    existing: dict,
    current_findings: list[dict],
    ignore: list[str],
    now: str,
    *,
    lang: str | None,
) -> tuple[set[str], int, int, dict[str, int], int, set[str]]:
    """Insert new findings and update existing ones.
    
    Returns (current_ids, new_count, reopened_count, by_detector, ignored_count, changed_detectors).
    """
    ...
    for finding in current_findings:
        finding_id = finding["id"]
        detector = finding.get("detector", "unknown")
        current_ids.add(finding_id)
        by_detector[detector] = by_detector.get(detector, 0) + 1
        matched_ignore = matched_ignore_pattern(finding_id, finding["file"], ignore)
        if matched_ignore:
            ignored_count += 1

        if lang:
            finding["lang"] = lang

        if finding_id not in existing:  # NEW FINDING DETECTED HERE
            existing[finding_id] = dict(finding)
            if matched_ignore:
                existing[finding_id]["suppressed"] = True
                existing[finding_id]["suppressed_at"] = now
                existing[finding_id]["suppression_pattern"] = matched_ignore
                continue
            new_count += 1  # COUNTER INCREMENTED
            changed_detectors.add(detector)
            continue
        
        # ... update existing finding logic ...
```

**Key tracking points:**
- Line 146: `if finding_id not in existing:` — detects new findings
- Line 153: `new_count += 1` — increments the new finding counter
- Returns: `(current_ids, new_count, reopened_count, by_detector, ignored_count, changed_detectors)`

---

## 4. Merge Diff Construction

**File: `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_state/merge_history.py` (Lines 134-162)**

**Function: `_build_merge_diff()` - Constructs the diff dict returned to the user:**

```python
def _build_merge_diff(
    *,
    new_count: int,
    auto_resolved: int,
    reopened_count: int,
    current_ids: set[str],
    suspect_detectors: set[str],
    chronic_reopeners: list[dict],
    skipped_other_lang: int,
    skipped_out_of_scope: int,
    ignored_count: int,
    ignore_pattern_count: int,
    raw_findings: int,
    suppressed_pct: float,
) -> ScanDiff:
    return {
        "new": new_count,  # NUMBER OF NEW FINDINGS
        "auto_resolved": auto_resolved,
        "reopened": reopened_count,
        "total_current": len(current_ids),
        "suspect_detectors": sorted(suspect_detectors) if suspect_detectors else [],
        "chronic_reopeners": chronic_reopeners,
        "skipped_other_lang": skipped_other_lang,
        "skipped_out_of_scope": skipped_out_of_scope,
        "ignored": ignored_count,
        "ignore_patterns": ignore_pattern_count,
        "raw_findings": raw_findings,
        "suppressed_pct": suppressed_pct,
    }
```

---

## 5. ScanDiff TypedDict Schema

**File: `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_state/schema.py` (Lines 164-179)**

```python
class ScanDiff(TypedDict):
    new: int                              # NEW FINDINGS COUNT
    auto_resolved: int
    reopened: int
    total_current: int
    suspect_detectors: list[str]
    chronic_reopeners: list[dict]
    skipped_other_lang: int
    skipped_out_of_scope: int
    ignored: int
    ignore_patterns: int
    raw_findings: int
    suppressed_pct: float
    skipped: NotRequired[int]
    skipped_details: NotRequired[list[dict]]
```

---

## 6. Scan History Tracking

**File: `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_state/merge_history.py` (Lines 87-131)**

**Function: `_append_scan_history()` - Persists scan metadata for historical tracking:**

```python
def _append_scan_history(
    state: StateModel,
    *,
    now: str,
    lang: str | None,
    new_count: int,
    auto_resolved: int,
    ignored_count: int,
    raw_findings: int,
    suppressed_pct: float,
    ignore_pattern_count: int,
) -> None:
    history = state.setdefault("scan_history", [])
    history.append(
        {
            "timestamp": now,
            "lang": lang,
            "strict_score": state.get("strict_score"),
            "verified_strict_score": state.get("verified_strict_score"),
            "objective_score": state.get("objective_score"),
            "overall_score": state.get("overall_score"),
            "open": state["stats"]["open"],
            "diff_new": new_count,           # TRACKS NEW FINDINGS PER SCAN
            "diff_resolved": auto_resolved,
            "ignored": ignored_count,
            "raw_findings": raw_findings,
            "suppressed_pct": suppressed_pct,
            "ignore_patterns": ignore_pattern_count,
            ...
        }
    )
```

This history is limited to the last 20 scans (line 131).

```python
def plan_aware_queue_count(state: dict, plan: dict | None = None) -> int:
    """Count remaining plan-aware queue items (skips excluded, clusters collapsed)."""
    from desloppify.engine.work_queue import QueueBuildOptions, build_work_queue

    result = build_work_queue(
        state,
        options=QueueBuildOptions(
            status="open",
            count=None,
            plan=plan,
            collapse_clusters=True,
            include_skipped=False,
        ),
    )
    return result["total"]

def print_frozen_score_with_queue_context(plan: dict, queue_remaining: int) -> None:
    """Show frozen plan-start score + queue progress."""
    ...
    print(
        colorize(
            f"  Queue: {queue_remaining} item{'s' if queue_remaining != 1 else ''}"
            " remaining. Score will not update until the queue is clear...",
            "dim",
        )
    )
```

**Note:** Currently, `plan show` reports "X items prioritized" but does NOT explicitly show "N new since last scan". The new findings count comes from `show_diff_summary()` immediately after the merge.

```python
) -> tuple[list[str], dict]:
    """Print critical warnings + headline + pointers. Returns (warnings, narrative)."""
    warnings: list[str] = []
    if diff["reopened"] > 5:
        warnings.append(...)
    if diff["new"] > 10 and diff["auto_resolved"] < 3:
        warnings.append(
            f"{diff['new']} new findings with few resolutions — likely cascading."
        )
    # ... more warnings ...
```

---

## Key Data Flow Summary

1. **Scan generates findings** → `run_scan_generation()`
2. **Findings merged into state** → `merge_scan_results()` calls `merge_scan()`
3. **New findings identified** → `upsert_findings()` increments `new_count` when `finding_id not in existing`
4. **Diff constructed** → `_build_merge_diff()` returns `{"new": new_count, ...}`
5. **Printed to user** → `show_diff_summary(diff)` prints `"+{new_count} new"`
6. **Persisted in history** → `_append_scan_history()` stores `"diff_new": new_count` in state
7. **Plan aware** → `plan_aware_queue_count()` can compute queue items separately from findings

---

## Data Structures

**ScanMergeResult** (returned from merge):
```python
@dataclass
class ScanMergeResult:
    diff: dict[str, object]  # Contains "new" key with count
    prev_overall: float | None
    prev_objective: float | None
    prev_strict: float | None
    prev_verified: float | None
    prev_dim_scores: dict[str, object]
```
